## [Unreleased] / [Unreleased]

### Security / セキュリティ

- **CodeQL hardening — resolve `js/incorrect-suffix-check` in `truncateOrigJobId` (validation hardening; no production runtime behavior change) — 2026-06-04** / **CodeQL ハードニング — `truncateOrigJobId` の `js/incorrect-suffix-check` を解消 (validation hardening; production runtime の挙動変更なし) — 2026-06-04**:
  - **EN — Security**: Resolved the CodeQL alert **`js/incorrect-suffix-check`** in `truncateOrigJobId` (`apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts`) by changing the separator-position guard from `sepIndex === origJobId.length - 2` to `sepIndex >= origJobId.length - 2` (a 1-character length-based-comparison hardening) plus a 5-line explanatory comment. **No production runtime behavior change (validation hardening, CodeQL `js/incorrect-suffix-check` resolution only; `>=` is equivalent to `===` since `indexOf("__")` ≤ length-2 for a found separator)** — the `__`-separator index of a found separator can be at most `length - 2`, so `>=` matches exactly the same inputs as the previous `===` and the thrown-vs-accepted set is unchanged. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-04. Modified OSS-synced production file: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts`.
  - **JP — セキュリティ**: `truncateOrigJobId` (`apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts`) の CodeQL alert **`js/incorrect-suffix-check`** を、separator 位置の guard を `sepIndex === origJobId.length - 2` から `sepIndex >= origJobId.length - 2` へ変更 (1 文字の length-based-comparison ハードニング) + 5 行の説明コメント追加で解消。**production runtime の挙動変更なし (validation hardening、CodeQL `js/incorrect-suffix-check` の解消のみ; found separator では `indexOf("__")` ≤ length-2 ゆえ `>=` は `===` と等価)** — found separator の `__` index は最大でも `length - 2` であり、`>=` は従来の `===` と完全に同一の入力に match し、throw / accept する集合は不変。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-04。改変 OSS-synced production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts`。

- **Vitest CVE remediation — resolve GHSA-5xrq-8626-4rwp (vitest `<4.1.0`, critical) by unifying all 6 workspace packages to vitest `^4.1.0` — 2026-06-03** / **Vitest CVE 対応 — 全 6 workspace パッケージを vitest `^4.1.0` に統一して GHSA-5xrq-8626-4rwp (vitest `<4.1.0`、critical) を解消 — 2026-06-03**:
  - **EN — Security**: Resolved the critical advisory **GHSA-5xrq-8626-4rwp** (the Vitest UI server arbitrary file read / arbitrary code execution surface affecting vitest `<4.1.0`) by bumping all 6 workspace `package.json` manifests to vitest `^4.1.0` (and `@vitest/coverage-v8` to the matching `^4.1.0`); the lockfile now resolves vitest **4.1.8**. The `packages/database` package was a 3.2.x → 4.x **major bump** (the other 5 packages were already on 4.x). The exploit precondition (`@vitest/ui` installed + a `--ui` run script) is **absent in this repository** — i.e. the critical was **dormant** — but it is resolved here regardless because the GHSA critical classification trips the merge-gate hard requirement that `pnpm audit --audit-level=high` must report 0 High/Critical advisories. This closes Dependabot alerts **#174** and **#175**. **No production runtime code was changed** (manifest/lockfile only; vitest is a devDependency). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-03.
  - **JP — セキュリティ**: critical advisory **GHSA-5xrq-8626-4rwp** (vitest `<4.1.0` に影響する Vitest UI server の任意ファイル読込 / 任意コード実行 surface) を、全 6 workspace `package.json` を vitest `^4.1.0` (および `@vitest/coverage-v8` を同 `^4.1.0`) に bump して解消; lockfile は vitest **4.1.8** に解決される。`packages/database` は 3.2.x → 4.x の **major bump** (他 5 パッケージは既に 4.x)。exploit 前提 (`@vitest/ui` 導入 + `--ui` run script) は **本リポジトリには不在** — すなわち critical は **休眠** — だが、GHSA critical 判定が merge-gate の hard 要件 (`pnpm audit --audit-level=high` で High/Critical=0) を発火させるため、いずれにせよここで解消する。これにより Dependabot alert **#174** と **#175** を close する。**production runtime コードの変更はなし** (manifest/lockfile のみ; vitest は devDependency)。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-03。

- **Dependabot remediation — resolve 5 Dependabot security alerts (`ws` / `qs` / `uuid` / `turbo` / `next`) + preventive `brace-expansion` pin — 2026-05-30** / **Dependabot 対応 — 5 件の Dependabot セキュリティアラート (`ws` / `qs` / `uuid` / `turbo` / `next`) を解消 + `brace-expansion` の予防的 pin — 2026-05-30**:
  - **EN — Security**: Resolved 5 Dependabot security alerts via workspace-root `pnpm.overrides` and example app manifest bumps. `ws: ">=8.20.1"` (resolved 8.21.0) and `qs: ">=6.14.2"→">=6.15.2"` (resolved 6.15.2); `uuid` aggregated to a single general override `uuid: ">=11.1.1"` (resolved 14.0.0) by removing the scoped `bullmq>uuid` override; `turbo` (devDependency) `^2.5.0"→"^2.9.14"` (resolved 2.9.16, 2 advisories); and `next` `16.2.3→16.2.6` (high + low severity advisories) in the two example apps. The `ws` and `uuid` overrides reach the `apps/mcp-server` runtime dependency tree; `turbo` is a build-only devDependency and the `next` bumps are in `example/` apps (not part of the published OSS package). **Preventive pin (not a Dependabot alert)**: `brace-expansion: ">=5.0.6"` is a **preventive version pin, not the resolution of an active advisory** — `brace-expansion` 5.x is **not affected** by GHSA-jxxr-4gwj-5jf2 (whose affected range is 1.x–4.x), and 5.0.6 was already resolved safely via `minimatch ^10`. After these changes, `pnpm audit --audit-level=high` returns exit 0 and `pnpm audit` (all severity levels) reports No known vulnerabilities (exit 0). **No production runtime code was changed** (manifest/lockfile only).
  - **JP — セキュリティ**: workspace-root の `pnpm.overrides` と example アプリの manifest 更新により 5 件の Dependabot セキュリティアラートを解消。`ws: ">=8.20.1"` (resolved 8.21.0)、`qs: ">=6.14.2"→">=6.15.2"` (resolved 6.15.2)、scoped `bullmq>uuid` override を削除して `uuid` を general override `uuid: ">=11.1.1"` に集約 (resolved 14.0.0)、`turbo` (devDependency) `"^2.5.0"→"^2.9.14"` (resolved 2.9.16、advisory 2 件)、2 つの example アプリの `next` `16.2.3→16.2.6` (high + low severity advisory)。`ws` と `uuid` の override は `apps/mcp-server` の runtime 依存ツリーに到達する; `turbo` は build 専用 devDependency、`next` の bump は `example/` アプリ内 (公開 OSS パッケージには含まれない)。**予防的 pin (Dependabot alert ではない)**: `brace-expansion: ">=5.0.6"` は **active advisory の解消ではなく予防的な version pin** である — `brace-expansion` 5.x は GHSA-jxxr-4gwj-5jf2 (affected 範囲 1.x–4.x) の **対象外**であり、5.0.6 は既に `minimatch ^10` 経由で安全に解決済み。これらの変更後、`pnpm audit --audit-level=high` は exit 0、`pnpm audit` (全 severity level) は No known vulnerabilities (exit 0) を返す。**production runtime コードの変更はなし** (manifest/lockfile のみ)。

- **main CI-green hotfix — bump `tmp` to `>=0.2.6` (resolves CVE-2026-44705 Path Traversal) — 2026-05-30** / **main CI-green hotfix — `tmp` を `>=0.2.6` に更新 (CVE-2026-44705 パストラバーサル解消) — 2026-05-30**:
  - **EN — Security**: Added a `pnpm.overrides` pin `tmp: ">=0.2.6"` (resolved 0.2.7, MIT, zero transitive dependencies) to resolve the `tmp` Path Traversal high-severity advisory (CVE-2026-44705 / GHSA-ph9p-34f9-6g65). `tmp` is a **transitive devDependency only** (via testcontainers); it is not shipped at runtime. `pnpm audit --audit-level=high` returns exit 0 after the override.
  - **JP — セキュリティ**: `tmp` の Path Traversal high 脆弱性 (CVE-2026-44705 / GHSA-ph9p-34f9-6g65) を解消するため `pnpm.overrides` に `tmp: ">=0.2.6"` (resolved 0.2.7、MIT、推移依存ゼロ) を追加。`tmp` は **testcontainers 経由の推移 devDependency のみ** であり runtime には同梱されない。override 適用後 `pnpm audit --audit-level=high` は exit 0。

### Added / 追加

- **PR-L1a — `INV-PAGE-BATCH-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001` standing regression (batch same-URL enqueue dedup to 1 survivor via URL-stable UUIDv5 jobId) — 2026-06-04** / **PR-L1a — `INV-PAGE-BATCH-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001` standing regression (batch 内同一 URL enqueue を URL-stable UUIDv5 jobId で 1 survivor に dedup) — 2026-06-04**:
  - **EN — Added**: Added a new large-page standing regression invariant `INV-PAGE-BATCH-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001` (4 tests) that pins the batch-request counterpart of the same-URL dedup landed in the `page.analyze` same-URL near-concurrent resubmit race fix (PR #54): N identical URLs submitted in a single batch request collapse to **1 surviving BullMQ job** because `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` derives the jobId from the URL-stable UUIDv5 (`buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)`), and every element of the returned `jobIds[]` is a valid RFC 4122 v5 UUID. The invariant routes through the production helper end-to-end (not a simulation) so it fails RED in CI on a dedup regression. **No production code change in this entry** — this is a CI-failing test addition that guards the existing dedup contract.
  - **JP — 追加**: 新規 large-page standing regression invariant `INV-PAGE-BATCH-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001` (4 tests) を追加。`page.analyze` 同一 URL 近接多重 submit race 修正 (PR #54) で landing した same-URL dedup の **batch リクエスト版**を pin する: 単一 batch リクエスト内で同一 URL を N× submit すると、`addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` が jobId を URL-stable UUIDv5 (`buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)`) から導出するため **1 survivor の BullMQ job** に collapse し、返却される `jobIds[]` の各要素は valid RFC 4122 v5 UUID となる。本 invariant は production helper を end-to-end 経由 (simulation ではない) するため、dedup regression 時に CI で RED に落ちる。**本 entry に production code 変更なし** — 既存 dedup 契約を守る CI-failing test 追加。

- **backfill `queued`-stuck rescue — worker-present recovery of pages whose BullMQ job was lost (give-up scan from-status 2-branch + Section C rescue + 2 new audit actions) — 2026-05-31** / **backfill `queued` 固着救済 — BullMQ ジョブを失ったページの worker 稼働時リカバリ (give-up scan の from-status 2分岐 + Section C rescue + 新規監査アクション 2 個) — 2026-05-31**:
  - **EN — Added**: Added a worker-present rescue path for embedding-backfill pages stuck at `queued` after their BullMQ job was lost (worker interruption / Redis flush / job-retention expiry), so large-page overflow embeddings reliably reach a terminal state (`completed` / `failed`). The give-up (terminal) scan now splits its terminal write by **from-status**: `queued`-origin rows (stranded by worker-absence, verified-rescuable) transition to `failed_with_known_reason` + reason `supervisor_restart_orphan` so the recovery cron re-enqueues them toward `completed` (recovery-IN); `in_progress`-origin rows (stranded by a genuine vision-unload timeout) keep the unchanged bare `failed` + reason `vision_unload_timeout` (recovery-OUT). A new reconciliation Section C (`reconcileQueuedRows`) re-arms rescuable rows (P2) or gives them up after the retry-budget cap (P3). Two new GDPR Art.30 audit actions are emitted — `backfill_rescue_queued` (P2 re-enqueue) and `backfill_rescue_queued_gave_up` (P3 give-up, `details.originStatus="queued"`) — both with 365-day retention, PII-free numeric/enum `details`, and `targetId` truncation (CWE-209). Covered by the standing regression invariant `INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001` (large-page) `INV-BACKFILL-QUEUED-RESCUE-014` (10 contracts) and the worker-lifecycle `INV-BACKFILL-SECONDARY-SPAWN-TIMEOUT-TERMINAL-013` (from-status 2-branch terminal).
  - **JP — 追加**: BullMQ ジョブを失って `queued` に固着した embedding-backfill ページ (worker 中断 / Redis flush / job-retention 失効) を worker 稼働時に救済する経路を追加し、large-page の overflow embeddings が確実に terminal 状態 (`completed` / `failed`) に到達するようにした。give-up (terminal) scan は terminal write を **from-status** で 2 分岐する: `queued`-origin 行 (worker-absence で stranded、rescue 可能と検証済) は `failed_with_known_reason` + reason `supervisor_restart_orphan` へ遷移し recovery cron が `completed` 方向へ re-enqueue する (recovery-IN); `in_progress`-origin 行 (真の vision-unload timeout で stranded) は従来どおり bare `failed` + reason `vision_unload_timeout` を維持する (recovery-OUT)。新規 reconciliation Section C (`reconcileQueuedRows`) が rescue 可能行を re-arm し (P2)、retry budget cap 超過後は give-up する (P3)。GDPR Art.30 監査アクションを 2 個新規 emit — `backfill_rescue_queued` (P2 re-enqueue) と `backfill_rescue_queued_gave_up` (P3 give-up、`details.originStatus="queued"`) — いずれも 365 日保持、PII-free な numeric/enum `details`、`targetId` truncation (CWE-209)。standing regression invariant `INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001` (large-page) `INV-BACKFILL-QUEUED-RESCUE-014` (10 契約) および worker-lifecycle の `INV-BACKFILL-SECONDARY-SPAWN-TIMEOUT-TERMINAL-013` (from-status 2分岐 terminal) で検証。

### Removed / 削除

- **PR-L1b — legacy `addPageAnalyzeJob` dead-path helper removal + test caller migration to `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` (URL-stable UUIDv5 dedup) — 2026-06-04** / **PR-L1b — legacy `addPageAnalyzeJob` dead-path ヘルパー削除 + test caller を `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` (URL-stable UUIDv5 dedup) に移行 — 2026-06-04**:
  - **EN — Removed**: Removed the legacy `addPageAnalyzeJob` helper from `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts`. It was a `@deprecated` dead path (zero production call sites) that performed a bare `queue.add` keyed on `data.webPageId`, lacking the atomic SETNX claim + observability-only audit emit of `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard`. Because `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` derives the jobId from the URL-stable UUIDv5 (`buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)`), keeping a parallel `webPageId`-keyed enqueue path was a latent same-URL-dedup hazard. The removal narrows the orphaned `type Job` import token (the sole `Job<…>` usage was inside the removed function; `noUnusedLocals` would otherwise raise TS6133) and re-frames an internal JSDoc reference from a `{@link addPageAnalyzeJob}` symbol link to plain prose. All test callers were migrated to `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` so `pnpm test` builds without `ReferenceError`. **No production runtime behavior change** — production already routed exclusively through `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard`; this is a dead-code deletion + test migration. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-04. Modified OSS-synced production file: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts`.
  - **JP — 削除**: legacy `addPageAnalyzeJob` ヘルパーを `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts` から削除。これは `@deprecated` な dead path (production call site 0) で、`data.webPageId` を key とした bare `queue.add` を行い、`addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` の atomic SETNX claim + observability-only audit emit を欠いていた。`addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` は jobId を URL-stable UUIDv5 (`buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)`) から導出するため、`webPageId` を key とする並立 enqueue path を残すことは same-URL dedup の latent hazard だった。削除に伴い孤立した `type Job` import token を narrow 除去し (唯一の `Job<…>` 使用は削除関数内のみ、放置すると `noUnusedLocals` が TS6133 を発生させる)、内部 JSDoc の `{@link addPageAnalyzeJob}` symbol link を plain prose に re-frame した。全 test caller を `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` に移行し、`pnpm test` が `ReferenceError` なしで build するようにした。**production runtime の挙動変更なし** — production は既に `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` のみを経由しており、本変更は dead-code 削除 + test 移行である。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-04。改変 OSS-synced production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts`。

### Fixed / 修正

- **PR-L2 (CO-ASYNC-03) — page.analyze advertised inputSchema nested scalar coercion parity — additively expose 21 nested boolean/number fields (+1 enum) so string-typed nested MCP arguments no longer raise `VALIDATION_ERROR` — 2026-06-04** / **PR-L2 (CO-ASYNC-03) — page.analyze advertised inputSchema のネストスカラー coercion parity — 21 個のネスト boolean/number フィールド (+enum 1) を additive に公開し、文字列で送られたネスト MCP 引数が `VALIDATION_ERROR` を起こさないよう修正 — 2026-06-04**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Extended PR #55's top-level coercion parity to **nested-object scalars**. The async-tool `coerceArgs` (`apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/args-type-coercion.ts`) already recurses into nested objects, but only coerces fields the advertised inputSchema declares — so 21 nested boolean/number fields that were absent from `page.analyze`'s advertised schema reached Zod un-coerced and raised `VALIDATION_ERROR` when an MCP client sent them as strings (e.g. `layoutOptions.perSectionVision: "false"`, `motionOptions.video_options.timeout: "30000"`). The fix additively declares those 21 nested scalars — `layoutOptions` (`perSectionVision` / `visionBatchSize` / `scrollVision` / `scrollVisionMaxCaptures`), `visionOptions.visionFallbackToHtmlOnly`, and `motionOptions` (`detect_webgl_animations` + the boolean/number fields under `video_options` / `runtime_options` / `webgl_animation_options`) — plus the `responsiveOptions.breakpoint_resolution` enum (for advertised↔Zod string-field parity; coercion is a no-op for enums). Each nested scalar's `minimum`/`maximum`/`default` mirrors the Zod canonical (`pageAnalyzeInputSchema`). Additive only: the Zod canonical field definitions and the existing client contract are unchanged; correctly-typed inputs are unaffected (coercion is a no-op when the value already matches). Covered by `apps/mcp-server/tests/tools/page/analyze-advertised-schema-coercion.test.ts` — nested regression cases (string→scalar coerce + parse), a forward-compat SSOT-derived nested drift guard `deriveCoercibleNestedZodFields` (recursive, ZodEffects-unwrapping + ZodArray-excluding, with a ≥21 non-vacuity meta-assert), and a nested constraint (default/min/max) SSOT-diff test. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-04. Modified production file: `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts`.
  - **JP — 修正**: PR #55 の top-level coercion parity を **ネストオブジェクト内のスカラー**に拡張した。async ツールの `coerceArgs` (`apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/args-type-coercion.ts`) は既にネストオブジェクトに再帰するが、coerce 対象は advertised inputSchema が declared した field のみであり、`page.analyze` の advertised schema から欠落していた 21 個のネスト boolean/number field が un-coerced のまま Zod に到達し、MCP クライアントが文字列で送ると `VALIDATION_ERROR` を起こしていた (例: `layoutOptions.perSectionVision: "false"`、`motionOptions.video_options.timeout: "30000"`)。本修正はこれら 21 個のネストスカラー — `layoutOptions` (`perSectionVision` / `visionBatchSize` / `scrollVision` / `scrollVisionMaxCaptures`)、`visionOptions.visionFallbackToHtmlOnly`、`motionOptions` (`detect_webgl_animations` + `video_options` / `runtime_options` / `webgl_animation_options` 配下の boolean/number) — を additive に declared し、加えて `responsiveOptions.breakpoint_resolution` enum (advertised↔Zod の string-field parity 用; enum は coercion no-op) を追加する。各ネストスカラーの `minimum`/`maximum`/`default` は Zod canonical (`pageAnalyzeInputSchema`) と一致させた。additive のみ: Zod canonical の field 定義および既存 client 契約は変更なし; 正しい型で送られた入力は影響なし (値が既に一致する場合 coercion は no-op)。`apps/mcp-server/tests/tools/page/analyze-advertised-schema-coercion.test.ts` でカバー — ネスト regression ケース (文字列→スカラー coerce + parse)、forward-compat な SSOT-derived ネスト drift guard `deriveCoercibleNestedZodFields` (再帰、ZodEffects unwrap + ZodArray 除外、≥21 の non-vacuity meta-assert)、ネスト constraint (default/min/max) の SSOT-diff test。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-04。改変 production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts`。

- **PR-L1a — same-URL dedup fail-open log defense-in-depth (BullMQ key-missing transient classification) + stale claim-key JSDoc correction — 2026-06-04** / **PR-L1a — same-URL dedup fail-open ログ defense-in-depth (BullMQ key-missing transient 分類) + stale claim-key JSDoc 訂正 — 2026-06-04**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Hardened the observability of the same-URL dedup fail-open path (`enqueue-with-collision-guard.ts`). When a loser races ahead to `handleFailOpen` and re-adds the same jobId while the incumbent's BullMQ keys are mid-lifecycle, the resulting "Missing key for job …" transient is now discriminated via a module-level SSOT regex named-export `BULLMQ_KEY_MISSING_TRANSIENT_RE` and logged as a distinct structured `logger.warn` (the jobId is `truncateId(8)`-truncated and the error is `sanitizeErrorMessage`-routed, CWE-209). This is **pure observability**: the returned outcome is `enqueued_fail_open` in **both** branches, so it never flips fail-open → fail-closed and same-URL dedup correctness is unchanged (BullMQ jobId uniqueness still guarantees ≤1 surviving job). Also corrected a stale JSDoc comment on the page-analyze SETNX claim-key namespace from `…jobclaim:<webPageId>` to `…jobclaim:<jobId>` where `<jobId>` is the URL-stable UUIDv5 from `buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)` (ADR-0018 Amendment 11) — comment-only. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-04. Modified production file: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/enqueue-with-collision-guard.ts` (log defense-in-depth) + `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts` (comment-only).
  - **JP — 修正**: same-URL dedup fail-open path (`enqueue-with-collision-guard.ts`) の observability を強化。loser が `handleFailOpen` に先回りし、incumbent の BullMQ key が lifecycle 途中の状態で同一 jobId を re-add したとき発生する "Missing key for job …" transient を、module-level SSOT regex named-export `BULLMQ_KEY_MISSING_TRANSIENT_RE` で判別し、distinct な構造化 `logger.warn` として記録する (jobId は `truncateId(8)` で truncate、error は `sanitizeErrorMessage` 経由、CWE-209)。これは **pure observability**: returned outcome は **両分岐とも** `enqueued_fail_open` であり、fail-open → fail-closed に変えず same-URL dedup の正しさも不変 (BullMQ jobId uniqueness が依然 ≤1 surviving job を保証)。併せて page-analyze SETNX claim-key namespace の stale JSDoc comment を `…jobclaim:<webPageId>` から `…jobclaim:<jobId>` (`<jobId>` は `buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)` 由来の URL-stable UUIDv5、ADR-0018 Amendment 11) に訂正 — comment-only。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-04。改変 production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/enqueue-with-collision-guard.ts` (log defense-in-depth) + `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts` (comment-only)。

- **page.analyze advertised inputSchema string coercion — additively expose boolean/number/object fields so string-typed MCP arguments no longer raise `VALIDATION_ERROR` — 2026-06-02** / **page.analyze advertised inputSchema 文字列強制変換 — boolean/number/object フィールドを additive に公開し、文字列で送られた MCP 引数が `VALIDATION_ERROR` を起こさないよう修正 — 2026-06-02**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Additively exposed `page.analyze`'s boolean/number/object inputs (`async`, `timeout_strategy`, `partial_results`, `layoutTimeout`/`motionTimeout`/`qualityTimeout`, `auto_retry`/`max_retries`, `layout_first`, `respect_robots_txt`, `narrativeOptions`/`visionOptions`) on the advertised inputSchema, so the tool-layer `coerceArgs` (`apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/args-type-coercion.ts`) can read their advertised type hints and coerce boolean/number values that an MCP client sends as strings (e.g. `async: "true"`, `timeout: "600000"`) to the correct type before Zod validation — eliminating `VALIDATION_ERROR` from string-typed arguments on the async tool path. Additive only: the Zod canonical (`pageAnalyzeInputSchema`) field definitions and the existing client contract are unchanged. Covered by the standing regression invariant `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` (schema-enum-sync) and `apps/mcp-server/tests/tools/page/analyze-advertised-schema-coercion.test.ts` (9 tests, incl. a forward-compat SSOT-derived parity guard `deriveCoercibleTopLevelZodFields`). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-02. Modified production file: `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts`.
  - **JP — 修正**: `page.analyze` の boolean/number/object 入力 (`async`、`timeout_strategy`、`partial_results`、`layoutTimeout`/`motionTimeout`/`qualityTimeout`、`auto_retry`/`max_retries`、`layout_first`、`respect_robots_txt`、`narrativeOptions`/`visionOptions`) を advertised inputSchema に additive に公開し、ツール層の `coerceArgs` (`apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/args-type-coercion.ts`) が advertised の型ヒントを参照して、MCP クライアントが文字列で送った boolean/number (例: `async: "true"`、`timeout: "600000"`) を Zod 検証前に正しい型へ強制変換できるようにした — async ツール経路での文字列引数による `VALIDATION_ERROR` を解消。additive のみ: Zod canonical (`pageAnalyzeInputSchema`) の field 定義および既存 client 契約は変更なし。standing regression invariant `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` (schema-enum-sync) および `apps/mcp-server/tests/tools/page/analyze-advertised-schema-coercion.test.ts` (9 tests、forward-compat SSOT-derived parity guard `deriveCoercibleTopLevelZodFields` を含む) でカバー。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-02。改変 production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts`。

- **page.analyze same-URL near-concurrent resubmit race — URL-stable UUIDv5 jobId dedup at the enqueue layer (PR-SAMEURL-DEDUP) — 2026-06-02** / **page.analyze 同一 URL 近接多重 submit race — enqueue 層での URL-stable UUIDv5 jobId dedup (PR-SAMEURL-DEDUP) — 2026-06-02**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closed a structural race where near-concurrent same-URL `page.analyze` submits each minted a fresh per-call `webPageId = uuidv7()` used directly as the BullMQ jobId, so the collision guard's SETNX claim key (`reftrix:page-analyze:jobclaim:<jobId>`) never collided and the guard was a permanent no-op (empirically `page_analyze_collision_resolved` had 0 audit rows over all time). Independent same-URL jobs then ran in parallel, and a prior job's planned restart (`maxJobsBeforeRestart=1`) plus `removeOnComplete` trim collateral caused an in-flight sibling's `await job.updateProgress()` to throw `"Missing key for job"`, killing the job (`attempts:1`) and clobbering embeddings. The fix unifies the page-analyze BullMQ jobId from the per-call `webPageId` to a **URL-derived stable identifier** `buildUrlStableJobId(url) = uuidv5(normalizeUrlForValidation(url), uuid.v5.URL)` (RFC 4122 deterministic UUIDv5 seeded by the predefined `v5.URL` namespace `6ba7b811-9dad-11d1-80b4-00c04fd430c8` — no fabricated magic UUID), so near-concurrent same-URL submits collide on the claim key and dedup at the enqueue layer (the `reused_active` path becomes production-reachable for the first time). Because UUIDv5 is a valid RFC 4122 UUID, it passes the 4 `z.string().uuid()` / MCP `format:"uuid"` schema gates on the async / polling path with **no schema relaxation**; the async response now returns the URL-stable UUIDv5 `jobId` (polling contract preserved) plus an additive `webPageId` field exposing the DB row id. On Redis-unreachable the guard fails open (`enqueued_fail_open`, availability > dedup, documented accepted-risk). GDPR Recital 26 pseudonymisation: no raw URL is exposed in claim keys / logs / audit details (only a `truncateId(8)` 32-bit UUIDv5 fragment in `enqueued_retry` audit details, non-identifying per CWE-209). Covered by the new standing regression invariant `INV-PAGE-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001` (large-page, 3-block: round-trip / ≤1 survivor / Layer-2) and the sibling `INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001-E` C16 URL-distinctness pin. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-02. Modified production files: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/output.schemas.ts`.
  - **JP — 修正**: 同一 URL を近接同時に `page.analyze` で submit すると毎回 per-call `webPageId = uuidv7()` を新規発番し、それがそのまま BullMQ jobId として使われるため、collision guard の SETNX claim key (`reftrix:page-analyze:jobclaim:<jobId>`) が衝突せず guard が恒久 no-op になる (実証: `page_analyze_collision_resolved` audit は全期間 0 件) 構造的 race を closure。同一 URL の独立 job が並行 enqueue され、前 job の planned restart (`maxJobsBeforeRestart=1`) + `removeOnComplete` trim 巻き添えで in-flight sibling の `await job.updateProgress()` が `"Missing key for job"` を throw → job 死亡 (`attempts:1`) + embedding clobber、という故障連鎖が成立していた。本修正は page-analyze の BullMQ jobId を per-call `webPageId` から **URL 派生 stable 識別子** `buildUrlStableJobId(url) = uuidv5(normalizeUrlForValidation(url), uuid.v5.URL)` (RFC 4122 決定論的 UUIDv5、predefined `v5.URL` namespace `6ba7b811-9dad-11d1-80b4-00c04fd430c8` を seed に使用、magic UUID を捏造しない) に統一し、近接同時の同一 URL submit が claim key で衝突して enqueue 層で dedup されるようにした (`reused_active` path が初めて production 到達可能になる)。UUIDv5 は valid RFC 4122 UUID のため、async / polling path の 4 つの `z.string().uuid()` / MCP `format:"uuid"` schema gate を **schema relax 不要**で通過する; async response は URL-stable UUIDv5 `jobId` を返す (polling 契約無傷) + DB 行 ID を露出する additive `webPageId` field を新設する。Redis 到達不能時は guard が fail-open (`enqueued_fail_open`、availability > dedup、documented accepted-risk)。GDPR Recital 26 pseudonymisation: claim key / log / audit details に raw URL を露出しない (`enqueued_retry` audit details に入るのは `truncateId(8)` の 32-bit UUIDv5 fragment のみ、CWE-209 上 non-identifying)。新 standing regression invariant `INV-PAGE-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001` (large-page、3-block: round-trip / ≤1 survivor / Layer-2) + sibling `INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001-E` C16 URL-distinctness pin でカバー。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-02。改変 production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts`、`apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts`、`apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/output.schemas.ts`。

- **section_visual blank / no-position backfill terminal-skip — large-page overflow sections reach `completed` (3→5 skip reasons) — 2026-06-01** / **section_visual blank / no-position backfill terminal-skip — large-page の overflow section が `completed` に到達 (skip reason 3→5) — 2026-06-01**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closed a structural stall where, on the backfill path (`fallbackEnabled === false`), section-visual embeddings that are either rendered-but-empty (white/uniform crop, `isBlank === true`) or have absent/degenerate geometry (no `sectionPositionMap` position, or `height < 10`) were left perpetually pending, so the page never reached `completed`. The two affected exits now write a per-row terminal-skip marker to `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` — `section_visual_blank` and `section_visual_no_position` — which the SSOT exclusion predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` symmetrically excludes from the section-visual pending query (mirroring the existing `uncroppable` / `duplicate` / `pii_excluded`). The terminal-skip enum set grows additively 3→5 across the 4 canonical lockstep sites (Prisma CHECK ↔ TS SSOT ↔ schema field ↔ exclusion predicate) via the new additive migration `20260531090000_add_section_visual_blank_no_position_skip_reasons`. Both new values are **non-PII degraded-coverage technical terminals** (GDPR Art.4(1) non-applicable), distinct in meaning from the `section_visual_pii_excluded` PII exclusion. Covered by the new standing regression invariant `INV-SECTION-VISUAL-BLANK-TERMINAL-015` (large-page) and the `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` section-visual 4-site lockstep test. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-01.
  - **JP — 修正**: backfill path (`fallbackEnabled === false`) において、描画済だが空 (white/uniform crop、`isBlank === true`) または geometry 欠落/退化 (`sectionPositionMap` に position 無し、または `height < 10`) の section-visual embedding が永久 pending に滞留し、ページが `completed` に到達しない構造的 stall を closure。該当する 2 つの exit は `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` に per-row terminal-skip マーカー — `section_visual_blank` と `section_visual_no_position` — を書込むようになり、SSOT exclusion predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` が section-visual pending クエリから対称に除外する (既存の `uncroppable` / `duplicate` / `pii_excluded` と同様)。terminal-skip enum セットは新規 additive migration `20260531090000_add_section_visual_blank_no_position_skip_reasons` により 4 つの canonical lockstep site (Prisma CHECK ↔ TS SSOT ↔ schema field ↔ exclusion predicate) を横断して additive に 3→5 へ拡張。新 2 値はいずれも **非 PII の degraded-coverage technical terminal** (GDPR Art.4(1) 非該当) であり、`section_visual_pii_excluded` の PII 除外とは意味が異なる。新 standing regression invariant `INV-SECTION-VISUAL-BLANK-TERMINAL-015` (large-page) および `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` section-visual 4-site lockstep test でカバー。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-01。

- **backfill worker auto-start — deferred-spawn retry + scan-based terminal closure (large-page overflow embeddings reach a terminal state) — 2026-05-31** / **backfill worker 自動起動 — deferred-spawn リトライ + scan-based terminal closure (large-page overflow embeddings が terminal 状態に到達) — 2026-05-31**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closed a structural stall where large-page overflow embeddings delegated to the secondary `embedding-backfill` worker could stay perpetually `queued`/`in_progress` and never reach a terminal state when the worker failed to spawn. The WorkerSupervisor now runs a bounded deferred-spawn retry (30s cadence, 10min / 20-attempt bound) gated on the Ollama vision-unload probe; on a 3-strike consecutive probe failure it stops retrying and emits the new `audit_logs.action='vision_probe_unavailable'` (CWE-770 unbounded-retry prevention), and on the 10-min timeout with the worker still absent it emits `audit_logs.action='backfill_secondary_spawn_timeout'` and runs a fallback-on-absence scan that CAS-guard-transitions stranded overflow rows (`embeddingBackfillStatus IN ('queued','in_progress') AND embeddingBackfillStartedAt < now-10min`) to terminal (`failed`, reason `vision_unload_timeout`). Both audit actions use `actor=system:worker-supervisor`, 365-day retention, and PII-free numeric/enum `details` (GDPR Art.30). Covered by the `INV-BACKFILL-SECONDARY-SPAWN-RETRY-011` and `INV-BACKFILL-SECONDARY-SPAWN-TIMEOUT-TERMINAL-013` standing regression tests (worker-lifecycle + large-page domains). See ADR-0011 Amendment 7.
  - **JP — 修正**: secondary `embedding-backfill` worker に委譲された large-page overflow embeddings が、worker の spawn 失敗時に永久 `queued`/`in_progress` に滞留し terminal 状態に到達しない構造的 stall を closure。WorkerSupervisor は Ollama vision-unload probe に gate された bounded deferred-spawn retry (30s cadence、10min / 20-attempt bound) を実行し、`probe_failed` が 3 回連続したとき retry を停止して新 `audit_logs.action='vision_probe_unavailable'` を emit (CWE-770 unbounded retry 排除)、10min timeout で worker が依然不在のとき `audit_logs.action='backfill_secondary_spawn_timeout'` を emit + fallback-on-absence scan で stranded overflow row (`embeddingBackfillStatus IN ('queued','in_progress') AND embeddingBackfillStartedAt < now-10min`) を terminal (`failed`, reason `vision_unload_timeout`) へ CAS-guard 遷移する。両 audit action は `actor=system:worker-supervisor`、365日保持、PII-free numeric/enum `details` (GDPR Art.30)。`INV-BACKFILL-SECONDARY-SPAWN-RETRY-011` + `INV-BACKFILL-SECONDARY-SPAWN-TIMEOUT-TERMINAL-013` standing regression (worker-lifecycle + large-page domain) でカバー。ADR-0011 Amendment 7 参照。

- **main CI-green hotfix — standing test CI-only flake determinised (INV-PHASE5-COLDLOAD-DELTA-EXCLUSION-001 branch 4) — 2026-05-30** / **main CI-green hotfix — standing test の CI-only flake を決定論化 (INV-PHASE5-COLDLOAD-DELTA-EXCLUSION-001 branch 4) — 2026-05-30**:
  - **EN — Fixed (test-only)**: Determinised a CI-only flake in the `INV-PHASE5-COLDLOAD-DELTA-EXCLUSION-001` standing test caused by a host-RAM-dependent memory-pressure threshold being evaluated against a fixed mocked absolute rss. The test now `vi.spyOn`-stubs `checkMemoryPressure` to evaluate the mocked rss against fixed dev-baseline thresholds (12288/14336MB), making the abort/degrade gate host-RAM-independent. **Production code is unchanged**; the expected branch result `[0,1,2]` is unchanged.
  - **JP — 修正 (テストのみ)**: `INV-PHASE5-COLDLOAD-DELTA-EXCLUSION-001` standing test の CI-only flake (実機RAM依存の memory-pressure 閾値を固定 mock 絶対 rss に対して評価していたことが原因) を決定論化。`checkMemoryPressure` を `vi.spyOn` で stub し、mocked rss を固定 dev-baseline 閾値 (12288/14336MB) に対して評価することで、abort/degrade gate を host-RAM 非依存にした。**production code は無変更**、期待 branch 結果 `[0,1,2]` も不変。

- **main CI-green hotfix — stale source-pin / mock-contract tests updated to new source shape (4 test files; production unchanged) — 2026-05-30** / **main CI-green hotfix — stale 化した source-pin / mock-contract test を新 source shape に追従 (4 test file; production 無変更) — 2026-05-30**:
  - **EN — Fixed (test-only)**: Updated 4 test files left stale by the earlier section_visual refactor so their source-pins and mock contracts follow the new production source shape — the SSOT predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate`, the `truncateAuditTargetId` SSOT helper, and the `highPiiSectionIdSet` set. The tests were pinning the old shape (inline literals / a missing PII symbol in a `vi.mock`); production code was already correct. **Production source is unchanged**; the net PII-protection coverage (high-PII sections symmetrically excluded, CWE-209 truncation) is preserved.
  - **JP — 修正 (テストのみ)**: 先行の section_visual リファクタで stale 化した 4 test file の source-pin / mock contract を、新 production source shape (SSOT predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` / `truncateAuditTargetId` SSOT helper / `highPiiSectionIdSet` set) に追従させた。test は旧 shape (inline literal / `vi.mock` の PII symbol 欠落) を pin していたが、production code は当初から正であった。**production source は無変更**、PII 保護 coverage (high-PII section の対称除外、CWE-209 truncation) は net 維持。

- **PR-C4 — section_visual pending PII asymmetry closure (high-PII sections no longer leave heavy CPU pages perpetually `in_progress`; new `section_visual_pii_excluded` terminal-skip reason + GDPR Art.30 marker) — working-tree (baseline `e6ae8414`; IO Impl Decision V3 anchor APPROVE per Finding Registry V4; ADR-0018 Amendment PR-C4 Accepted; 2026-05-30; T+1d 2026-05-31)** / **PR-C4 — section_visual pending PII 非対称 closure (high-PII section が heavy CPU page を永続 `in_progress` で stuck させない; 新 `section_visual_pii_excluded` terminal-skip reason + GDPR Art.30 marker)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closes a **PII asymmetry** in the backfill `section_visual` pending predicate. The work side intentionally excludes high-PII sections (containing a `component_parts.pii_risk_level='high'` part) from DINOv2 visual embedding for GDPR Art.5(1)(c) data-minimisation, but the pending predicate lacked the symmetric PII filter, so those sections stayed pending forever and the page never reached `completed` (run3 CPU: w3.org / wikipedia stuck). **Path A** adds a symmetric `NOT EXISTS (… cp.pii_risk_level='high')` PII filter to the SSOT predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` (parity with `part_visual` / `part_text`). **Path B** additively introduces the new terminal-skip reason `section_visual_pii_excluded` (`EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` index 22, additive CHECK migration) written by the SSOT helper `emitSectionVisualPiiExcludedMarkersForPage` on both the work-loop and backfill paths, emitting the new GDPR Art.30 audit action `embedding_section_visual_pii_excluded` (PII-free numeric/enum `details`; `truncateAuditTargetId`-truncated `targetId`; 365d retention). Secondary fixes: 16 hardcoded `.slice(0,8)` PII-truncations migrated to the `truncateAuditTargetId` SSOT; the Chrome-spoof User-Agent in `part-bbox-playwright` / `section-screenshot-fallback` replaced with the honest `ROBOTS_TXT.USER_AGENT` (declared bot, no misrepresentation); a CWE-770 fail-closed budget guard `assertBudgetBelowKillThreshold` (`budget-guard.ts`). **One DB migration** (additive CHECK, model count stays 32), **one new audit action**, **no new MCP tool, no new env var**.
  - **JP — 修正**: backfill `section_visual` pending predicate の **PII 非対称** を closure。work 側は high-PII section (`component_parts.pii_risk_level='high'` パーツ含有) を GDPR Art.5(1)(c) data-minimisation のため DINOv2 visual embedding から意図的除外するが、pending predicate に対称な PII filter が欠落していたため当該 section が永久 pending に残り page が `completed` に到達しなかった (run3 CPU: w3.org / wikipedia stuck)。**Path A** は SSOT predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` に対称な `NOT EXISTS (… cp.pii_risk_level='high')` PII filter を追加 (`part_visual` / `part_text` と対称化)。**Path B** は新 terminal-skip reason `section_visual_pii_excluded` (`EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` index 22、additive CHECK migration) を additive 導入し、SSOT helper `emitSectionVisualPiiExcludedMarkersForPage` が work-loop + backfill 両 path で write、発火時に新 GDPR Art.30 audit action `embedding_section_visual_pii_excluded` を emit (PII-free numeric/enum `details`; `truncateAuditTargetId` truncated `targetId`; 365d retention)。副次修正: 16 hardcoded `.slice(0,8)` PII-truncation を `truncateAuditTargetId` SSOT に migrate; `part-bbox-playwright` / `section-screenshot-fallback` の Chrome-spoof User-Agent を honest な `ROBOTS_TXT.USER_AGENT` (declared bot、misrepresentation なし) に置換; CWE-770 fail-closed budget guard `assertBudgetBelowKillThreshold` (`budget-guard.ts`)。**DB migration 1 件** (additive CHECK、model count 32 不変)、**新 audit action 1 件**、**新 MCP tool / 新 env var なし**。
  - **AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice / AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**:
    - **EN**: The following files were modified under AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) on 2026-05-30 as part of the PR-C4 section_visual PII asymmetry closure: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/types.ts` (`EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` 22→23, `SECTION_VISUAL_TERMINAL_REASON_CANDIDATES`, `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` PII NOT EXISTS), `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/phase-5-embedding.ts` (`emitSectionVisualPiiExcludedMarkersForPage` SSOT helper + audit emit + 16 `.slice(0,8)`→`truncateAuditTargetId`), `apps/mcp-server/src/audit/audit-actions.ts` (`AUDIT_ACTION_EMBEDDING_SECTION_VISUAL_PII_EXCLUDED` SSOT), `apps/mcp-server/src/services/embedding-backfill.service.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/services/backfill-status.helper.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-bbox-playwright.service.ts` + `section-screenshot-fallback.service.ts` (honest UA), `apps/mcp-server/src/config/budget-guard.ts` (new, CWE-770 fail-closed), `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-processors.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/scripts/start-workers.ts`, and `packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma` + new migration `20260530120000_add_section_visual_pii_excluded_skip_reason`. - **PR-INGEST-FAIL-ROW — Phase 0 ingest-fail terminal row persistence (BUG-2 NOROW closure: fetch-fail pages now leave a `failed` row + audit_logs instead of being fully unobservable) — commit `b69ae798` (baseline `49013161`; IO Plan Decision V0 `019e723f-1113` / V1 `019e724c-3164`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e7284-fa61` APPROVE; ADR-0016 Amendment 6 Accepted; 2026-05-30; T+1d 2026-05-31)** / **PR-INGEST-FAIL-ROW — Phase 0 ingest-fail terminal row 永続化 (BUG-2 NOROW closure: fetch-fail page が完全観測不能ではなく `failed` row + audit_logs を残す)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closes the Phase 0 (ingest) fetch-fail **NOROW** observability gap (run3 CPU 10-site verification: textfiles / neverssl produced no `web_pages` row + no terminal status + no audit_logs = fully unobservable). Root cause was a 4-link chain: `PHASE0_EARLY_INSERT` default `false` → W0 early INSERT skipped → fetch fail throws before W1 → `markFailedAndAuditAtomic` id-key plain UPDATE hits P2025 (row absent) → `$transaction` abort → `recordWorkerRelease` FK P2003. Two fixes: (1) flip `PHASE0_EARLY_INSERT` default `false→true` (`page-analyze-worker.ts:365` `=== "true"` → `!== "false"`, opt-out preserved) so the W0 row exists before fetch; (2) unify the failure-path `markFailedAndAuditAtomic` from id-key plain UPDATE to **url-key upsert** (`url @unique`), consistent with W0/W1 — an id-key upsert+create branch would regress to NOROW (CWE-362) on a same-url × different-webPageId concurrent retry (P2002), whereas url-key converges concurrent retries to the same row, structurally eliminating the race. The hardcoded actor `"system:page-analyze-worker"` is migrated to the `AUDIT_ACTOR_PAGE_ANALYZE_WORKER` SSOT import. **No DB migration, no new MCP tool, no new audit action, no new env var** (`PHASE0_EARLY_INSERT` default-value flip only, opt-out retained).
  - **JP — 修正**: Phase 0 (ingest) fetch-fail の **NOROW** observability gap を closure (run3 CPU 10-site 検証で textfiles / neverssl が `web_pages` row 未作成 + terminal status なし + audit_logs なし = 完全に観測不能だった)。root cause は 4 経路の連鎖: `PHASE0_EARLY_INSERT` default `false` → W0 early INSERT skip → fetch fail throw (W1 前) → `markFailedAndAuditAtomic` id-key plain UPDATE が row 不在で P2025 → `$transaction` abort → `recordWorkerRelease` FK P2003。修正 2 件: (1) `PHASE0_EARLY_INSERT` default を `false→true` に flip (`page-analyze-worker.ts:365` の `=== "true"` → `!== "false"`、opt-out 保持) し fetch 前に W0 row を存在させる; (2) failure-path `markFailedAndAuditAtomic` を id-key plain UPDATE → **url-key upsert** (`url @unique`) に統一 (W0/W1 と一貫) — id-key upsert+create 分岐は同一 url × 別 webPageId の並行 retry で P2002 → NOROW 退行 (CWE-362) するが、url-key は並行 retry を同一行に収束させ race を構造消滅させる。hardcoded actor `"system:page-analyze-worker"` を `AUDIT_ACTOR_PAGE_ANALYZE_WORKER` SSOT import に migrate。**DB migration / 新 MCP tool / 新 audit action / 新 env var なし** (`PHASE0_EARLY_INSERT` の default 値 flip のみ、opt-out 保持)。
  - **AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice / AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**:
    - **EN**: The following files were modified under AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) on 2026-05-30 as part of the PR-INGEST-FAIL-ROW Phase 0 ingest-fail terminal row persistence fix: `apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor-failure-path.service.ts` (id-key plain UPDATE → url-key upsert + `buildFailedWebPageCreateData` helper extraction + actor SSOT migrate), `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (`PHASE0_EARLY_INSERT` default flip + `normalizedUrl` pass-through to `markFailedAndAuditAtomic`), and `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/types.ts` (`PipelineState.normalizedUrl` field). feat-only 84 LoC. No new DB migration, no new MCP tool, no new audit action, no new env var. Modified by Reftrix Contributors on 2026-05-30.

- **PR-PART30CAP — Phase 5 part-embedding partial-completion residual enqueue gap fix (dual-trigger; parts≤100 heavy CPU pages now complete embedding instead of perpetual `in_progress`) — commit `d7536724` (baseline `fafe1677`; IO Plan Decision V0 `019e71f4-cc90` / V1 `019e7201-471c`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e7225-812e` APPROVE; ADR-0007 Amendment 2 Accepted; 2026-05-30; T+1d 2026-05-31)** / **PR-PART30CAP — Phase 5 part-embedding partial-completion residual enqueue gap 修正 (dual-trigger; parts≤100 heavy CPU page が永続 `in_progress` ではなく embedding 完走)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Extended the part_text / part_visual backfill enqueue trigger to a **dual-trigger** firing on `(partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD) OR (inline partial-completion residual pending > 0)`. Previously, on parts≤100 pages a C1 per-chunk RSS budget break (chunk 0 = 30 parts under CPU, e5-base ONNX load + chunk-0 encode exceeding the 1536MB budget) embedded only 30 parts inline, while the residual parts (e.g. 51 on an 81-part page) were never enqueued because the enqueue trigger was gated solely on `partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` (= parts>100); the parity gate (`verifyCategoryParity`) requires part_text pending to reach 0, so the page was stuck perpetually `in_progress` (run3 observed 5 sites: parts 56–94). The second disjunct is determined from DB self-discovery via a single `collectCategoryPendingSnapshot` call (no N+1) at the call site `dispatchBackfillJobsForPage`; the resolver `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` receives a bool `hasPendingParts` flag and is NOT given a DB argument (preserving its pure-function unit-pin). `PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD = 100` is not dead-coded — it remains valid as an inline-cap semantic, decoupled from the enqueue trigger. **No new DB migration, no new MCP tool, no new audit action, no new env var.**
  - **JP — 修正**: part_text / part_visual backfill enqueue trigger を `(partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD) OR (inline partial-completion residual pending > 0)` の **dual-trigger** に拡張した。従来 parts≤100 ページでは C1 per-chunk RSS budget break (CPU で chunk 0 = 30 parts、e5-base ONNX load + chunk-0 encode が 1536MB budget を超過) が inline で 30 parts のみ embed し、残余 part (例 81-part ページで 51) は enqueue trigger が `partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` (= parts>100) gate のみに固定されていたため enqueue されなかった; parity gate (`verifyCategoryParity`) は part_text pending が 0 になることを要求するため、ページは永続 `in_progress` で stuck していた (run3 で 5 サイト観測: parts 56–94)。第 2 項は call site `dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` で `collectCategoryPendingSnapshot` の 1-call (N+1 なし) による DB self-discovery から判定; resolver `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` は bool `hasPendingParts` flag を受け取り DB 引数注入はしない (pure-function unit-pin を維持)。`PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD = 100` は dead 化せず inline-cap semantic として残存し enqueue trigger から直交化する。**新 DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / env var なし。**
  - **AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice / AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**:

No new DB migration, no new MCP tool, no new audit action, no new env var. Modified by Reftrix Contributors on 2026-05-30.

- **PR-BACKFILL-PAUSE-RACE — Stage 2 `worker.pause(true)` formal removal (BullMQ `moveToCompleted` Lua race H2 + event-loop pause race H3 structurally eliminated; callback-based exit listener fire becomes deterministic) — commit `49013161` (baseline `b2f6f8b8`; Plan v1.1 candidate B; IO Plan Decision V0 `019e6eed-ed92` / V1 `019e6f0d-7c8b` / V2 `019e6f1a-b580`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e6f95-0428` APPROVE; ADR-0034 Amendment 5 + ADR-0009 §Status further retract; 2026-05-28; T+1d 2026-05-29)** / **PR-BACKFILL-PAUSE-RACE — Stage 2 `worker.pause(true)` 正式 removal (BullMQ `moveToCompleted` Lua race H2 + event-loop pause race H3 を構造的に消滅、callback-based exit listener fire が deterministic に)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Formally removed `await worker.pause(true)` from both worker success paths (`apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` + `page-analyze-worker.ts`), retiring the ADR-0034 Decision 1 Stage 2 "Pre-Return Pause" stage. The pause caused two interleaved race windows: (H2) BullMQ `moveToCompleted` Lua evaluated the `paused` flag non-atomically against the in-flight `worker.pause(true)`, and (H3) under event-loop starvation the pause-related machinery (BullMQ taskforcesh issue #359 indirect evidence) defeated `emit('completed')` fire ordering — together making callback-based exit listener fire non-deterministic (run1 PASS / run2 fail in real-machine repro). Removing the pause collapses the 8-stage Stage-1→8 lifecycle to 7 stages, satisfies the user contract "CPU must complete (not all users have GPU)", and is orthogonal to ADR-0035 §Decision 1 (H1, dispose ceiling 5s microtask race — out of scope, active maintained). `applyPostJobLifecycleGate` is retired to a `Promise<void>` no-op stub for legacy test caller backward compat (IO Plan Decision V1 §Conflict 2 ruling (a) TPA backward compat preferred); `applyPostJobMemoryGate` is the sole post-job gate on both success and failure paths, with `WORKER_MAX_JOBS_BEFORE_RESTART=0` fail-closed semantics explicit (SEC-PLAN-V0-M-01 closure). New AST gate `verify-no-worker-pause.mjs` enforces zero `worker.pause(` callsites in production code with exempt scope = BullMQ `pause:` event handler + test files, rejecting 3 bypass patterns (computed access / indirect call / `vi.spyOn`, SEC-PLAN-V0-M-02 closure).
  - **JP — 修正**: 両 worker (`embedding-backfill-worker.ts` + `page-analyze-worker.ts`) の success path から `await worker.pause(true)` を正式 removal し、ADR-0034 Decision 1 Stage 2 "Pre-Return Pause" stage を引退させた。pause は 2 つの race window を交絡させていた: (H2) BullMQ `moveToCompleted` Lua が in-flight `worker.pause(true)` に対し `paused` flag を非アトミックに評価、(H3) event-loop starvation 下で pause 関連機構 (BullMQ taskforcesh issue #359 間接 evidence) が `emit('completed')` fire ordering を defeat する — 重畳で callback-based exit listener fire が非決定 (実機 repro で run1 PASS / run2 fail) になっていた。pause 削除で 8-stage Stage-1→8 lifecycle を 7 stage に縮退し、user 必須要件「CPU でも完走 (全ユーザーが GPU を使えるわけではない)」を満たす。ADR-0035 §Decision 1 (H1、dispose ceiling 5s microtask race、本 PR scope 外、active 維持) と直交。`applyPostJobLifecycleGate` は legacy test caller backward compat 目的の `Promise<void>` no-op stub に縮退 (IO Plan Decision V1 §Conflict 2 ruling (a) TPA backward compat 優先); `applyPostJobMemoryGate` が success/failure 両 path での唯一の post-job gate となり、`WORKER_MAX_JOBS_BEFORE_RESTART=0` fail-closed semantic を明示 (SEC-PLAN-V0-M-01 closure)。新 AST gate `verify-no-worker-pause.mjs` が production code 全域での `worker.pause(` callsite 0 件を enforce、exempt scope = BullMQ `pause:` event handler + test files、3 bypass pattern (computed access / indirect call / `vi.spyOn`) を reject する (SEC-PLAN-V0-M-02 closure)。
  - **AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice / AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**:
    - **EN**: The following files were modified under AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) on 2026-05-28 as part of the PR-BACKFILL-PAUSE-RACE Stage 2 `worker.pause(true)` formal removal: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/shared/post-job-lifecycle.ts` (no-op stub signature retention for legacy test caller backward compat + explicit `WORKER_MAX_JOBS_BEFORE_RESTART=0` fail-closed contract), `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` (success/failure path unification on `applyPostJobMemoryGate`-only + `_workerInstanceRef` removal), `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (success path unification on `applyPostJobMemoryGate`-only + `_workerInstanceRef` removal), and the new file `apps/mcp-server/scripts/verify-no-worker-pause.mjs` (INV-WORKER-NO-PAUSE-001 AST gate, `AGPL-3.0-only` SPDX header). No new env var, no new DB migration, no new MCP tool, no new audit action. Modified by Reftrix Contributors on 2026-05-28.
    - **JP**: 上記改変は AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) に基づき PR-BACKFILL-PAUSE-RACE Stage 2 `worker.pause(true)` 正式 removal の一部として 2026-05-28 に行われた。改変対象ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/shared/post-job-lifecycle.ts` (legacy test caller backward compat 目的の no-op stub signature 維持 + `WORKER_MAX_JOBS_BEFORE_RESTART=0` fail-closed 契約明示)、`apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` (success/failure path を `applyPostJobMemoryGate`-only に統一 + `_workerInstanceRef` 削除)、`apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (success path を `applyPostJobMemoryGate`-only に統一 + `_workerInstanceRef` 削除)、および新設ファイル `apps/mcp-server/scripts/verify-no-worker-pause.mjs` (INV-WORKER-NO-PAUSE-001 AST gate、`AGPL-3.0-only` SPDX header)。新 env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action なし。改変者: Reftrix Contributors、改変日: 2026-05-28。

- **Embedding backfill — terminal failure status now `failed_with_known_reason` + motion backfill DI fix (heavy CPU sites complete embedding; rows auto-recover once DB is complete) — working-tree (branch `fix/backfill-terminal-section-visual`; IO Impl Decision anchor `019e6a93-bcb0` CONDITIONAL; 2026-05-28)** / **Embedding backfill — terminal failure status を `failed_with_known_reason` に統一 + motion backfill DI 修正 (heavy CPU site が embedding 完走、DB 完全時に自動復帰)**:
  - **EN — Fixed (defect B)**: The embedding-backfill terminal failure transition now writes `embeddingBackfillStatus='failed_with_known_reason'` + `failure_reason` (SSOT `EmbeddingBackfillFailureReason` enum) + `failed_at`, instead of pinning plain `failed` with NULL metadata. Previously a remaining stale `in_progress` row reconciled to plain `failed` (and a job-exception terminal failure likewise) left `failure_reason`/`failed_at` NULL (observability gap) and stayed outside the recovery service's scan window, so a row never auto-recovered even after the DB became complete. The reconciliation path now uses `supervisor_restart_orphan` and the worker job-exception path uses `stall_timeout` (both `auto_recoverable`), routing rows into the existing recovery path (re-enqueue → all 7 categories → terminal `completed` once complete).
  - **EN — Fixed (defect A)**: Registered the missing `setFramePrismaClientFactory` in the fork-isolated backfill child DI setup. Motion backfill's `saveMotionEmbedding` uses `frame-embedding.service.ts`'s own module-scoped Prisma client factory (separate from the layout factory); without registering it the motion backfill child failed at DB save with an unset factory.
  - **JP — 修正 (defect B)**: embedding backfill の terminal 失敗遷移が、NULL メタデータの plain `failed` を pin する代わりに `embeddingBackfillStatus='failed_with_known_reason'` + `failure_reason` (SSOT `EmbeddingBackfillFailureReason` enum) + `failed_at` を書き込むようになりました。従来は残余ありの stale `in_progress` 行 (および job 例外の terminal 失敗) が plain `failed` のまま `failure_reason`/`failed_at` を NULL に残し (observability 欠落)、recovery service の scan window 外に貼り付いていたため、後から DB が完全になっても自動復帰しませんでした。reconciliation 経路は `supervisor_restart_orphan`、worker job 例外経路は `stall_timeout` (いずれも `auto_recoverable`) を使い、既存 recovery 経路 (re-enqueue → 全 7 カテゴリ → DB 完全時に terminal `completed`) に正しく乗せます。
  - **JP — 修正 (defect A)**: fork 分離された backfill child の DI setup に欠落していた `setFramePrismaClientFactory` を登録しました。motion backfill の `saveMotionEmbedding` は `frame-embedding.service.ts` 自身の module-scoped Prisma client factory (layout 側とは別) を使うため、未登録だと motion backfill child が DB save で factory 未設定により失敗していました。

- **PR-BT-5 — Phase 5 per-sub-phase fork-only model + recycle-disable fix (M-1-RSS structural fix: heavy CPU sites now complete embedding instead of SIGKILL) — working-tree (baseline `05f91d18`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e65a3-aaf7` CONDITIONAL / documentation-only check; ADR-0039 Accepted; 2026-05-27; T+1d 2026-05-28)** / **PR-BT-5 — Phase 5 per-sub-phase fork-only model + recycle-disable 修正 (M-1-RSS 構造修正: heavy CPU site が SIGKILL せず embedding 完走)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Migrated Phase 5 embedding from the 2-child (text/visual) fork model to a **per-sub-phase fork-only model**: each of the ≤9 sub-phases (7 `_text` via e5-base + 2 `_visual` via DINOv2) runs in its own `child_process.fork()` that processes one sub-phase and `exit(0)`s, so the OS reclaims the whole glibc-malloc arena at the fork boundary — rooting out the **inter-sub-phase reload** arena accumulation that previously SIGKILL'd heavy CPU sites (e.g. stripe.com 254-part) at the Part-phase start with `analysis_status` stuck at `processing`. **Recycle-disable fix**: the in-process pipeline recycle (`PIPELINE_RECYCLE_THRESHOLD`) is set to `0` (no-op) inside the one-shot fork because, when chunk size (=30) equals the recycle threshold (=30), the recycle reset the arena at chunk end and masked the immediately-following per-chunk RSS budget check, defeating runaway-loop detection (real-machine-measured decisive harm). The fork count is the fixed sub-phase count (≤9), data-row-count-independent (CWE-770 defense). `subPhase` IPC discriminator is additive + SSOT-derived (no breaking change). **No DB migration, no new MCP tool, no new audit action.** New env var: `PHASE5_SUBPHASE_FORK_ENABLED` (default `true`, rollback escape hatch). Real-machine CPU verification (stripe.com, forced CPU): `page.analyze success=true` / `failedPhases=[]` / all text fork delta peak < 4096MB (background_text 2758MB / motion_text 2382MB) / SIGKILL=0 / recycle=0 / backfill `completed` / `part_text` 254-254.
  - **JP — 修正**: Phase 5 embedding を 2-child (text/visual) fork model から **per-sub-phase fork-only model** に移行: ≤9 個の sub-phase (e5-base の 7 `_text` + DINOv2 の 2 `_visual`) が各々独立した `child_process.fork()` で 1 sub-phase を処理し `exit(0)` するため、OS が fork 境界で glibc-malloc arena を全回収する — これにより従来 heavy CPU site (例 stripe.com 254-part) を Part-phase 開始時に SIGKILL し `analysis_status` を `processing` で固着させていた **inter-sub-phase reload** arena 累積を根絶。**recycle-disable 修正**: one-shot fork 内で in-process pipeline recycle (`PIPELINE_RECYCLE_THRESHOLD`) を `0` (no-op) に設定。chunk size (=30) と recycle threshold (=30) が一致すると recycle が chunk 末で arena を reset し直後の per-chunk RSS budget check を mask して runaway-loop 検出を fail させるため (実機 measured な decisive harm)。fork 数は固定 sub-phase 数 (≤9) でデータ件数非依存 (CWE-770 防御)。`subPhase` IPC discriminator は additive + SSOT 由来 (breaking change なし)。**DB migration / 新 MCP tool / 新 audit action なし。** 新 env var: `PHASE5_SUBPHASE_FORK_ENABLED` (default `true`、rollback escape hatch)。実機 CPU 検証 (stripe.com、CPU 強制): `page.analyze success=true` / `failedPhases=[]` / 全 text fork delta peak < 4096MB (background_text 2758MB / motion_text 2382MB) / SIGKILL=0 / recycle=0 / backfill `completed` / `part_text` 254-254。

- **PR-BT-4 — Backfill terminal correctness (analysis-status guard H-1 + part_visual residual marker H-3) — working-tree (baseline `05f91d18`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e65a3-aaf7` CONDITIONAL / documentation-only check; ADR-0018 Amendment 10; 2026-05-27; T+1d 2026-05-28)** / **PR-BT-4 — Backfill terminal correctness (analysis-status guard H-1 + part_visual residual marker H-3)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Two backfill terminal-correctness fixes. **(H-1) analysis-status guard**: the backfill worker must NOT process part categories until the owning `web_pages.analysisStatus` reaches a terminal state; while the page is still analyzing, the job is bounded-re-enqueued via the `embeddingBackfillRetryCount` CAS-increment mechanism (NOT BullMQ retry — `INV-RETRY-AMPLIFICATION-001` gates `attempts: 1`), with the retryCount cap providing a finite deadlock guard so the loop terminates at the cap with terminal `failed` even if `analysisStatus` is permanently stuck. **(H-3) part_visual residual marker**: the backfill `PartVisualProcessor` residual bbox-zero path now writes the per-row `component_part_embeddings.visual_skip_reason='bbox_unresolvable'` terminal marker (reusing the existing PR-F migration column) so residual bbox-zero parts reach a terminal state instead of staying `visual_embedding IS NULL` permanently pending. **No new DB migration** (reuses the PR-F `visual_skip_reason` column + the PR-BT-2 `vision_skip_reason` column), no new MCP tool, no new audit action.
  - **JP — 修正**: backfill terminal-correctness 修正 2 件。**(H-1) analysis-status guard**: backfill worker は所有元 `web_pages.analysisStatus` が終端状態に到達するまで part category を処理してはならない; ページが解析中の間は `embeddingBackfillRetryCount` CAS-increment 機構で bounded-re-enqueue する (BullMQ retry ではない — `INV-RETRY-AMPLIFICATION-001` が `attempts: 1` を gate)、retryCount cap が finite な deadlock guard を提供し `analysisStatus` が恒久 stuck でも loop は cap で終端 `failed` に到達する。**(H-3) part_visual residual marker**: backfill `PartVisualProcessor` の residual bbox-zero path が per-row `component_part_embeddings.visual_skip_reason='bbox_unresolvable'` terminal marker を書き込むようになり (既存 PR-F migration 列を再利用)、residual bbox-zero part が `visual_embedding IS NULL` の永久 pending ではなく終端状態に到達する。**新 DB migration なし** (PR-F の `visual_skip_reason` 列 + PR-BT-2 の `vision_skip_reason` 列を再利用)、新 MCP tool / audit action なし。
  - **AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice / AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**:
    - **EN**: The following files were modified under AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) on 2026-05-27 as part of the PR-BT-4 backfill terminal-correctness fixes: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` (analysis-status guard CAS transitions), `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/embedding-backfill-child.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-processors.ts` (residual `bbox_unresolvable` marker write), and the new pure decision-leaf `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/backfill-analysis-guard.ts` (`AGPL-3.0-only` SPDX header). No new DB migration, no new MCP tool, no new audit action. Modified by Reftrix Contributors on 2026-05-27.

- **PR-BT-3 — `section_visual` fork-child throw-fallback reachability (makes the PR-BT-2 terminal-skip markers fire in production fork-only mode) — `fix/backfill-terminal-section-visual` (HEAD `05f91d18` = PR-BT-2; PR-BT-3 working-tree; IO Impl Decision anchor `019e5b01` CONDITIONAL APPROVE; 2026-05-25; T+1d 2026-05-26)** / **PR-BT-3 — `section_visual` fork-child throw-fallback 到達性 (PR-BT-2 の terminal-skip marker を production fork-only mode で発火させる)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closes the reachability gap discovered during the PR-BT-2 real-machine verification (finding `019e5a11`): the PR-BT-2 `section_visual` terminal-skip markers (`section_visual_uncroppable` / `section_visual_duplicate`) never fired in production fork-only mode, so a page containing an uncroppable or dedup-skipped section still could not reach `completed`. The `section_visual` fork-child dispatch previously called a **text-only** service wrapper (`backfillSectionVisualsForPage`, `getMissingSectionEmbeddings` `se.id IS NULL` only — no DINOv2, no marker) and **returned success**, so `runForkOrFallback` returned the fork result and the in-process marker path (`SectionVisualProcessor.processInProcess`) was never reached. The fix mirrors the established `part_visual` pattern (ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1, the LCC-known-gap throw→in-process-fallback): the `section_visual` fork-child now `throw`s, `runForkOrFallback` catches it and routes to `processInProcess` → `runVisualEmbeddingSubPhases({ fallbackEnabled: false })` (persisted fullPage screenshot only, **no** per-section Playwright capture) → `processSingleSectionVisualEmbedding` → `writeSectionVisionSkipReason`, which fires the terminal markers at the uncroppable / dedup exits. Both visual categories (`part_visual` + `section_visual`) are now symmetric under the throw→in-process-fallback contract. Real-machine verification YES (anchor `019e5ae4`): backfill_status reached `completed`; 7 `section_visual_uncroppable` markers fired. **No new DB migration** (reuses the PR-BT-2 `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` column), no new enum, no new env var, no new MCP tool, no new audit action, no new IPC type.
  - **JP — 修正**: PR-BT-2 実機検証 (finding `019e5a11`) で判明した到達性 gap を closure: PR-BT-2 の `section_visual` terminal-skip marker (`section_visual_uncroppable` / `section_visual_duplicate`) が production fork-only mode で一度も発火せず、uncroppable / dedup-skip section を含む page が依然 `completed` に到達できなかった。`section_visual` fork-child dispatch は従来 **text-only** service wrapper (`backfillSectionVisualsForPage`、`getMissingSectionEmbeddings` の `se.id IS NULL` のみ — DINOv2 なし・marker なし) を呼び **success を返した**ため、`runForkOrFallback` は fork 結果を返し in-process marker path (`SectionVisualProcessor.processInProcess`) に到達しなかった。修正は確立済の `part_visual` パターン (ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1、LCC known-gap の throw→in-process-fallback) を mirror: `section_visual` fork-child は `throw` するようになり、`runForkOrFallback` が catch して `processInProcess` → `runVisualEmbeddingSubPhases({ fallbackEnabled: false })` (永続 fullPage screenshot のみ、per-section Playwright capture **なし**) → `processSingleSectionVisualEmbedding` → `writeSectionVisionSkipReason` に routing し、uncroppable / dedup exit で terminal marker を発火させる。両 visual category (`part_visual` + `section_visual`) が throw→in-process-fallback 契約で対称化。実機検証 YES (anchor `019e5ae4`): backfill_status が `completed` に到達、`section_visual_uncroppable` marker 7 発火。**新 DB migration なし** (PR-BT-2 の `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` 列を再利用)、新 enum / env var / MCP tool / audit action / IPC type なし。
  - **Modification notice / 改変通知 (AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b))**:
    - **EN**: The following files were modified under AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) on 2026-05-25 as part of the PR-BT-3 `section_visual` fork-child throw-fallback reachability fix: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/embedding-backfill-child.ts` (the `section_visual` fork-child case now `throw`s, mirroring the existing `part_visual` case, so the orchestrator catch-fallback routes to the in-process DINOv2 + terminal-skip-marker path per ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1) and `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-processors.ts` (JSDoc clarifying the `SectionVisualProcessor` throw→in-process-fallback reachability contract). No new DB migration, no new env var, no new MCP tool, no new audit action, no new IPC type. Modified by Reftrix Contributors on 2026-05-25.
    - **JP**: 上記改変は AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) に基づき PR-BT-3 `section_visual` fork-child throw-fallback 到達性修正の一部として 2026-05-25 に行われた。改変対象ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/embedding-backfill-child.ts` (`section_visual` fork-child case が既存 `part_visual` case を mirror して `throw` するようになり、orchestrator catch-fallback が ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1 に従い in-process DINOv2 + terminal-skip-marker path に routing する)、`apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-processors.ts` (`SectionVisualProcessor` の throw→in-process-fallback 到達性契約を明示する JSDoc)。新 DB migration / env var / MCP tool / audit action / IPC type なし。改変者: Reftrix Contributors、改変日: 2026-05-25。
  - **Refs**: IO Impl Decision anchor `019e5b01` (CONDITIONAL APPROVE) / PR-BT-2 real-machine finding `019e5a11` / PR-BT-3 real-machine verification `019e5ae4` (backfill `completed`, 7 `section_visual_uncroppable` markers) / `INV-BACKFILL-TERMINAL-COMPLETED-007` Block F (large-page standing, fork→throw→in-process-fallback dispatch routing) / ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1 (part_visual throw→in-process-fallback precedent; ADR-0036 is the per-job fork-only architectural baseline) / ADR-0018 Amendment 9 reachability note / `apps/mcp-server/CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]` PR-BT-3 entry (package-level details) / ` `[Unreleased]` PR-BT-3 entry (internal full). Depends on PR-BT-2 (below); PR-BT-2 added the markers, PR-BT-3 makes them reachable in fork-only mode.

- **PR-BT-2 — `section_visual` terminal-skip symmetry for uncroppable + duplicate sections (System-B correctness fix) — `feature/pr-1-gpu-coord-fork-child-vram-probe` (IO Impl Decision anchor `019e59d8` CONDITIONAL APPROVE; 2026-05-24; T+1d 2026-05-25)** / **PR-BT-2 — uncroppable + duplicate section の `section_visual` terminal-skip 対称化 (系統B correctness 修正)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Fixes the System-B correctness bug where two classes of `section_visual` rows could never be terminated and left a page permanently non-`completed` (eventually mis-pinned to `failed`). The backfill `SectionVisualProcessor` relies on the persisted fullPage screenshot only (`fallbackEnabled: false`, no per-section Playwright capture), so (a) a section taller than the screenshot is **uncroppable** → `vision_embedding` stays NULL forever, and (b) the backfill path **shares the dedup loop**, so a same-type near-duplicate section (cosine > 0.995) is dedup-skipped → `vision_embedding` also stays NULL forever. The `section_visual` pending query previously had no terminal-skip exclusion (unlike `part_visual`, fixed earlier in Amendment 7), so both classes stayed pending → the terminal parity gate never reached `pending = 0`. The fix mirrors the `part_visual` terminal-skip pattern: a new nullable `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` TEXT column (with a `CHECK` constraint restricting it to the 2-literal SSOT-derived subset + a partial index) records a per-row terminal marker; a new single SSOT exclusion predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` (verbatim mirror of `partVisualPendingExclusionPredicate`, parameter-free static SQL, no reason-literal interpolation) excludes terminal-skip rows from the pending query; and `processSingleSectionVisualEmbedding` writes `section_visual_uncroppable` at the no-crop-buffer exit when `isOutOfRange && !fallbackEnabled`, and `section_visual_duplicate` at the dedup exit when `!fallbackEnabled`. The `EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` SSOT grows 20 → 22 (additive tail-append of the 2 new values; `EMBEDDING_SECTION_VISUAL_SKIP_REASONS` is `.filter()`-derived, no hardcoded literals), and `skipReasonToBackfillStatus()` gains 2 explicit switch arms mapping both reasons to `not_required` (a non-retry / page-non-blocking status — NOT the `skipped_fork_error` retry bucket that would re-create the false-`failed` pin). A recoverable null (transient Sharp/RAW decode failure, `isOutOfRange === false`) is **not** terminal-marked (no-fake-success reverse direction); main-path (`fallbackEnabled === true`) sections are **not** terminal-marked (Phase-5-proper non-contamination, the off-screen/duplicate section is recoverable via the dynamic Playwright fallback). The two reasons are written only on the backfill path. **System-B fix only**; the System-A happy-path enqueue completeness fix is the separately-landed PR-BT-1 (above).
  - **JP — 修正**: 2 種類の `section_visual` 行が一度も終端化されず page を永久に非 `completed` (最終的に `failed` に誤 pin) のまま残す系統B correctness bug を修正。backfill の `SectionVisualProcessor` は永続 fullPage screenshot のみに依存 (`fallbackEnabled: false`、per-section Playwright capture を起動しない) するため、(a) screenshot 高さを超える section は **uncroppable** → `vision_embedding` が永久 NULL、(b) backfill path は **dedup loop を共有**するため、同一 type の near-duplicate section (cosine > 0.995) が dedup-skip され → `vision_embedding` も永久 NULL になる。`section_visual` pending query は従来 terminal-skip 除外を持たず (Amendment 7 で先行修正済の `part_visual` と非対称)、両 class が pending に残留 → terminal parity gate が `pending = 0` に到達しなかった。修正は `part_visual` の terminal-skip パターンを mirror: 新 nullable `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` TEXT 列 (2-literal SSOT 由来 subset に制約する `CHECK` + partial index) が per-row terminal marker を記録、新 single SSOT exclusion predicate `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` (`partVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` を verbatim mirror、parameter-free static SQL、reason literal 補間なし) が terminal-skip 行を pending query から除外、`processSingleSectionVisualEmbedding` が no-crop-buffer exit で `isOutOfRange && !fallbackEnabled` 条件付き `section_visual_uncroppable` を、dedup exit で `!fallbackEnabled` 条件付き `section_visual_duplicate` を書込む。`EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` SSOT は 20 → 22 に拡張 (2 新値の additive tail-append、`EMBEDDING_SECTION_VISUAL_SKIP_REASONS` は `.filter()` 由来、hardcode literal なし)、`skipReasonToBackfillStatus()` に両 reason を `not_required` (non-retry / page を block しない status — false-`failed` pin を再生成する `skipped_fork_error` retry bucket では**ない**) にマップする explicit switch arm 2 個を追加。recoverable null (一過性 Sharp/RAW decode 失敗、`isOutOfRange === false`) は terminal-mark **しない** (no-fake-success 逆方向)、main-path (`fallbackEnabled === true`) section も terminal-mark **しない** (Phase 5 proper 非汚染、off-screen/duplicate section は動的 Playwright fallback で recoverable)。両 reason は backfill path のみで書込まれる。**系統B 修正のみ**。系統A の happy-path enqueue completeness 修正は別 landing 済の PR-BT-1 (上記)。
  - **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) Modification Notice / AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**:
    - **EN**: The following files were modified under AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) on 2026-05-24 as part of the PR-BT-2 System-B `section_visual` terminal-skip fix: a new DB migration `packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260524192012_add_section_visual_skip_reason/migration.sql` (nullable `vision_skip_reason` TEXT column + 2-literal `CHECK` constraint + partial index, precedent mirror of `20260523090000_add_part_visual_skip_reason`); `packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma` (`SectionEmbedding.visionSkipReason String? @map("vision_skip_reason")` field); `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/types.ts` (`EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` 20→22 + `SECTION_VISUAL_TERMINAL_REASON_CANDIDATES` + `.filter()`-derived `EMBEDDING_SECTION_VISUAL_SKIP_REASONS` + `sectionVisualPendingExclusionPredicate`); `apps/mcp-server/src/services/backfill-status.helper.ts` (section_visual pending query adopts the predicate); `apps/mcp-server/src/services/embedding-backfill.service.ts`; `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (`skipReasonToBackfillStatus` 2 explicit arms); `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/phase-5-embedding.ts` (no-crop-buffer + dedup exit marker writes, narrowed). A new DB migration is introduced (column add to the existing `section_embeddings` table; no new model — model count stays 32). No new env var, no new MCP tool, no new audit action. - **PR-BT-1 — Backfill dispatch completeness for 4 gate-less embedding categories (System-A correctness fix) — `feature/pr-bt-1-backfill-dispatch-completeness` (commits 4ab2f257 + 73bdb8b9; IO Impl Decision anchor `019e5872` APPROVE; 2026-05-24; T+1d 2026-05-25)** / **PR-BT-1 — 4 gate-less embedding category の backfill dispatch completeness (系統A correctness 修正)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Fixes a System-A correctness bug where `motion` / `background` / `js_animation` / `responsive` embeddings were never enqueued on the happy path. `dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` previously had only two enqueue gates (`part_text`/`part_visual` gated by `partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD`; `section_visual` under its own `sections>0 && screenshot` condition), so the four gate-less categories rode neither gate. On small pages especially, those 4 categories stayed permanently un-enqueued → the terminal parity gate saw `pending > 0` → the reconciliation cron eventually **mis-pinned the page to `failed`** even though the queue work had effectively completed (i.e. "queue completed but DB=failed" misclassification). The fix expands the dispatcher into a hybrid delegate: a new pure, exported `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` derives the gate-less set from the `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES` SSOT (drift-proof, so any future category is gate-less unless explicitly added to the gated set), unconditionally enqueues the 4 gate-less categories, and **PRESERVES** the `part_text`/`part_visual` `PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` gate and the `section_visual` condition unchanged. The call-site gate is moved off the part threshold (`partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD`) to `actualWebPageId` existence so every completed page dispatches the gate-less categories. BullMQ jobId uniqueness (`<webPageId>__<category>`) prevents duplicate submission. This is the **System-A** fix only; the **System-B** fix (`section_visual` dedup/uncroppable terminal-skip asymmetry) is a separate follow-up PR (PR-BT-2) and is NOT included here.
  - **JP — 修正**: `motion` / `background` / `js_animation` / `responsive` の embedding が happy path で一度も enqueue されない系統A correctness bug を修正。`dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` は従来 2 つの enqueue gate のみ (`part_text`/`part_visual` は `partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD`、`section_visual` は独立 `sections>0 && screenshot` 条件) を持ち、4 つの gate-less category はどちらの gate にも乗らなかった。特に small page では当該 4 category が永久未 enqueue → terminal parity gate が `pending > 0` を観測 → reconciliation cron が、queue 作業は実質完了しているにもかかわらず最終的にページを **`failed` に誤 pin** していた (= 「queue completed なのに DB=failed」誤分類)。修正は dispatcher を hybrid delegate に拡張: 新規 pure・exported `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` が gate-less 集合を `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES` SSOT から導出 (drift-proof、将来 category は gated set に明示追加しない限り gate-less)、4 gate-less category を無条件 enqueue し、`part_text`/`part_visual` の `PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` gate と `section_visual` 条件は **PRESERVE** (不変)。call-site gate を part threshold (`partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD`) から `actualWebPageId` 存在のみに変更し、全完了ページが gate-less category を dispatch するように修正。BullMQ jobId 一意化 (`<webPageId>__<category>`) で重複投入を防止。本 PR は **系統A** 修正のみ。**系統B** 修正 (`section_visual` の dedup/uncroppable terminal-skip 非対称) は別 follow-up PR (PR-BT-2) であり本 PR には **含まれない**。
  - **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) Modification Notice / AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**:
    - **EN**: The following files were modified under AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) on 2026-05-24 as part of the PR-BT-1 System-A backfill dispatch completeness fix: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (new `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` + SSOT-derived gate-less set + call-site gate change), and the test files `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-backfill-terminal-completed-007.test.ts` (new `INV-BACKFILL-TERMINAL-COMPLETED-007` large-page standing test + Block-C comment correction), `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/large-page/_fixtures/seed-large-page.ts`, and `apps/mcp-server/tests/workers/page-analyze-phase5-backfill-dispatch.test.ts`. No new env var, no new MCP tool, no new audit action, no DB migration. Modified by Reftrix Contributors on 2026-05-24.
    - **JP**: 上記改変は AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) (Modified Versions) に基づき PR-BT-1 系統A backfill dispatch completeness 修正の一部として 2026-05-24 に行われた。改変対象ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (新 `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` + SSOT 由来 gate-less set + call-site gate 変更)、テストファイル `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-backfill-terminal-completed-007.test.ts` (新 `INV-BACKFILL-TERMINAL-COMPLETED-007` large-page standing test + Block-C comment 訂正)、`apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/large-page/_fixtures/seed-large-page.ts`、`apps/mcp-server/tests/workers/page-analyze-phase5-backfill-dispatch.test.ts`。新規 env var / MCP tool / audit action / DB migration なし。改変者: Reftrix Contributors、改変日: 2026-05-24。
  - **Refs**: IO Impl Decision anchor `019e5872` (APPROVE) / `INV-BACKFILL-TERMINAL-COMPLETED-007` (large-page standing) / `apps/mcp-server/CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]` PR-BT-1 entry (package-level details) / ` `[Unreleased]` PR-BT-1 entry (internal full).

The CO-SAMEURL-02 family is closure-complete (D1 landed + D2/D3 accepted-risk). **No production source was modified** (docs-only); AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice is not applicable.
