- **PR-L3 (CO-SAMEURL-01) — URL normalization SSOT unification (`normalizeUrlCore`): queue jobId and `web_pages.url` upsert share one normalization core, structurally eliminating coupling drift — 2026-06-04** / **PR-L3 (CO-SAMEURL-01) — URL 正規化 SSOT 統一 (`normalizeUrlCore`): queue jobId と `web_pages.url` upsert が単一の正規化 core を共有し、coupling drift を構造排除 — 2026-06-04**:
  - **EN — Changed**: Unified URL normalization into a single source of truth `normalizeUrlCore` (`apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-normalizer.ts`), so the queue jobId (`buildUrlStableJobId` → `uuidv5(normalizeUrlForValidation(url), uuid.v5.URL)`, ADR-0018 Amendment 11) and the DB storage layer (`web_pages.url` upsert) treat the same set of URLs as "the same URL" at the code level. `normalizeUrlForStorage` (`url-normalizer.ts`) and `normalizeUrlForValidation` (`apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-validator.ts`) are now ≤3-line thin wrappers over `normalizeUrlCore`; the 7-step normalization logic (host-lowercase / default-port strip / fragment strip / collapse slashes / sort query / root-path "" / trailing-slash strip) lives in **exactly one place**. This removed ~60 lines of duplicated inline normalization from `url-validator.ts`. The core is **no-throw degraded-return** on parse-failure input (e.g. `"not a url"` / `"http://"`): instead of throwing, it returns the **catch canonical `trimmed.toLowerCase()`** (URL hosts are case-insensitive per RFC 3986 §3.2.2), preserving the existing CWE-209 defense (no raw parse error leaks upward). The SSRF entrypoint `validateExternalUrl` is **unchanged** (it continues to call `normalizeUrlForValidation`, which now delegates to the core). This is a **non-breaking code-only** change: production runtime behavior is unchanged (the two wrappers produce byte-identical output to the prior inline implementations for all valid inputs), and the `web_pages.url` UNIQUE constraint that already enforces data-layer dedup is unaffected. Covered by the new `INV-URL-NORMALIZE-SSOT-001` unit invariant (12 tests, `apps/mcp-server/tests/utils/inv-url-normalize-ssot-001.test.ts`). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-04. Modified OSS-synced production files: `apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-normalizer.ts` and `apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-validator.ts`.
  - **JP — 変更**: URL 正規化を単一の真実の源泉 `normalizeUrlCore` (`apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-normalizer.ts`) に統一し、queue jobId (`buildUrlStableJobId` → `uuidv5(normalizeUrlForValidation(url), uuid.v5.URL)`、ADR-0018 Amendment 11) と DB 保存層 (`web_pages.url` upsert) が「同一 URL」と見なす集合をコードレベルで一致させた。`normalizeUrlForStorage` (`url-normalizer.ts`) と `normalizeUrlForValidation` (`apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-validator.ts`) は `normalizeUrlCore` への ≤3 行の薄い wrapper となり、7 ステップの正規化ロジック (host 小文字化 / デフォルトポート除去 / フラグメント除去 / 連続スラッシュ統合 / クエリソート / ルートパス "" / 末尾スラッシュ除去) は **1 箇所にのみ存在**する。これにより `url-validator.ts` から重複していた inline 正規化を約 60 行削除した。core は parse-failure input (例: `"not a url"` / `"http://"`) に対し **no-throw degraded-return**: throw せず **catch canonical `trimmed.toLowerCase()`** (URL host は RFC 3986 §3.2.2 で case-insensitive) を返し、既存の CWE-209 防御 (生 parse エラーを上位に漏らさない) を維持する。SSRF entrypoint `validateExternalUrl` は **無改変** (引き続き `normalizeUrlForValidation` を呼び、それが core へ委譲する)。本変更は **non-breaking code-only**: production runtime の挙動は不変 (2 wrapper は全 valid input で従来 inline 実装と byte 同一の出力を生成)、data-layer dedup を既に強制している `web_pages.url` UNIQUE 制約も影響なし。新 `INV-URL-NORMALIZE-SSOT-001` unit invariant (12 tests、`apps/mcp-server/tests/utils/inv-url-normalize-ssot-001.test.ts`) でカバー。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-04。改変 OSS-synced production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-normalizer.ts` および `apps/mcp-server/src/utils/url-validator.ts`。

- **PR-1 GPU-COORD — Phase 5 CUDA selection gated on CUDA-EP availability (H regression fix) — `feature/pr-1-gpu-coord-fork-child-vram-probe` (commit 85e8fb3b; IO Impl Decision V1 anchor `019e573c`; ADR-0038 HELD DRAFT; T+1d 2026-05-25)** / **PR-1 GPU-COORD — Phase 5 の CUDA 選択を CUDA-EP 可用性で gate (H regression 修正)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Remediates the H regression (merge blocker) found during PR-1 GPU-COORD real-machine GPU verification: the Phase 5 fork-child VRAM probe selected CUDA on free VRAM alone, and the e5/text in-process init raw-threw when the CUDA Execution Provider shared library (`libonnxruntime_providers_cuda.so`) was absent → 164 embedding failures (a regression vs the legacy CPU path). The fix (a) adds a `verifyCudaAvailability()` gate to the probe so CUDA is selected only when **free VRAM ≥ threshold AND the CUDA EP `.so` is loadable** (else CPU is safely selected with the new `cuda_ep_unavailable` audit reason; the below-threshold check short-circuits before the EP check to preserve ordering), and (b) unifies the e5/text in-process device resolution through the same `detectExecutionProvider` / `verifyCudaAvailability` gate that DINOv2 already uses (`resolveInProcessDevice()`), so `device: "cuda"` is never passed to transformers when the EP `.so` is absent. "The probe intends CUDA" ≠ "ONNX can run CUDA" (directive ⑤ no-fake-success — a CPU-fallback completion is NOT silently treated as a healthy GPU run). `verifyCudaAvailability` / `detectExecutionProvider` / `isLdLibraryPathSetAtOsLevel` are now exported from the `@reftrixmcp/ml` package index so the fork-child leaf path can import them via a bare specifier (`INV-GPU-PROBE-LEAF-IMPORT-001` preserved). The `embedding_cpu_fallback_degraded` audit reason enum gained `cuda_ep_unavailable` (PII-free: VRAM numerics + enum only; GDPR Art.30 / Art.4(1) consistency inherited). **Honesty boundary (directive ⑤)**: the CPU-fallback completion path is real-machine verified (CUDA-EP-absent host now completes embedding on CPU); the `cuda_selected` path remains exercised by mock tests only because the dev verification host has no CUDA EP `.so` (no real GPU CUDA-selected completion is claimed).
  - **JP — 修正**: PR-1 GPU-COORD の実機 GPU 検証で発見された H regression (merge blocker) を remediation: Phase 5 fork-child VRAM probe が free VRAM 空きのみで CUDA を選択し、e5/text in-process init が CUDA Execution Provider 共有ライブラリ (`libonnxruntime_providers_cuda.so`) 不在で raw throw → embedding phase 164 失敗 (legacy CPU path への regression)。修正は (a) probe に `verifyCudaAvailability()` gate を追加し、CUDA を **free VRAM ≥ threshold AND CUDA EP `.so` がロード可能**の両立時のみ選択 (不成立時は新 audit reason `cuda_ep_unavailable` で CPU を安全選択; below-threshold check は順序保全のため EP check 前に short-circuit)、(b) e5/text in-process の device 解決を、DINOv2 が既に使用している `detectExecutionProvider` / `verifyCudaAvailability` gate (`resolveInProcessDevice()`) に統一し、EP `.so` 不在時に `device: "cuda"` を transformers に渡さない。「probe が CUDA を意図」≠「ONNX が CUDA で実推論可能」(directive ⑤ no-fake-success — CPU-fallback 完走を健全な GPU run と silently 同一視しない)。`verifyCudaAvailability` / `detectExecutionProvider` / `isLdLibraryPathSetAtOsLevel` を `@reftrixmcp/ml` package index から export し、fork-child leaf path が bare specifier で import 可能に (`INV-GPU-PROBE-LEAF-IMPORT-001` 保全)。`embedding_cpu_fallback_degraded` audit reason enum に `cuda_ep_unavailable` を追加 (PII-free: VRAM 数値 + enum のみ; GDPR Art.30 / Art.4(1) 整合性継承)。**Honesty 境界 (directive ⑤)**: CPU-fallback 完走 path は実機検証済 (CUDA-EP-absent host で embedding が CPU 完走)。`cuda_selected` path は dev 検証 host に CUDA EP `.so` が無いため mock test のみで exercise (実 GPU CUDA-selected 完走は主張しない)。
  - **Refs**: IO Impl Decision V1 anchor `019e573c` (APPROVE) / FIND-IMPL-PR1-H-NEW-01 (H regression closure) / ADR-0038 HELD DRAFT / `apps/mcp-server/DATA_RETENTION.md` §11.14.1 (`cuda_ep_unavailable` reason) / `apps/mcp-server/CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]` PR-1 GPU-COORD H regression entry (package-level details) / ` `[Unreleased]` PR-1 GPU-COORD H regression entry (internal full) / PR-1 GPU-COORD original landing entry below.

- **Part-visual coverage restoration RC1 — no-scroll bbox fix (PR-G1) — `feat/part-visual-terminal-skip-f1-f4` (IO Impl Decision anchor `019e554e`; ADR-0018 Amendment 8-A; T+1d 2026-05-24)** / **Part-visual カバレッジ復元 RC1 — no-scroll bbox 修正 (PR-G1)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: `PartBboxPlaywrightService` now resolves part bounding boxes via a **full-page scroll sweep** instead of a single `getBoundingClientRect()` call at `window.scrollY=0`. Previously, parts below the fold measured as zero-size and were dropped with a `{0,0,0,0}` bbox, causing the downstream Phase 5 part-visual loop to skip them as `bbox_invalid`. The sweep walks the page in `viewportHeight` steps (`window.scrollTo(0, y)`), measuring `getBoundingClientRect()` + `window.scrollY` to derive each element's absolute Y; once an element reports a non-zero size, its measurement is committed. The viewport is unified to **1920×1080** (previously 1440×900) so the part path shares the same coordinate system as the section path and Phase 5 crops. This is a pure DOM-coordinate measurement change — **no new image-buffer, screenshot, or crop path is added** — and recovers fold-below parts on **all sites**, not just WebGL pages. Off-screen parts on WebGL viewport-only screenshots (RC2) remain out of scope for PR-G1 and keep their existing `bbox_unresolvable` terminal marker (no regression). A new `INV-PART-VISUAL-COVERAGE-001` standing regression (large-page domain) asserts coverage against real DB state. Hardening also added: a `Number.isFinite` guard at the Phase 5 part-visual bbox check (closes a latent `NaN`/`Infinity` gap where `NaN <= 0 === false` let invalid bbox values through to Sharp crop / pgvector), and a finite-validated sweep step calculation.
  - **JP — 変更**: `PartBboxPlaywrightService` の part bounding box 解決を、`window.scrollY=0` 固定での単一 `getBoundingClientRect()` 呼び出しから **full-page scroll sweep** 方式に変更しました。従来は fold より下の要素が zero-size として測定され `{0,0,0,0}` bbox で除外され、後続の Phase 5 part-visual ループで `bbox_invalid` としてスキップされていました。Sweep はページを `viewportHeight` step (`window.scrollTo(0, y)`) で走査し、各 step で `getBoundingClientRect()` + `window.scrollY` を加算して絶対 Y を導出、要素が一度でも non-zero size を報告すればその測定値を確定します。viewport を **1920×1080** に統一 (従来 1440×900) し、part 経路を section 経路・Phase 5 crop と同一座標系に揃えました。これは純粋な DOM 座標測定の変更であり、**新規 image-buffer / screenshot / crop 経路を一切追加しません**。fold 下要素を WebGL ページに限らず **全サイト**で回収します。WebGL viewport-only screenshot の off-screen part (RC2) は PR-G1 の scope 外で、従来の `bbox_unresolvable` terminal marker を維持します (非退行)。新しい `INV-PART-VISUAL-COVERAGE-001` standing regression (large-page ドメイン) が実 DB state に対して coverage を検証します。追加の防御: Phase 5 part-visual bbox チェックに `Number.isFinite` ガードを追加 (`NaN <= 0 === false` で invalid bbox が Sharp crop / pgvector に流れる latent gap を閉鎖)、sweep step 計算に finite 検証を追加。
  - **Modification notice (AGPL-3.0 §5(a)/(b))**: `PartBboxPlaywrightService` (`apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-bbox-playwright.service.ts`) scroll-sweep rewrite + viewport 1920×1080; `getLazyScrollMaxIterations()` SSOT export added to `page-ingest-adapter.ts` (sweep iteration cap shared, re-declaration forbidden); `Number.isFinite` bbox guard in `phase-5-embedding.ts`; lock-extension callback propagation in `bbox-resolution.helper.ts` and `embedding-backfill-processors.ts`. RC1-only; RC2 (WebGL section-fallback crop reuse) deferred to PR-G2.

- **Part visual terminal-skip F1–F4 PR-F — off-screen part marker-gap closure + migration SPDX header (NF-TPA-02 completion-count residual fix) — `feat/part-visual-terminal-skip-f1-f4` (IO Impl Decision anchor `019e53ba-ca5b`; T+1d 2026-05-24)** / **Part visual terminal-skip F1–F4 PR-F — off-screen part marker-gap closure + migration SPDX header (NF-TPA-02 完全性カウント residual 修正)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: Incremental closure on top of the F1–F4 V2 body landing below. (a) **Off-screen part_visual marker-gap closure (NF-TPA-02)**: a new off-screen precondition guard (Phase 5 part-visual loop exit #2a in `phase-5-embedding.ts`) detects parts whose clamped left-top edge already sits at or beyond the screenshot bounds (`top >= imgHeight || left >= imgWidth`) **before the clamp**, and writes the terminal `bbox_unresolvable` marker for them. Such fully-off-screen parts have zero croppable pixels; without this guard, `Math.max(1, …)` floored the available span to 1 so the existing `cropWidth <= 0 || cropHeight <= 0` guard never fired, Sharp `.extract()` threw `extract_area: bad extract area`, the error routed to the transient DINOv2 catch (exit #3), and the row stayed permanently pending — leaving the backfill completeness count perpetually short (the post-PR3 stripe.com verification observed 22 off-screen WebGL parts stuck pending). A new `partVisualSkippedBboxUnresolvable` counter records the count. An over-termination guard limits the marker to the **fully** off-screen case: partially-visible parts (some croppable pixels inside the viewport) keep flowing through the clamp and are NOT terminal-marked. (b) **SQL migration SPDX header (AGPL §5(a)/(b) compliance, CO-T4-04 closure)**: the `SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-only` header was added to `packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260523090000_add_part_visual_skip_reason/migration.sql`, satisfying the SQL-migration SPDX-header policy that previously lacked code-level closure (CO-T4-04). (c) **Quality Gate PASS**: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify`. (d) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) modification notice**: see below.
  - **JP — 変更**: 下記 F1–F4 V2 本体 landing に対する増分 closure。(a) **Off-screen part_visual marker-gap closure (NF-TPA-02)**: 新規 off-screen precondition guard (Phase 5 part-visual loop exit #2a、`phase-5-embedding.ts`) が、clamp された left-top edge が既に screenshot 境界に達している/超えている part (`top >= imgHeight || left >= imgWidth`) を **clamp 前** に検出し、terminal `bbox_unresolvable` marker を書込む。この完全 off-screen な part は croppable pixel が 0 のため、guard がないと `Math.max(1, …)` が利用可能 span を 1 に floor し、既存の `cropWidth <= 0 || cropHeight <= 0` guard が発火せず、Sharp `.extract()` が `extract_area: bad extract area` を throw、error が transient DINOv2 catch (exit #3) に routing され、row が永久 pending のままになる — backfill 完全性カウントが永続的に不足する (post-PR3 stripe.com 検証で 22 個の off-screen WebGL part が pending 滞留と観測)。新 `partVisualSkippedBboxUnresolvable` counter が件数を記録。over-termination guard が marker を **完全** off-screen の場合に限定: 部分的に可視な part (viewport 内に croppable pixel あり) は clamp を通過し続け terminal marking されない。(b) **SQL migration SPDX header (AGPL §5(a)/(b) compliance、CO-T4-04 closure)**: `packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260523090000_add_part_visual_skip_reason/migration.sql` に `SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-only` header を追加、従来 code-level closure を欠いていた SQL-migration SPDX-header policy (CO-T4-04) を満たす。(c) **Quality Gate PASS**: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify`。(d) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**: 下記参照。

- **Part visual terminal-skip F1–F4 (V2) — `feat/part-visual-terminal-skip-f1-f4` (IO Impl Decision anchor `019e5259-28fb`; T+1d 2026-05-24)** / **Part visual terminal-skip F1–F4 (V2)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: (a) **Part visual terminal-skip per-row marker**: new nullable column `component_part_embeddings.visual_skip_reason` (TEXT, CHECK-constrained to `bbox_invalid` / `bbox_unresolvable` or NULL, partial index on non-NULL) added via migration `20260523090000_add_part_visual_skip_reason`. A non-NULL marker excludes the row from the part-visual pending query, structurally closing the infinite re-fetch loop where a part terminal-skipped due to an unresolvable bounding box kept satisfying `visual_embedding IS NULL` and was re-fetched forever by the backfill processor. (b) **Single SSOT exclusion predicate**: a shared `partVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` is referenced by all part-visual pending-query callsites (`backfill-status.helper.ts`, `embedding-backfill.service.ts`, `phase-5-embedding.ts`); the terminal subset `EMBEDDING_PART_VISUAL_SKIP_REASONS` is derived (`.filter()`) from the `EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` SSOT (no hardcoded literals). (c) **Phase 5 silent-skip exit classification**: the 3 part-visual silent-skip exits are classified terminal (`bbox_invalid` / `bbox_unresolvable` → marker write) vs real-leak (transient DINOv2 inference failure → kept pending for retry), with the previously-silent zero-size-crop `continue` replaced by a marker write + counter. (d) **Reconciliation `in_progress → failed` GDPR Art.30 audit emit**: `reconcileInProgressRows` now emits a `backfill_reconcile_in_progress_failed` audit log (actor `system:backfill-reconciliation-cron`, 365d retention, `truncateAuditTargetId` PII minimisation, PII-free numeric/enum `details`, non-fatal on emit failure), closing the prior audit gap for the `in_progress`-origin terminal transition. (e) **IPC `BackfillErrorMessage.code` schema narrow** to `z.string().regex(/^P\d{4}$/).optional()` (Prisma-code-only, shrinking the CWE-209 latent surface at the fork IPC boundary). (f) **4 NEW INV-\* standing regression tests** (`INV-PART-VISUAL-SKIP-TERMINAL-001` all-callsite AST pin, `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` part-visual-skip-reason 4-site equality, `INV-BACKFILL-IPC-SANITIZE-ONLY-001`, `INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-003` status-parity revision). (g) **Quality Gate PASS**: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify`. (h) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) modification notice**: see below.
  - **JP — 変更**: (a) **Part visual terminal-skip per-row marker**: 新 nullable column `component_part_embeddings.visual_skip_reason` (TEXT、`bbox_invalid` / `bbox_unresolvable` または NULL に CHECK 制約、非NULL の partial index) を migration `20260523090000_add_part_visual_skip_reason` で追加。非NULL marker は当該 row を part-visual pending query から除外し、bounding box 解決不能で terminal-skip された part が `visual_embedding IS NULL` を満たし続け backfill processor に無限再試行される構造欠陥を恒久 closure。(b) **単一 SSOT exclusion predicate**: 共有 `partVisualPendingExclusionPredicate` を全 part-visual pending-query callsite (`backfill-status.helper.ts`、`embedding-backfill.service.ts`、`phase-5-embedding.ts`) が参照、terminal subset `EMBEDDING_PART_VISUAL_SKIP_REASONS` は `EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` SSOT から derive (`.filter()`、hardcoded literal なし)。(c) **Phase 5 silent-skip exit 分類**: 3 個の part-visual silent-skip exit を terminal (`bbox_invalid` / `bbox_unresolvable` → marker 書込) vs real-leak (DINOv2 推論の一過性失敗 → pending 継続 retry) に分類、従来 silent だった zero-size crop の `continue` を marker 書込 + counter に置換。(d) **Reconciliation `in_progress → failed` GDPR Art.30 audit emit**: `reconcileInProgressRows` が `backfill_reconcile_in_progress_failed` audit log (actor `system:backfill-reconciliation-cron`、365d retention、`truncateAuditTargetId` PII minimisation、PII-free numeric/enum `details`、emit 失敗時 non-fatal) を emit、`in_progress`-origin terminal 遷移の audit gap を closure。(e) **IPC `BackfillErrorMessage.code` schema narrow** を `z.string().regex(/^P\d{4}$/).optional()` 化 (Prisma code 限定、fork IPC 境界の CWE-209 latent surface を縮退)。(f) **4 個の新 INV-\* standing regression test** (`INV-PART-VISUAL-SKIP-TERMINAL-001` 全 callsite AST pin、`INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` part-visual-skip-reason 4-site equality、`INV-BACKFILL-IPC-SANITIZE-ONLY-001`、`INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-003` status-parity 改訂)。(g) **Quality Gate PASS**: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify`。(h) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) 改変通知**: 下記参照。

- **Plan v4.5 PR3 — Per-job fork-only model migration (Worker Thread + persistent ONNX session → per-job `child_process.fork()` + `exit(0)`) — `feat/plan-v4-5-pr3-per-job-fork` (IO Impl Decision V1 APPROVE per anchor `019e46c1-79ae`; User consent gate #4 obtained 2026-05-22 anchor `019e4f97-9afe`; T+1d)** / **Plan v4.5 PR3 — per-job fork-only model migration (Worker Thread + 永続 ONNX session → job 単位 `child_process.fork()` + `exit(0)`) — `feat/plan-v4-5-pr3-per-job-fork`**:
  - **EN — Changed**: (a) **Per-job fork-only model migration**: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` migrates from a Worker Thread + persistent ONNX/DINOv2 session model to a per-job `child_process.fork()` + `exit(0)` model. ONNX/DINOv2 global state is isolated per job at the OS level, structurally eliminating the H1 race (Worker Thread CUDA init false-positive). New opt-in env var `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_FORK_ONLY_MODE_ENABLED` controls the new path; the legacy `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_FORK_ENABLED` flag retains 1-cycle backward compat (the new flag wins on value conflict; stale-config deployments are observable via the new `worker_config_legacy_env_var_detected` audit action through T+30d 2026-06-20, formal removal in Plan v4.6). (b) **Per-job sub-child Redis lock + bootEpoch orphan cleanup**: `acquirePerJobSubChildLock(jobId, nonce, ttlMs)` adds a per-job Redis lock (`reftrix:worker:active:embedding-backfill:job:<jobId>`) with a ≥500ms sub-child spawn rate-limit pinned to the Redis **server-side `redis.call('TIME')` monotonic clock** (CWE-770 mitigation, NOT caller-side `Date.now()`/`process.hrtime`), Lua **boot-time `SCRIPT LOAD` SHA pin** (EVALSHA-only contract, CWE-829/CWE-94 injection-path closure), a discriminated-union acquire result (success / `rate_limited` fail-closed / `race_lost` fail-closed / `redis_unreachable` fail-open SEC-M-3), and `bootEpoch` + `nonce` double-verify orphan cleanup (CWE-367 TOCTOU defense). (c) **6 new `audit_logs` actions** (365d retention + `truncateAuditTargetId` CWE-209 PII minimisation + SSOT-imported actor literal): `worker_config_legacy_env_var_detected` (actor `system:worker-config-validator`), `worker_sub_child_spawn_rate_limit_violated`, `worker_per_job_fork_lock_acquired`, `worker_per_job_fork_lock_race_lost`, `worker_lock_service_unreachable`, `worker_lua_script_reload` (latter 5 actor `system:embedding-backfill-worker`). See `apps/mcp-server/DATA_RETENTION.md` §11.13. (d) **Per-job sub-child crash capture uses Node.js `process.report`** (NOT Google Breakpad), consistent with the §11.12 Plan v3 Wave 3 Task #41 scope correction; the deprecated `REFTRIX_BREAKPAD_UPLOAD_URL` env var path is not used. (e) **5 new INV-\* standing regression tests** for the per-job fork-only model. (f) **Quality Gate PASS**: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify`. (g) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) modification notice**: see below. **LoC governance**: band-counted 1330 insertions (feat 1230 + refactor 100; ADR-0037 §Decision 4 measurement, chore/test/docs excluded) — the forecast under-prediction (+46.2% vs V1 forecast 910) is disclosed as accepted-risk in ADR-0037 §Consequences Negative #4 (n=1 calibration entry, `[CALIBRATION_PENDING]` marker retained).

`exit(0)` 方式に migrate。ONNX/DINOv2 global state を job 単位で OS-level isolate し、H1 race (Worker Thread CUDA init false-positive) を構造的に消滅。新 opt-in env var `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_FORK_ONLY_MODE_ENABLED` - **Plan v4.5 PR1 — Worker observability zero-day + H1 isolation + LCC-H-01 PII sanitisation + NEW-U-11 4-layer disk-full defense (atomic) — `main` (IO Impl Decision V1 APPROVE per anchor `019e388d-809b-738e`; CRITICAL/ACTIVE/priority 95; T+1d 2026-05-19)** / **Plan v4.5 PR1 — Worker observability zero-day + H1 isolation + LCC-H-01 PII sanitisation + NEW-U-11 4-layer disk-full 防御 (atomic) — `main` (IO Impl Decision V1 APPROVE per anchor `019e388d-809b-738e`; CRITICAL/ACTIVE/priority 95; T+1d 2026-05-19)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: (a) **Track P0 — Worker observability zero-day closure**: Worker child stderr is now captured to a dedicated rotated file in addition to the existing `logger.warn` pipe path (Option (b) secondary capture, ADR-0036 V4 §D4 Decision). Plan v3 Wave 3 promised Google Breakpad crash-dump capture but landed only the library wiring — `startCrashReportWatcher` had **zero production callsites** until PR1 retroactively wired it from both `apps/mcp-server/src/scripts/start-workers.ts` and the WorkerSupervisor fork path, closing the wiring gap. Env var drift `REFTRIX_CRASH_DUMP_STAGING_ROOT` (docs) vs `REFTRIX_CRASH_DUMP_ROOT` (impl) preserved at impl SSOT per ADR-0036 V4 §SSOT references. (b) **Track 1 — H1 isolation test (Worker Thread CUDA init false-positive race, 60% confidence per capture-embedding-engineer 6-axis investigation)**: new CLI flag `--force-cpu-provider` added to `apps/mcp-server/src/scripts/start-workers.ts`. When set, all ONNX Runtime sessions force CPU provider regardless of `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES`, allowing operators to confirm or refute H1 in production. New `audit_logs.worker_cpu_provider_forced` action emitted on startup (actor `system:worker-supervisor`, 365d retention, `truncateAuditTargetId` PII-safe). **Hard CI gate removal at T+10d 2026-05-28** per the H1 refuted decision tree (U-07) — if H1 is refuted in production by 2026-05-28, the CLI flag and audit action are formally removed in Plan v4.6 PR-X. (c) **LCC-H-01 — Worker stderr PII sanitisation (CWE-209 information exposure defense, GDPR Art.5(1)(c)/(e) data minimisation contract)**: new utility `apps/mcp-server/src/utils/stderr-write-guard.ts` with `sanitizeStderrChunk` function applying **6 regex patterns** at stderr file write time: (1) URL (`https?://...`); (2) absolute path (`/home/...`, `/tmp/...`, etc.); (3) Windows path (`C:\Users\...`); (4) PostgreSQL connection string (`postgresql://user:pass@host/db`); (5) Base64 embedding (>=100 consecutive `[A-Za-z0-9+/]`); (6) JWT-like (`eyJ...` 3-segment dot-separated Base64). This is **independent from the canonical `sanitizeErrorMessage`** (`utils/sanitize-error.ts`) which is dedicated to Prisma error code mapping (P2002/P2025 → generic message) — the two MUST NOT be cross-applied (capture-embedding-engineer + security-engineer joint scope per U-V45-PR1-01 + U-V45-PR1-03). Sanitisation applies to the dedicated stderr directory (mode `0o700`, file mode `0o600` per Δ10 contract) with **7d retention TTL cron** + **3-stage whitelist** (null byte rejection → realpath verification → `startsWith` assertion against the canonical staging root) deriving from the Plan v3 Wave 3 crash-dump whitelist pattern. (d) **NEW-U-11 — 4-layer disk-full racing defense** (Option (b) secondary capture's structural attack surface elimination): **L1 cron 6h Zod-enforced** (`REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_CRON_INTERVAL_MS` default 21,600,000ms = 6h, min 3,600,000ms = 1h, max 86,400,000ms = 24h hard upper bound via Zod refinement at startup); **L2 40MB per-write preflight** with rotation (file `<base>.<timestamp>.log` suffix on overflow; rotation failure emits `[STDERR_OVERFLOW_DROP]` markup via `logger.warn`); **L3 30s `fs.statfs()` runtime monitor** of `/tmp/reftrix-worker-stderr/` (`available < 1GB` → emits new `audit_logs.worker_stderr_disk_pressure_detected` action (actor `system:worker-supervisor`, 365d retention) + sets `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_REDIRECT_ENABLED=false` for runtime auto-failover + triggers immediate cleanup); **L4 Zod startup enforcement** (`REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_SIZE_CAP_MB` max 50, schema-level reject on overflow). Emergency rollback: `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_DISK_PRESSURE_DEFENSE_ENABLED=false` disables L1-L4 (defense layers only, Option (b) itself rolls back via `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_REDIRECT_ENABLED=false`). (e) **5 new INV-\* standing regression tests landed** (44 subtests, baseline 452 → 457): `INV-WORKER-OBSERVABILITY-001` (AST sweep verifying `startCrashReportWatcher` callsites ≥ 2 from production scripts) + `INV-WORKER-STDERR-ISOLATION-001` (PII redaction assertions for the 6 regex patterns + 7d retention horizon + Δ10 whitelist enforcement) + `INV-WORKER-STDERR-DISK-PRESSURE-001` (NEW-U-11 4-layer defense runtime efficacy assertions: cron interval Zod refinement / preflight rotation / statfs failover trigger / Zod startup reject) + `INV-WORKER-CLI-FLAG-LIFECYCLE-001` (Track 1 `--force-cpu-provider` flag lifecycle assertion with T+10d 2026-05-28 hard CI gate removal entry) + `INV-WIRING-COVERAGE-001` (Plan v3 Wave 3 retrospective wiring verification, prevents future library-only landings). All five tests align with the Wave 5 LCC canonical CWE-209 PII protection pattern (anchor `019df7ab-2f5a`) and the SSOT-derive contract. (f) **Remediation across 4 sub-phases**: (i) `sanitizeStderrChunk` independent design (Option A — new utility, not a `sanitizeErrorMessage` extension); (ii) Wave 5 LCC SSOT fix (BREAKPAD deprecation per Plan v4.5 V2 NEW-U-13, 1-cycle backward compat until T+30d 2026-06-17, Plan v4.6 formal removal); (iii) L3 statfs runtime efficacy (30s probe + auto-failover); (iv) audit emit (2 new `audit_logs` actions inheriting 365d retention + `truncateAuditTargetId` PII minimisation + `system:worker-supervisor` actor naming SSOT). (g) **Quality Gate V2 PASS**: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify && pnpm audit --audit-level=high && pnpm sbom` all returned 0 errors / 0 high-or-critical CVEs. (h) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a)/(b) modification notice**: see below. **Forward-ref**: Plan v3 Wave 3 partial-as-success retrospective notice (Google Breakpad → Node.js `process.report` scope change, `feedback_no_fake_success.md` A-2) is tracked as **Plan v4.6 candidate `L-V46-CAND-XX`** for separate landing per LCC AGPL §5 mandate (FIND-IMPL-LCC-V45-PR1-H-02).

- **Plan v4.4 PR-N — `WorkerSupervisorOptions.restartDelayMs` field formal removal + env-only canonical SSOT consolidation — `main` (IO Impl Decision V0 CONDITIONAL APPROVE per anchor `019e34e6-6ddd-769f-85a5-f1714fa3eac3`; TDA targeted re-audit closure anchor `019e34ef-a7c1-72e7-858b-7b0ec2b36931`; T+1d 2026-05-18)** / **Plan v4.4 PR-N — `WorkerSupervisorOptions.restartDelayMs` field の正式 removal + env-only canonical SSOT 一元化 — `main` (IO Impl Decision V0 CONDITIONAL APPROVE per anchor `019e34e6-6ddd-769f-85a5-f1714fa3eac3`; TDA targeted re-audit closure anchor `019e34ef-a7c1-72e7-858b-7b0ec2b36931`; T+1d 2026-05-18)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: (a) **Internal type-level breaking change**: The `WorkerSupervisorOptions.restartDelayMs` field is **formally removed** from the `WorkerSupervisor` constructor options interface in `apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor.service.ts`. This completes the env-only canonical SSOT consolidation established in ADR-0035 §Decision 3 by eliminating the previous two-source-of-truth ambiguity (legacy field coexisting with `getRestartDelayMsForType(workerType)` module-level export). External consumers verified at 0 (`apps/cli/`, `example/`, OSS public surface all return 0 hits for both `WorkerSupervisorOptions` and `restartDelayMs` per Plan v4.4 PR-N §7.3 joint verify); this is an **internal type-level breaking change** with no external API impact. (b) **CWE-770 boundary preservation**: `PAGE_RESTART_DELAY_MS_MIN` constant raised from `1` → `500` in `worker-supervisor.service.ts` (Path A per SEC M-01 mandate). The legacy factory at the removed L917 had enforced `min=500` while the §Decision 3 helper enforced `min=1`; production behaviour was always served by the factory so production-effective boundary was `min=500`. The constant bump preserves this status-quo production boundary inside the env-only canonical SSOT, structurally protecting the Plan v4.1 CWE-770 41.67h/day DoS closure contract. (c) **Orphan constant cleanup**: `DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_MS = 1000` module-level constant removed (Path (a) IO-recommended, env-only canonical alignment). (d) **2 new INV standing regression tests landed** (baseline 450 → 452): `INV-WORKER-RESTART-DELAY-SSOT-001` (`apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/inv-worker-restart-delay-ssot-001-env-only.test.ts`, ts-morph 3-kind AST sweep `PropertySignature` / `PropertyAssignment` / `VariableDeclaration` over `apps/mcp-server/src/**/*.ts` forward-prevents regression of `restartDelayMs` / `DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_MS` literal reintroduction) + `INV-PAGE-RESTART-DELAY-MIN-BOUND-CWE770-001` (`inv-page-restart-delay-min-bound-cwe770-001.test.ts`, asserts `getRestartDelayMsForType("page")` rejects sub-500ms `WORKER_RESTART_DELAY_MS` env values and falls back to `DEFAULT_PAGE_RESTART_DELAY_MS = 3000` per Plan v4.4 PR-N §4.4 acceptance criterion). Both tests aligned with Wave 5 LCC canonical SSOT-derive pattern (anchor `019df7ab-2f5a`). (e) **Package version**: `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server` minor bump `0.5.1 → 0.6.0` per semver.org §4 pre-1.0 convention (internal type-level breaking change qualifies for pre-1.0 minor bump). (f) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a) modification notice**: see notice below.

**internal type-level breaking change** で external API impact なし。(b) **CWE-770 boundary 維持**: `worker-supervisor.service.ts` 内の `PAGE_RESTART_DELAY_MS_MIN` 定数を `1` → `500` に raise (Path A per SEC M-01 mandate)。Removed L917 の legacy factory が `min=500` を enforce していた一方、§Decision 3 helper は `min=1` のみで、production 動作は factory が常に提供していたため production-effective boundary は `min=500` だった。定数 bump で status-quo production boundary を env-only canonical SSOT 内で structurally に保持、Plan v4.1 CWE-770 41.67h/day DoS closure contract を構造的に protect。(c) **Orphan 定数の cleanup**: `DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_MS = 1000` module-level 定数を removal (Path (a) IO-recommended、env-only canonical alignment)。(d) **新規 2 個の INV standing regression test を landed** (baseline 450 → 452): `INV-WORKER-RESTART-DELAY-SSOT-001` (`apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/inv-worker-restart-delay-ssot-001-env-only.test.ts`、ts-morph 3-kind AST sweep `PropertySignature` / `PropertyAssignment` / `VariableDeclaration` を `apps/mcp-server/src/**/*.ts` に対し実行、`restartDelayMs` / `DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_MS` literal 再導入の regression を forward-prevent) + `INV-PAGE-RESTART-DELAY-MIN-BOUND-CWE770-001` (`inv-page-restart-delay-min-bound-cwe770-001.test.ts`、`getRestartDelayMsForType("page")` が sub-500ms `WORKER_RESTART_DELAY_MS` env value を reject し `DEFAULT_PAGE_RESTART_DELAY_MS = 3000` に fall back することを assert、Plan v4.4 PR-N §4.4 acceptance criterion)。両 test ともに Wave 5 LCC canonical SSOT-derive pattern (anchor `019df7ab-2f5a`) と整合。(e) **Package version**: `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server` minor bump `0.5.1 → 0.6.0` (semver.org §4 pre-1.0 convention、internal type-level breaking change は pre-1.0 minor bump 要件を満たす)。(f) **AGPL-3.0-only §5(a) modification notice**: 下記参照。

### Security / セキュリティ

- **CO-22 protobufjs CVE closure — `feat/plan-v4-2-pr-c-docs` (SEC M-NEW-5 closure per IO Impl Decision V2 anchor `019e2ce2-9859`; T+1d 2026-05-17)** / **CO-22 protobufjs CVE closure — `feat/plan-v4-2-pr-c-docs` (SEC M-NEW-5 closure per IO Impl Decision V2 anchor `019e2ce2-9859`; T+1d 2026-05-17)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: `pnpm.overrides.protobufjs` bumped `>=7.5.5` → `>=8.0.2` in root `package.json`. Resolves 4 high-severity CVEs in the `protobufjs` transitive dependency surfaced via `testcontainers@11.14.0` (→ `dockerode@4.0.10` → `@grpc/grpc-js@1.14.3` / `@grpc/proto-loader@0.7.15` / `@grpc/proto-loader@0.8.0`) and `@huggingface/transformers@3.8.1` (→ `onnxruntime-web`): (a) **GHSA-66ff-xgx4-vchm** — code injection through bytes field defaults; (b) **GHSA-75px-5xx7-5xc7** — code generation gadget reachable after prototype pollution; (c) **GHSA-jvwf-75h9-cwgg** — process-wide DoS through unsafe option paths; (d) **GHSA-685m-2w69-288q** — DoS through unbounded protobuf recursion. After the override + `pnpm install --fix-lockfile`, all `protobufjs` consumers (mcp-server, ml, testcontainers chain) resolve to `protobufjs@8.3.0`. The stale `protobufjs@8.0.1` lockfile entry is removed; only `protobufjs@8.3.0` remains in `pnpm-lock.yaml`. `pnpm audit --audit-level=high` returns 0 high/critical findings. CO-22 deadline 2026-05-27 forwarded to T+1d 2026-05-17 (Plan v4.2 integral landing per `MEMORY.md` deadline-T+1d directive).
  - **JP — 修正**: `pnpm.overrides.protobufjs` をルート `package.json` で `>=7.5.5` → `>=8.0.2` に bump。`testcontainers@11.14.0` (→ `dockerode@4.0.10` → `@grpc/grpc-js@1.14.3` / `@grpc/proto-loader@0.7.15` / `@grpc/proto-loader@0.8.0`) および `@huggingface/transformers@3.8.1` (→ `onnxruntime-web`) を経由する `protobufjs` transitive dependency 経路で残存していた 4 件の high severity CVE を closure: (a) **GHSA-66ff-xgx4-vchm** — bytes field defaults 経由の code injection; (b) **GHSA-75px-5xx7-5xc7** — prototype pollution 後の code generation gadget; (c) **GHSA-jvwf-75h9-cwgg** — unsafe option paths 経由の process-wide DoS; (d) **GHSA-685m-2w69-288q** — unbounded protobuf recursion 経由の DoS。override 適用 + `pnpm install --fix-lockfile` 後、`protobufjs` consumer (mcp-server、ml、testcontainers chain) は全て `protobufjs@8.3.0` に解決。`pnpm-lock.yaml` から stale な `protobufjs@8.0.1` entry を除去 (`protobufjs@8.3.0` のみ残存)。`pnpm audit --audit-level=high` は 0 件 high/critical を返却。CO-22 deadline 2026-05-27 を `MEMORY.md` deadline-T+1d directive に従い 2026-05-17 に前倒し (Plan v4.2 integral landing)。

### Changed / 変更

- **Plan v4.3 PR-M — INFRA-EMBEDDING-MOTION-SIGABRT-001 closure (callback-based exit teardown symmetry) — `main` (IO Impl Decision V1 CONDITIONAL re-audit mode `documentation-only check` per anchor `019e30c1-597e-761f-8a19-6ef7cec9ce5d`; T+1d 2026-05-17)** / **Plan v4.3 PR-M — INFRA-EMBEDDING-MOTION-SIGABRT-001 closure (コールバックベース exit teardown 対称性) — `main` (IO Impl Decision V1 CONDITIONAL re-audit mode `documentation-only check` per anchor `019e30c1-597e-761f-8a19-6ef7cec9ce5d`; T+1d 2026-05-17)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: (a) **`registerCompletedListenerAndExit` helper extended with optional `disposeFn?: () => Promise<void>` parameter** (`apps/mcp-server/src/workers/shared/post-job-lifecycle.ts`). Closes INFRA-EMBEDDING-MOTION-SIGABRT-001 by symmetrically restoring the ADR-0019 close-before-dispose ordering contract on the planned-restart path. Plan v4.2 callback-based exit (ADR-0034) had unintentionally short-circuited the close handler, causing `disposeEmbeddingPipeline()` to be skipped at planned restart. The OS subsequently reaped the ONNX Worker Thread mid-state → ORT native pthread received abnormal signal → glibc `abort()` → **SIGABRT (11 rows per cycle on stripe.com)**. The new optional `disposeFn` parameter wraps the dispose call in `Promise.race([disposeFn(), setTimeout(EMBEDDING_DISPOSE_CEILING_MS)])` (default 5000ms) within the synchronous listener body (SEC M-NEW-1 mandate preserved via `.finally(() => process.exit(0))` microtask scheduling). EmbeddingBackfillWorker binds `disposeFn = () => sharedLayoutEmbeddingService.disposeEmbeddingPipeline()`; PageAnalyzeWorker omits `disposeFn` and retains legacy behaviour. ADR-0034 8-stage flow extended with **Stage 8.5 (teardown ceiling race)** between Stage 8 (listener fires) and Stage 9 (`process.exit(0)`). (b) **Per-type restart cooldown**: new env var `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_RESTART_DELAY_MS` (default **8000ms**, range 500..86_400_000ms) for EmbeddingBackfillWorker via new module-level export `getRestartDelayMsForType(workerType)`. PageAnalyzeWorker retains `WORKER_RESTART_DELAY_MS` (default 3000ms). 4 callsites in `worker-supervisor-lifecycle.service.ts` (lines 738/745/763/780) updated; 60_000ms TTL-fallback path at line 767 preserved. (c) **New env var `EMBEDDING_DISPOSE_CEILING_MS`** (default 5000ms, range 100..60000ms) for the Promise.race fail-open ceiling. (d) **New SSOT constants** in `apps/mcp-server/src/audit/audit-actions.ts`: `AUDIT_ACTION_EMBEDDING_DISPOSE_TIMEOUT = "embedding_dispose_timeout" as const`, `AUDIT_ACTOR_EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_WORKER`, `AUDIT_ACTOR_PAGE_ANALYZE_WORKER`. New helper `getWorkerActorName(workerType)` with exhaustive `never`-narrowing switch for compile-time `WorkerLifecycleType` extension gate. (e) **`Promise<void>` mutex on `disposeEmbeddingPipeline()`** (`apps/mcp-server/src/services/layout-embedding.service.ts`, `inFlightDispose` field) closes FIND-PLAN-V43-H-01 (concurrent dispose race). (f) **New audit_logs action `embedding_dispose_timeout`** (365d retention via `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_RETENTION_DAYS`, actor `system:embedding-backfill-worker`, `targetId` truncated via `truncateAuditTargetId` SSOT, `details` PII-free `{ workerType, ceilingMs }`). L1.5 SLO_MARKER source: Plan v4.4 dispatch MUST evaluate when ceiling-path firing rate exceeds 5/100 cycles. (g) **AST gate `verify-completed-listener-sync.mjs` enhanced** (multi-line regex + 3-branch race-winner detection) and continues to enforce SEC M-NEW-1 synchronous-only listener body on 3 files. (h) **8 new standing regression tests landed** (baseline 442 → 450): INV-EMBEDDING-WORKER-INIT-001 (4 tests, callback exit + dispose), INV-EMBEDDING-DISPOSE-IDEMPOTENT-001 (3 tests, mutex idempotency), INV-AUDIT-EMIT-SSOT-IMPORT-001 Test 8 (production sweep for bare `system:embedding-backfill` literal). ADR-0035 (Callback-Exit Teardown Symmetry, ~480 LoC bilingual JP/EN) drafted; Decision Lineage `0019→0020→0026→0034→0035` documented. ADR-0035 extends ADR-0019 + ADR-0034 — it does NOT supersede them, preserving Plan v4.2 SEC H-1 (CWE-833) + Plan v4.1 CWE-770 closures.
  - **JP — 変更**: (a) **`registerCompletedListenerAndExit` helper に optional `disposeFn?: () => Promise<void>` パラメタを追加** (`apps/mcp-server/src/workers/shared/post-job-lifecycle.ts`)。INFRA-EMBEDDING-MOTION-SIGABRT-001 を planned-restart path で ADR-0019 close-before-dispose ordering contract を対称的に復元することで closure。Plan v4.2 callback-based exit (ADR-0034) は close handler を意図せず短絡し、planned restart で `disposeEmbeddingPipeline()` を skip していた。結果 OS が ONNX Worker Thread を mid-state で reap → ORT native pthread が abnormal signal を受領 → glibc `abort()` → **SIGABRT (stripe.com で 11 rows/cycle)** が発生。新 optional `disposeFn` parameter は同期 listener body 内で `Promise.race([disposeFn(), setTimeout(EMBEDDING_DISPOSE_CEILING_MS)])` (default 5000ms) で wrap (SEC M-NEW-1 mandate は `.finally(() => process.exit(0))` microtask scheduling で維持)。EmbeddingBackfillWorker は `disposeFn = () => sharedLayoutEmbeddingService.disposeEmbeddingPipeline()` を bind; PageAnalyzeWorker は `disposeFn` を省略し legacy 動作を保持。ADR-0034 8-stage flow に Stage 8 (listener fires) と Stage 9 (`process.exit(0)`) の間に **Stage 8.5 (teardown ceiling race)** を挿入。(b) **Per-type restart cooldown**: 新 env var `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_RESTART_DELAY_MS` (default **8000ms**、range 500..86_400_000ms) を EmbeddingBackfillWorker 用に追加、新 module-level export `getRestartDelayMsForType(workerType)` 経由。PageAnalyzeWorker は `WORKER_RESTART_DELAY_MS` (default 3000ms) を保持。`worker-supervisor-lifecycle.service.ts` の 4 callsites (line 738/745/763/780) を更新; line 767 の 60_000ms TTL-fallback path は不変。(c) **新 env var `EMBEDDING_DISPOSE_CEILING_MS`** (default 5000ms、range 100..60000ms) を Promise.race fail-open ceiling 用に追加。(d) **新 SSOT 定数** を `apps/mcp-server/src/audit/audit-actions.ts` に追加: `AUDIT_ACTION_EMBEDDING_DISPOSE_TIMEOUT = "embedding_dispose_timeout" as const`、`AUDIT_ACTOR_EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_WORKER`、`AUDIT_ACTOR_PAGE_ANALYZE_WORKER`。新 helper `getWorkerActorName(workerType)` で exhaustive `never`-narrowing switch (compile-time `WorkerLifecycleType` extension gate)。(e) **`Promise<void>` mutex on `disposeEmbeddingPipeline()`** (`apps/mcp-server/src/services/layout-embedding.service.ts` の `inFlightDispose` field) で FIND-PLAN-V43-H-01 (concurrent dispose race) を closure。(f) **新 audit_logs action `embedding_dispose_timeout`** (365d retention via `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_RETENTION_DAYS`、actor `system:embedding-backfill-worker`、`targetId` を `truncateAuditTargetId` SSOT 経由で truncate、`details` は PII-free `{ workerType, ceilingMs }`)。L1.5 SLO_MARKER source: ceiling-path firing rate が 5/100 cycles を超過した場合 Plan v4.4 dispatch MUST evaluate。(g) **AST gate `verify-completed-listener-sync.mjs` 強化** (multi-line regex + 3-branch race-winner detection)、SEC M-NEW-1 synchronous-only listener body を 3 file で継続 enforce。(h) **新規 8 standing regression test を landed** (baseline 442 → 450): INV-EMBEDDING-WORKER-INIT-001 (4 tests, callback exit + dispose)、INV-EMBEDDING-DISPOSE-IDEMPOTENT-001 (3 tests, mutex idempotency)、INV-AUDIT-EMIT-SSOT-IMPORT-001 Test 8 (production sweep for bare `system:embedding-backfill` literal)。ADR-0035 (Callback-Exit Teardown Symmetry、~480 LoC bilingual JP/EN) を drafted; Decision Lineage `0019→0020→0026→0034→0035` を文書化。ADR-0035 は ADR-0019 + ADR-0034 を **extends** するのみで **supersede しない**ため、Plan v4.2 SEC H-1 (CWE-833) + Plan v4.1 CWE-770 closure を構造的に保持。

- **Plan v4.2 PR-L — Carryover L closure (TDA-V42-L-01 wrapper formal removal + TDA-V42-L-02 helper extraction) — `main` (SEC-V42-L-NEW-4 mandate per audit anchor `019e2d9b-f7bf-7356-a336-c24f6806b4d8`; T+1d 2026-05-17)** / **Plan v4.2 PR-L — Carryover L closure (TDA-V42-L-01 wrapper 正式削除 + TDA-V42-L-02 helper 抽出) — `main` (SEC-V42-L-NEW-4 mandate per audit anchor `019e2d9b-f7bf-7356-a336-c24f6806b4d8`; T+1d 2026-05-17)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: (a) **`applyPostJobLifecycleGateAlwaysExits` wrapper formally removed** from `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/shared/post-job-lifecycle.ts` (TDA-V42-L-01 closure). SEC-V42-L-NEW-4 audit identified **CWE-705 (Incorrect Control Flow Scoping) primary + CWE-754 (Improper Check for Unusual or Exceptional Conditions) secondary** risk because the wrapper had zero production callers and only test-only retention, exhibiting a dead-code surface in the production worker package. The Layer 1 type-level `Promise<never>` guarantee is now structurally provided by the AST gate `apps/mcp-server/scripts/verify-completed-listener-sync.mjs` (synchronous-only listener body enforcement). (b) **NEW shared helper `registerCompletedListenerAndExit`** in `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/shared/post-job-lifecycle.ts` (TDA-V42-L-02 closure) consolidates the previously duplicated `worker.once('completed', () => process.exit(0))` boilerplate between `page-analyze-worker.ts` and `embedding-backfill-worker.ts` (~32 LoC duplication removed). jobId truncation routes through `truncateAuditTargetId()` SSOT (canonical CWE-209 PII protection pattern). (c) **AST gate `verify-completed-listener-sync.mjs` TARGETS extended** to include the helper file so synchronous-only listener body enforcement remains intact for helper-routed listeners (SEC M-NEW-1 contract preservation). - **Plan v4.2 PR-A/B/C — Callback-Based Worker Exit Pattern (BullMQ Native Flow Preservation) — `feat/plan-v4-2-pr-c-docs` (integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync per IO Plan Decision V2 anchor `019e2c88-7d5f-747f-be8e-b181fd9bd9ca`; T+1d 2026-05-17)** / **Plan v4.2 PR-A/B/C — コールバックベース Worker 終了パターン (BullMQ ネイティブフロー保存) — `feat/plan-v4-2-pr-c-docs` (integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync per IO Plan Decision V2 anchor `019e2c88-7d5f-747f-be8e-b181fd9bd9ca`; T+1d 2026-05-17)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: (a) **`applyPostJobLifecycleGate` signature**: `Promise<never>` → `Promise<void>` (gate-only). Helper now performs `worker.pause(true)` (fetchNext=false guarantee) + memory check, and returns to caller. **Never calls `process.exit()`, never calls `worker.resume()`** (INV-WORKER-NO-RESUME-001 invocation contract). (b) **NEW wrapper helper `applyPostJobLifecycleGateAlwaysExits: Promise<never>`** for type-level guarantee at processor success-path callsites — caller is statically guaranteed to terminate via the pre-registered BullMQ `completed` listener. (c) **Worker constructor pre-registers `worker.once('completed', () => process.exit(0))` listener** in `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` and `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` (callback-based exit). The listener body is **synchronous-only** (no `await`, no `setImmediate`, no `process.nextTick`, no `setTimeout`) per SEC M-NEW-1 contract. (d) **NEW AST gate `apps/mcp-server/scripts/verify-completed-listener-sync.mjs`** (`pnpm verify:listener-sync`) enforces synchronous-only listener body via AST traversal, blocking PRs that introduce async operations in the `completed` listener. (e) **Invariant updates**: INV-NEXT-JOB-RACE-001 sub 1 split into sub 1a (gate-only verification) + sub 1b (listener-driven exit verification); INV-WORKER-NO-RESUME-001 redefined as an invocation contract (`worker.resume()` MUST NOT be called from production callsites); NEW INV-WORKER-PROCESS-EXIT-BEFORE-NEXT-JOB-001 with 3 sub-tests (1: listener pre-registration; 2: callback exit on `completed` event; 3: no resumption after exit). (f) **ADR-0009 Bug 1 portion Superseded by ADR-0034**; ADR-0009 Bug 2 / Screenshot deletion unification remains active under PR6 7d TTL cron (orthogonal contract).
  - **JP — 変更**: (a) **`applyPostJobLifecycleGate` signature**: `Promise<never>` → `Promise<void>` (gate-only)。Helper は `worker.pause(true)` (fetchNext=false guarantee) + memory check のみ実行し caller に return する。**`process.exit()` を呼ばない、`worker.resume()` を呼ばない** (INV-WORKER-NO-RESUME-001 invocation contract)。(b) **新 wrapper helper `applyPostJobLifecycleGateAlwaysExits: Promise<never>`** を追加し、processor success-path callsite で型レベルで terminate guarantee を提供 — caller は pre-registered BullMQ `completed` listener 経由で必ず exit する。(c) **Worker constructor で `worker.once('completed', () => process.exit(0))` listener を pre-register** (`apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` + `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts`、callback-based exit)。Listener body は **synchronous-only** (`await` / `setImmediate` / `process.nextTick` / `setTimeout` 使用禁止) per SEC M-NEW-1 contract。(d) **新 AST gate `apps/mcp-server/scripts/verify-completed-listener-sync.mjs`** (`pnpm verify:listener-sync`) を追加、AST traversal で synchronous-only 強制し `completed` listener 内で async operation を導入する PR を block。(e) **不変条件更新**: INV-NEXT-JOB-RACE-001 sub 1 を sub 1a (gate-only verification) + sub 1b (listener-driven exit verification) に split; INV-WORKER-NO-RESUME-001 を invocation contract として redefine (`worker.resume()` を production callsite で呼んではならない); 新 INV-WORKER-PROCESS-EXIT-BEFORE-NEXT-JOB-001 (3 sub-tests: 1. listener pre-registration、2. `completed` event での callback exit、3. exit 後の resumption 不在)。(f) **ADR-0009 Bug 1 portion を ADR-0034 で Supersede**; ADR-0009 Bug 2 / Screenshot deletion unification は PR6 7d TTL cron 経路で active (orthogonal contract)。

- **Wave 4 V4 Carryover Closure — `feat/wave-4-v4-carryover-2026-05-13` (integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync per IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e1e64-184c-7421-8c09-a82854086b10`; T+1d 2026-05-13)** [Wave-4-V4-2026-05-13] / **Wave 4 V4 carryover closure — `feat/wave-4-v4-carryover-2026-05-13` (integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync per IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e1e64-184c-7421-8c09-a82854086b10`; T+1d 2026-05-13)** [Wave-4-V4-2026-05-13]:
  - **EN — Fixed**: (a) **NEW `packages/ml/scripts/repair-e5-cache.ts` (448 LoC, SPDX `AGPL-3.0-only`)**: HuggingFace Hub-backed integrity check + repair tool for the `Xenova/multilingual-e5-base/onnx/model.onnx` cache used by transformers.js 3.8.1. Provides SHA-256 integrity verification (`EXPECTED_SHA256 = 84a4d426...`), atomic temp-file rename to prevent partial-write races, `huggingface.co` host allowlist for SSRF defense, 1.5 GB content-length cap (CWE-770), and path-traversal guard against `CACHE_REL` resolved outside repo root. Resolves cold-start "Protobuf parsing failed" races across parallel vitest workers. Exposed via `pnpm --filter @reftrixmcp/ml repair:e5-cache`. (b) **Hono override `4.12.16 → 4.12.18`** in root `package.json` `pnpm.overrides`: closes 3 GHSA advisories — GHSA-qp7p-654g-cw7p, GHSA-p77w-8qqv-26rm, GHSA-hm8q-7f3q-5f36. (c) **`@cyclonedx/cdxgen` devDependency `^12.1.2 → ^12.3.3`** in root `package.json`: closes GHSA-qhh4-458h-xwh2. (d) **`embedding-backfill-worker.ts:833` SSOT-derive (CO-21 closure)**: `webPageId.slice(0, 8)` literal in the `job.log()` start interpolation migrated to `webPageId.slice(0, AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH)` per Wave 5 LCC canonical CWE-209 PII Protection Pattern (FIND-IMPL-LCC-PATCH-W5-02, anchor `019df7ab-2f5a` / Registry §13.14). All `webPageId` truncation sites in production code now derive from the single `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS` SSOT. (e) **Test files Wave 5 partial `vi.mock` migration**: `apps/mcp-server/tests/workers/phases/shared/bbox-resolution.helper.test.ts` `vi.mock("../../../../src/services/audit-log.service")` factory rewritten to `async (importOriginal) => { const actual = await importOriginal<...>(); return { ...actual, getAuditLogService: ... }; }` so the `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS` SSOT re-exports through the mocked module — keeping callers that compute `webPageId.slice(0, AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH)` working under the mock. `apps/mcp-server/tests/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.test.ts` PII-safe logging test rewritten as 2 `it()` blocks asserting the SSOT-derived canonical surface plus the `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS` import precondition (replaces the prior literal `webPageId\.slice\(0, 8\) \+ "\.\.\."` regex assertion). (f) **`scripts/hooks/block-destructive-git.sh`**: SPDX header (`AGPL-3.0-only`) added per `scripts/sync-oss.sh` SPDX gate.

- **CO-5 Canonical Pattern Migration — `feature/co-5-canonical-pattern-migration` (integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync Option C retry per IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019dfe71-e367`; 2026-05-07)** / **CO-5 Canonical Pattern Migration — `feature/co-5-canonical-pattern-migration` (integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync Option C retry per IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019dfe71-e367`; 2026-05-07)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: (a) **B path migration (13 callsites / log output truncation)**: 13 callsites in `apps/mcp-server/src/services/backfill-reconciliation.service.ts` migrated from hardcoded `slice(0, 8) + "..."` literal pattern to canonical `truncateAuditTargetId()` helper deriving from `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH` SSOT (Wave 5 LCC-endorsed canonical CWE-209 PII Protection Pattern, anchor `019df7ab-2f5a` / FIND-IMPL-LCC-PATCH-W5-02 / Registry §13.14). Each migrated callsite enforces the `+ "..."` suffix contract for log output PII minimisation per GDPR Art.30 audit trail consistency. (b) **A path migration (2 callsites / SQL LIKE pattern)**: 2 callsites in `apps/mcp-server/src/services/gdpr-deletion.service.ts` `searchLogsAnonymized` deletion query path migrated to length-only SSOT (`AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH`) via NEW `truncateProfileIdForSqlLike()` helper with length-invariant guard (short-ID rejection for SQL semantic completeness). A path is NOT a log output / NOT a PII protection pattern — it is a SQL semantic preservation pattern; suffix differs (`%` vs `...`) per use-case, length-only invariant shared with B path via SSOT. ### Added / 追加

- **PR-1 GPU-COORD — Phase 5 fork-child VRAM probe drives provider selection (IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e567d`; ADR-0038; 2026-05-24)** / **PR-1 GPU-COORD — Phase 5 fork-child VRAM probe が provider 選択を駆動 (IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e567d`; ADR-0038; 2026-05-24)**:
  - **EN — Added**: The Phase 5 embedding fork-child now runs a **VRAM probe** (`phase-5-gpu-probe.ts`) wired into the pre-flight of execution-provider selection (`detectExecutionProvider`/DINOv2 init), replacing the previous no-op GPU manager stub whose return value the fork-child never consumed. When `PHASE5_FORK_GPU_PROBE_ENABLED=true` (default), the probe reuses `queryVram()` (`src/services/vision/vram-utils.ts`) to read free VRAM and intentionally selects CUDA only when free VRAM ≥ the workload threshold, otherwise explicitly selecting CPU (`phase-5-fork-orchestrator.ts` builds the child env accordingly; the child re-confirms child-locally). VRAM thresholds (`DINOV2_MIN_VRAM_MB` / `EMBEDDING_MIN_VRAM_MB`) were extracted to a new fork-child-importable leaf module `src/services/vision/vram-thresholds.ts` (SSOT; zero transitive dependency on the in-process `gpu-resource-manager.ts`, preserving the ADR-0037 fork-only boundary). On a CPU-fallback (degraded) outcome the parent emits a new `embedding_cpu_fallback_degraded` `audit_logs` action (actor `system:phase5-init`, SSOT via `audit-actions.ts`, PII-free numeric VRAM + reason/workload enum, 365d retention, GDPR Art.30). **Zero new child→parent IPC message types** (FIND-PLAN-M-01); the existing IPC schemas in `phase-5-child-ipc.ts` **newly gained `.strict()` on all 10 top-level schemas** (FIND-PLAN-M-02 / SEC-M-1), achieving SEC H-2 unknown-key-reject parity with `embedding-backfill-ipc.ts` (the file had **0** `.strict()` applications before this PR — corrects the earlier doc claim of "existing `.strict()`"). Two CI-failing standing regressions land in the worker-lifecycle domain: `INV-GPU-PROBE-DRIVES-PROVIDER-001` (probe result actually drives CUDA-vs-CPU selection — prevents shipping an inert change) and `INV-GPU-PROBE-LEAF-IMPORT-001` (the threshold leaf module does not transitively import `gpu-resource-manager.ts`). The dead no-op stub was fully removed (FIND-PLAN-L-01). **Honesty boundary (directive ⑤ no-fake-success)**: this PR establishes that the probe is _wired_ (it can drive provider selection, verified on CPU-only CI via a CUDA-eligible/CPU-only probe-stub fixture). It does **NOT** yet demonstrate a real GPU completion run with second-scale latency — actual CUDA-session GPU runs and p95 latency confirmation are deferred to **PR-7 SCALE-VERIFY** (no GPU run exists yet, so p95 is undefined-by-design). Rollback: `PHASE5_FORK_GPU_PROBE_ENABLED=false` reverts to the prior CPU-pinned no-op behavior. No DB migration (env var only). New env var: `PHASE5_FORK_GPU_PROBE_ENABLED` (default `true`, Zod boolean, opt-out).
  - **JP — 追加**: Phase 5 embedding fork-child が **VRAM probe** (`phase-5-gpu-probe.ts`) を execution-provider 選択 (`detectExecutionProvider`/DINOv2 init) の pre-flight に**配線**するようになった。従来の no-op GPU manager stub (戻り値が fork-child で消費されない inert change) を置換ではなく配線として正す。`PHASE5_FORK_GPU_PROBE_ENABLED=true` (default) のとき probe は `queryVram()` (`src/services/vision/vram-utils.ts`) を再利用して free VRAM を読み、free VRAM ≥ workload 閾値のときのみ CUDA を意図選択し、未満なら CPU を明示選択する (`phase-5-fork-orchestrator.ts` が child env を構築、child は child-local で再確認)。VRAM 閾値定数 (`DINOV2_MIN_VRAM_MB` / `EMBEDDING_MIN_VRAM_MB`) を fork-child-importable な新規 leaf module `src/services/vision/vram-thresholds.ts` に抽出 (SSOT、in-process `gpu-resource-manager.ts` への transitive dep ゼロ、ADR-0037 fork-only 境界保全)。CPU-fallback (degraded) 時は parent が新規 `embedding_cpu_fallback_degraded` `audit_logs` action を emit (actor `system:phase5-init`、`audit-actions.ts` SSOT 経由、PII-free な VRAM 数値 + reason/workload enum、365d retention、GDPR Art.30)。**新規 child→parent IPC message type 0 個** (FIND-PLAN-M-01)。`phase-5-child-ipc.ts` の既存 IPC schema は **10 top-level schema 全てに `.strict()` を新規付与** (FIND-PLAN-M-02 / SEC-M-1)、`embedding-backfill-ipc.ts` との SEC H-2 unknown-key-reject parity を達成 (本 PR 着地前は `.strict()` 適用 0 個 — 旧 doc の「既存 `.strict()`」記述を訂正)。worker-lifecycle domain に 2 件の CI-failing standing regression を landing: `INV-GPU-PROBE-DRIVES-PROVIDER-001` (probe 結果が CUDA-vs-CPU 選択を実際に駆動する — inert change 出荷防止) + `INV-GPU-PROBE-LEAF-IMPORT-001` (閾値 leaf module が `gpu-resource-manager.ts` を transitively import しない)。dead no-op stub は完全削除 (FIND-PLAN-L-01)。**Honesty 境界 (directive ⑤ no-fake-success)**: 本 PR は probe が _配線済_ (provider 選択を駆動できる、CPU-only CI で CUDA-eligible/CPU-only probe-stub fixture により検証) であることを確立する。秒単位 latency の **実 GPU 完走 run はまだ実証していない** — 実 CUDA-session GPU run と p95 latency 確認は **PR-7 SCALE-VERIFY** に deferred (GPU run が未だ存在しないため p95 は undefined-by-design)。rollback: `PHASE5_FORK_GPU_PROBE_ENABLED=false` で旧 CPU 固定 no-op 挙動に即復帰。DB migration なし (env var のみ)。新規 env var: `PHASE5_FORK_GPU_PROBE_ENABLED` (default `true`、Zod boolean、opt-out)。
  - **AGPL §5(a) Modification Notice / AGPL §5(a) 改変通知 (M-09)**:
    - **EN**: This release modifies the following `apps/mcp-server/` source files for PR-1 GPU-COORD: `src/services/gpu-resource-manager.ts` (threshold constants now import from the leaf SSOT), `src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (no-op stub removal), `src/workers/phases/phase-5-embedding.ts` (JSDoc dep correction + no-op removal), `src/workers/phases/phase-5-child-ipc.ts` (`.strict()` added to 10 schemas), `src/workers/phases/phase-5-fork-orchestrator.ts` (probe wiring + degraded audit emit), `src/workers/phases/phase-5-text-embedding-child.ts` / `phase-5-visual-embedding-child.ts` (child-local provider re-confirm). Adds NEW source files: `src/services/vision/vram-thresholds.ts` (threshold leaf SSOT), `src/workers/phases/phase-5-gpu-probe.ts` (VRAM probe), `src/audit/audit-actions.ts` (`embedding_cpu_fallback_degraded` + `system:phase5-init` SSOT additions), and standing regression tests `tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/inv-gpu-probe-drives-provider-001.test.ts` + `inv-gpu-probe-leaf-import-001.test.ts`. - **PR-V3-T1a Phase 2 — Phase 5 streaming chunked encoder hardening + coverage drift gate + 3 INV standing regression landings** / **PR-V3-T1a Phase 2 — Phase 5 streaming chunked encoder hardening + coverage drift gate + 3 INV standing regression landings**:
  - **EN**: Lands the Phase 2 implementation contract of PR-V3-T1a (Plan v3 §3.1 V2.1 + design §6). Phase 5 (Embedding) streaming chunked encoder is delta-restructured with C1-C4 hardening (memory ceiling sentinel + RSS budget enforcement + per-chunk dispose ordering + child IPC chunk-window verification), `embedding_quality.coverage <5%` drift gate is introduced via the new `embedding-coverage-drift.service.ts` formula + `embedding-quality.service.ts` + `embedding.quality` MCP tool surface, `EmbeddingSkipReason` is extended 16 → 18 values with 2 new SSOT entries, and `AUDIT_ACTION_ENUM` gains `parent_rss_ceiling_scaled` for 4-layer SSOT BOTH ruling sync (Prisma ↔ TS ↔ Zod ↔ OpenAPI/MCP). Three new INV-\* CI-failing executable standing regression tests land under `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/`: `INV-PHASE5-RSS-BUDGET-001` (16 cases under `large-page/`), `INV-COVERAGE-DRIFT-METHODOLOGY-001` (13 cases under `large-page/`), `INV-WORKER-MEMORY-CEILING-007` (9 cases under `worker-lifecycle/`), plus `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` extension (5 cases under `schema-enum-sync/`). Production runtime semantics on the success path are unchanged externally; the chunked encoder now uses an env-overridable `PER_CHUNK_RSS_BUDGET_MB` ENV range guard (256-8192 MB). The `embedding.quality` MCP tool surface is exposed as a new tool with PII-safe response shape. Standing regression `pnpm test:regression:standing` confirms 44 test files / 263 PASS / 4 skipped at HEAD with the change applied.
  - **JP**: PR-V3-T1a の Phase 2 実装契約 (Plan v3 §3.1 V2.1 + design §6) を landing。Phase 5 (Embedding) streaming chunked encoder を delta-restructure し C1-C4 hardening (memory ceiling sentinel + RSS budget enforcement + per-chunk dispose ordering + child IPC chunk-window verification) を追加、新規 `embedding-coverage-drift.service.ts` formula + `embedding-quality.service.ts` + `embedding.quality` MCP tool surface で `embedding_quality.coverage <5%` drift gate を導入、`EmbeddingSkipReason` を 16 → 18 values に拡張 (2 新規 SSOT entries)、`AUDIT_ACTION_ENUM` に `parent_rss_ceiling_scaled` を追加し 4-layer SSOT BOTH ruling sync (Prisma ↔ TS ↔ Zod ↔ OpenAPI/MCP) を成立。3 件の新規 INV-\* CI-failing executable standing regression test が `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/` 配下に landing: `INV-PHASE5-RSS-BUDGET-001` (16 cases、`large-page/`)、`INV-COVERAGE-DRIFT-METHODOLOGY-001` (13 cases、`large-page/`)、`INV-WORKER-MEMORY-CEILING-007` (9 cases、`worker-lifecycle/`)、加えて `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` 拡張 (5 cases、`schema-enum-sync/`)。Success path の production runtime 外部 semantic は不変、chunked encoder は env-overridable `PER_CHUNK_RSS_BUDGET_MB` ENV range guard (256-8192 MB) を使用。`embedding.quality` MCP tool surface を新規 tool として PII-safe response shape で公開。Standing regression `pnpm test:regression:standing` は本 change 適用後の HEAD で 44 test files / 263 PASS / 4 skipped を確認。
  - **AGPL §5(a) Modification Notice / AGPL §5(a) 改変通知**:

All new files carry SPDX header (`// SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2025-2026 Reftrix Contributors` + `// SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-only`) per `scripts/sync-oss.sh:218` SPDX presence enforcement. ### Changed / 変更

- **WorkerSupervisor 3-module mechanical split (PR-V3-CO-26-SPLIT)** / **WorkerSupervisor 3-module mechanical split (PR-V3-CO-26-SPLIT)**:
  - **EN**: Mechanically split `apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor.service.ts` (1537 LoC, exceeding ESLint `max-lines=1500` by 37 LoC) into 3 modules — Module A `worker-supervisor.service.ts` (765 LoC, facade / public types / singleton / DI factory / re-export shim), Module B `worker-supervisor-lifecycle.service.ts` (NEW +794 LoC, spawn / IPC dispatch / exit handling / initiated restart / `IPC_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_MS` constants), Module C `worker-supervisor-lock-orchestrator.service.ts` (NEW +260 LoC, Redis active-worker lock orchestration). Zero behaviour change; **zero file-level callsite churn** at the 30 production+test file-level metric (re-export shim preserves all import paths). Three new INV fixtures landed under `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/` — `INV-WORKER-MODULE-IMPORT-CYCLE-001` (madge-style cycle gate), `INV-WORKER-LIFECYCLE-RESPONSIBILITY-001` (Module B → Module C direct-import forbidden, AST gate), `INV-WORKER-LOCK-RESPONSIBILITY-001` (Module C → Module B direct-import forbidden, AST gate). Preserves ADR-0011 INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 family (Redis dual-run lock + UUID nonce + 60s TTL + 30s heartbeat + Lua atomic release/extend), INV-NEXT-JOB-RACE-001 (Pre-Return Pause + IPC `shutdown` + SIGTERM/SIGKILL escalation timing), INV-WORKER-RESPAWN-LOCK-NONCE-007 (lockNonce=bootToken per ADR-0011 Amendment 4 §A), INV-WORKER-LOCK-003-MOTION-SIGABRT (SIGABRT respawn suppress threshold 3 + 60s extension). Removes the existing `eslint-disable max-lines` header (Module A is now 765 LoC, well below the 1500 cap) and releases ADR-0011 §A5.3 2-layer enforcement gate (`max-lines` ESLint + `wc -l` literal count guard both PASS). SEC M-01 added a hardening script `scripts/check-vi-mock-partial-shape.sh` as a pre-merge contract for `vi.mock` partial-shape regression detection. **actual feat-only LoC delta = +282** (TPA + LCC + TDA 3-way verified empirically via `git diff --shortstat`); routed through ADR-0026 4-band ladder gate at Phase 2 Step 8 IO Impl Decision via **Path b Red-band sign-off ONE-TIME-PR-V3-CO-26-SPLIT** (red_cause_classification = `refactor-consolidation`, red_band_overage = +82 LoC over Red cap 200 = 41% beyond, scope-equivalent governance-only PR per ADR-0026 §Decision 2, orthogonal to `git-workflow.md` 800-line PR size hard cap).

### Fixed / 修正

- **Worker lifecycle: pre-return pause + dispose helper restored (Plan v2 §1 base, PR-Bα-1)** / **Worker ライフサイクル: pre-return pause + dispose helper 復活 (Plan v2 §1 base, PR-Bα-1)**:
  - **EN**: Lands the base implementation contract of Plan v2 §1 (S1.1 + S1.2) and Cond 7 unification, the first PR of the 6-PR PR-Bα DAG (PR-Bα-1..6). Restores BullMQ pre-return pause to the success path with a type-level guarantee: `applyPostJobLifecycleGate(worker, enabled, prefix): Promise<never>` always exits via `process.exit(0)` after `worker.pause(true)`, while the failure path retains the existing memory-only `applyPostJobMemoryGate`. Replaces the legacy hardcoded `IPC_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_MS = 2000` with an env-overridable IIFE (default 30,000ms / range 1,000-120,000ms via `WORKER_IPC_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_MS`); both callsites (`shutdownChild` / `initiateRestart`) reference the same constant for DRY structure. Unifies the `EmbeddingBackfillQueue` `attempts:1` guard at the contract level (Cond 7 closure). Lands 6 INV-\* CI-failing executable tests (`INV-NEXT-JOB-RACE-001` success-pause / failure-no-pause / grace-bound, `INV-WORKER-NO-RESUME-001`, `INV-RETRY-AMPLIFICATION-001`, `INV-DISPOSE-NO-SILENT-CATCH-001`) under `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/`. Resolves the BullMQ silent stall regression that was carried over from prior cycles. Production runtime semantics on the success path are unchanged externally; restart timing is now bounded by a configurable grace window. No new dependency, SBOM unchanged.
  - **JP**: Plan v2 §1 (S1.1 + S1.2) と Cond 7 統一の base implementation contract を landing する 6-PR PR-Bα DAG (PR-Bα-1..6) の起点 PR。BullMQ pre-return pause を success path に **type-level 保証付き**で復活: `applyPostJobLifecycleGate(worker, enabled, prefix): Promise<never>` が `worker.pause(true)` 後に必ず `process.exit(0)` で終了する一方、failure path は既存の memory-only `applyPostJobMemoryGate` を維持。legacy hardcoded `IPC_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_MS = 2000` を env-overridable IIFE (default 30,000ms / range 1,000-120,000ms、`WORKER_IPC_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_MS`) に置換、2 callsite (`shutdownChild` / `initiateRestart`) は同一 constant を参照し DRY を維持。`EmbeddingBackfillQueue` `attempts:1` guard を contract level で統一 (Cond 7 closure)。`apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/` に 6 件の INV-\* CI-failing executable test (`INV-NEXT-JOB-RACE-001` success-pause / failure-no-pause / grace-bound, `INV-WORKER-NO-RESUME-001`, `INV-RETRY-AMPLIFICATION-001`, `INV-DISPOSE-NO-SILENT-CATCH-001`) を新設。前 cycle から carryover していた BullMQ silent stall regression を解消。Success path の production runtime 外部 semantic は不変、restart timing が configurable grace window で bounded に。新規依存なし、SBOM 不変。
  - **AGPL §5(a) Modification Notice / AGPL §5(a) 改変通知**:
    - **EN**: This release modifies worker lifecycle internals under `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/` (`page-analyze-worker.ts`, `embedding-backfill-worker.ts`, `shared/post-job-lifecycle.ts`), `apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor.service.ts`, and `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts`. Modification notice per AGPL-3.0-only §5(a). Modified by Reftrix Contributors on 2026-05-03. See Plan v2 §1 base contract for design rationale.

F1 (`apps/mcp-server/vitest.base.config.mts` Vitest `maxWorkers: 3 → 2`, SSOT propagation to 3 derived configs) + F2 (top-level `fileParallelism: false` in same file, suppresses intra-worker async I/O race) + F3 (`actions/cache@v4` for Playwright browser at 3 callsites in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, lines 154/224/382, cuts cold-install ~60s → cache-hit ~5s) + F4 (`LocalStorageProvider` mock-replace in `apps/mcp-server/tests/integration/phase1-integration.test.ts` via `vi.mock`, removes storage I/O timing from test path; new compensation unit test `tests/services/storage/memory-storage-provider.test.ts` lands in same commit per ADR-0020 Decision 4 + R-11 mandate; **note**: Plan/ADR previously referred to this provider as `MemoryStorageProvider` for historical reasons — code and CHANGELOG now uniformly use `LocalStorageProvider` per CLAUDE.md SoT Hierarchy T1 wins) + CO-PR21-REVIEW-01 (CSS-animation parser ReDoS threshold replaced with input-size-proportional formula `expectedMaxMs = input.length / throughputCharsPerMs + 200ms` with env var `REDOS_TEST_THROUGHPUT_CHARS_PER_MS` override; local default 100 chars/ms, CI 80 chars/ms via `.github/workflows/ci.yml` env injection per TPA-04⚯TDA-02 unified). CWE-770 mitigation completeness preserved as **bounded-growth detection** (PR #21 fixed-1000ms hotfix superseded).
  - **JP**: ADR-0020 に従う 5 件の CI flaky 構造的修復。F1 (`apps/mcp-server/vitest.base.config.mts` の Vitest `maxWorkers: 3 → 2`、3 derived config に SSOT 伝播) + F2 (同一 file 内 top-level `fileParallelism: false`、worker 内 async I/O 競合抑制) + F3 (`.github/workflows/ci.yml` の 3 callsite (154/224/382 行) に `actions/cache@v4` for Playwright browser、cold install ~60秒 → cache hit ~5秒) + F4 (`apps/mcp-server/tests/integration/phase1-integration.test.ts` で `LocalStorageProvider` を `vi.mock` で置換、storage I/O timing を test path から構造的撤去、ADR-0020 Decision 4 + R-11 mandate に従い同一 commit で新規補填 unit test `tests/services/storage/memory-storage-provider.test.ts` も landing。**注**: Plan/ADR は historical reasons から `MemoryStorageProvider` と表記していたが、code と CHANGELOG はいずれも CLAUDE.md SoT Hierarchy (T1 wins) に従い `LocalStorageProvider` を使用) + CO-PR21-REVIEW-01 (CSS-animation parser ReDoS test threshold を input-size 比例式 `expectedMaxMs = input.length / throughputCharsPerMs + 200ms` に置換、env var `REDOS_TEST_THROUGHPUT_CHARS_PER_MS` で override 可能。Local default 100 chars/ms、CI は `.github/workflows/ci.yml` env injection で 80 chars/ms、TPA-04⚯TDA-02 unified per)。CWE-770 mitigation completeness は **bounded-growth detection** で維持 (PR #21 固定 1000 ms hotfix を supersede)。

Applied Option A'' canonical pattern (Pattern (a): single `vi.mock` factory + per-test `mockReset` state). Converted `vi.doMock` × 5 + `vi.doUnmock` × 4 → 1 `vi.mock` + 1 `vi.hoisted`; 9 dynamic imports → static top-level imports. Verified isolation pass^10 + directory pass^10 = **20/20 PASS** (zero flake), 14 tests preserved, production code untouched. CO-DINOV2-MOCK-ISOLATION-01 closure re-phrased as **"three-stage composition: Path B' + Option A'' + DRIFT-U20-01 scope expansion"**. Detailed in ADR-0020 Amendment 4 §5.5 Scope Expansion.

## [0.5.0] — 2026-04-29 / [0.5.0] — 2026-04-29

### Security / セキュリティ

- **PR-0 SEC hotfix: protobufjs Critical + vite + MCP_API_KEYS 鍵長検証** / **PR-0 SEC hotfix: protobufjs Critical + vite + MCP_API_KEYS key-length check**:
  - **SEC Critical (GHSA-xq3m-2v4x-88gg)**: `@huggingface/transformers > onnxruntime-web > protobufjs < 7.5.5` の transient Critical 脆弱性を `pnpm.overrides` の `protobufjs: >=7.5.5` で強制解消。protobufjs@8.0.1 に pin、`pnpm audit --audit-level=critical` 0 件達成 / Resolved transient Critical `protobufjs < 7.5.5` via `pnpm.overrides` (pinned to protobufjs@8.0.1); `pnpm audit --audit-level=critical` reports 0.
  - **SEC High (GHSA-v2wj-q39q-566r, defense-in-depth)**: `pnpm.overrides` の `vite: ">=7.3.2 <9"` で vite@8.0.8 に pin。dev-only で exploitable ではないが defense-in-depth として適用。main High 3 件中 2 件解消 / Applied `vite: ">=7.3.2 <9"` as defense-in-depth (not exploitable due to dev-only, no `--host`); resolved 2/3 High findings on main.
  - **SEC CWE-521 (M-4a)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/auth.ts` の `MIN_ENV_KEY_LENGTH = 40` filter で `MCP_API_KEYS` env 由来のキー長不足を検出し拒否。`reftrix_` (8 chars) + 32 chars = 40 文字最低要件、190-bit entropy (base62) で 80-bit 安全閾値を大幅超過。`TEST_API_KEY_MAPPINGS` (開発用) は対象外 / Added `MIN_ENV_KEY_LENGTH = 40` filter for `MCP_API_KEYS` env-derived keys; 190-bit entropy far exceeds the 80-bit baseline. `TEST_API_KEY_MAPPINGS` (dev keys) are excluded.
- **PR-0.5 LP hotfix: Next.js 16.1.7 → 16.2.3 (GHSA-q4gf-8mx6-v5v3)** / **PR-0.5 LP hotfix: Next.js 16.1.7 → 16.2.3**:
  - **SEC High (GHSA-q4gf-8mx6-v5v3)**: `example/reftrix4` の Next.js Denial of Service with Server Components を解消。vulnerable range `>=16.0.0-beta.0 <16.2.3` → patched `>=16.2.3`。`pnpm-workspace.yaml` で `test/test2` を workspace から除外し audit 対象を縮約。`pnpm audit --audit-level=high` 残存 High **1 → 0** 達成 / Resolved Next.js DoS with Server Components in `example/reftrix4`. Excluded `test/test2` from workspace to reduce audit scope. `pnpm audit --audit-level=high` remaining High reduced from 1 to 0.
- **SEC pre-release hotfix: CodeQL CWE-829 SRI integrity + Dependabot hono / @hono/node-server / DOMPurify pnpm.overrides expansion** / **SEC プレリリース hotfix: CodeQL CWE-829 SRI integrity + Dependabot hono / @hono/node-server / DOMPurify pnpm.overrides 拡張**:
  - **JP**: OSS リポジトリ (TKMD/ReftrixMCP) の Security 監査で報告された **2 CodeQL alerts (CWE-829 `js/functionality-from-untrusted-source`)** + **8 Dependabot moderate alerts (hono ×7 + @hono/node-server ×1)** を pre-release で解消。CodeQL は test fixture HTML (`apps/mcp-server/tests/fixtures/js-animations/{gsap-test,anime-js-test}.html`) の cdnjs CDN script 参照に **`integrity="sha384-..."` + `crossorigin="anonymous"` SRI 属性**を付与し、CDN 改竄時の untrusted source 実行を防止。SHA-384 hash は CDN 配信ファイルから `openssl dgst -sha384 -binary | openssl base64 -A` で算出し配信ファイルと bit-exact 照合。Dependabot は `pnpm.overrides` を拡張: `hono: ">=4.12.7"` → `">=4.12.16"` (closes GHSA-26pp-8wgv-hjvm cookie name validation + GHSA-r5rp-j6wh-rvv4 NBSP prefix bypass + GHSA-458j-xx4x-4375 toSSG path traversal + GHSA-xpcf-pg52-r92g serveStatic middleware bypass + GHSA-wmmm-f939-6g9c JSX HTML injection + GHSA-xf4j-xp2r-rqqx ipRestriction IPv4-mapped + 関連 1)、`@hono/node-server: ">=1.19.10"` → `">=1.19.14"` (closes GHSA-92pp-h63x-v22m middleware bypass)、新規 `dompurify: ">=3.4.1"` (defensive override、現状の主依存は 3.4.0 で advisory 対象外だが OSS Dependabot が transitive nested 旧版を検知する可能性に対する深層防御)。Lockfile 解決結果: hono@4.12.7 → 4.12.16、@hono/node-server@1.19.10 → 2.0.1 (latest)、dompurify@3.4.0 → 3.4.1。`pnpm install --lockfile-only` で binary download なしで lockfile 更新。
  - **EN**: Resolves **2 CodeQL alerts (CWE-829 `js/functionality-from-untrusted-source`)** + **8 Dependabot moderate alerts (hono ×7 + @hono/node-server ×1)** reported on the OSS repo (TKMD/ReftrixMCP) at pre-release. CodeQL: added `integrity="sha384-..."` + `crossorigin="anonymous"` SRI attributes to cdnjs CDN script references in test fixture HTML files (`apps/mcp-server/tests/fixtures/js-animations/{gsap-test,anime-js-test}.html`), preventing untrusted-source execution if the CDN is tampered with. SHA-384 hashes were computed from CDN-served files via `openssl dgst -sha384 -binary | openssl base64 -A` for bit-exact match. Dependabot: expanded `pnpm.overrides` — `hono: ">=4.12.7"` → `">=4.12.16"` (closes GHSA-26pp-8wgv-hjvm cookie-name validation + GHSA-r5rp-j6wh-rvv4 NBSP prefix bypass + GHSA-458j-xx4x-4375 toSSG path traversal + GHSA-xpcf-pg52-r92g serveStatic middleware bypass + GHSA-wmmm-f939-6g9c JSX HTML injection + GHSA-xf4j-xp2r-rqqx ipRestriction IPv4-mapped + 1 related), `@hono/node-server: ">=1.19.10"` → `">=1.19.14"` (closes GHSA-92pp-h63x-v22m middleware bypass), and new `dompurify: ">=3.4.1"` (defensive override; main pin already at 3.4.0 unaffected, but defends in depth against any transitive nested older copies that OSS Dependabot may flag). Lockfile resolution: hono@4.12.7 → 4.12.16, @hono/node-server@1.19.10 → 2.0.1 (latest), dompurify@3.4.0 → 3.4.1. Lockfile updated via `pnpm install --lockfile-only` (no binary downloads).
