### Fixed / 修正

- **fix(worker): Item 3 / CO-31 closure — `acquireRedisLockBestEffort` fail-open recovery via `tryAcquireLock` retry-with-backoff (v0.4.0 Item 3) [EU GDPR Art.32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **fix(worker): Item 3 / CO-31 closure — `acquireRedisLockBestEffort` fail-open recovery を `tryAcquireLock` retry-with-backoff で復旧 (v0.4.0 Item 3)**:
  - **JP**: PR-E-1 §1.3 で carryover として登録された **CO-31 (L severity, deadline 2026-Q4, pipeline-engineer)** を Item 3 PR で **早期 closure** (deadline よりも前倒し)。`apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor.service.ts:1260 acquireRedisLockBestEffort` の fail-open path に **retry-with-backoff (100/200/400ms、max 3 attempts、total 700ms bounded)** helper (`tryAcquireLockWithRetry` + `runAcquireLockWithRetryOrchestrator`) を追加し、Redis transient failure (network jitter / connection limit、`reason === "redis_unavailable"`) 時の lock-absent degradation window を **14 分超 → ≤700ms に縮小** (NOT elimination per ADR-0011 Amendment 5 §A5.5)。`reason === "already_held"` (legitimate race-lost path) は **no-retry** で即時 `held_by_other` を返却 — CO-31 retry trigger ではない。Helper は `worker-active-lock.service.ts:236 tryAcquireLock` の **discriminated union API** (PR7d-3 SEC M-1 lineage、`AcquireLockResult` = `{ ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: "already_held" } | { ok: false; reason: "redis_unavailable"; error: string }`) を consume し、legacy boolean wrapper `acquireLock` では区別不能だった transient vs held-by-other を構造的に separate。CWE-770 mitigation completeness は **degradation window 縮小 (NOT elimination)** として contract-true に維持 (retry budget exhaustion 後は依然 fail-open final で `lockAcquired=false`、次の `ensureWorkerRunning` cycle / heartbeat tick / restart cycle で再 acquire 試行)。
  - **EN**: Closes the PR-E-1 §1.3 carryover **CO-31 (L severity, deadline 2026-Q4, pipeline-engineer)** **earlier than deadline** via Item 3 PR. Adds **retry-with-backoff (100/200/400ms, max 3 attempts, 700ms total bounded)** helper (`tryAcquireLockWithRetry` + `runAcquireLockWithRetryOrchestrator`) to the fail-open path of `apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor.service.ts:1260 acquireRedisLockBestEffort`, **reducing the lock-absent degradation window from 14+ minutes to ≤700ms** on Redis transient failures (network jitter / connection limit, `reason === "redis_unavailable"`) — NOT elimination per ADR-0011 Amendment 5 §A5.5. `reason === "already_held"` (legitimate race-lost path) is **no-retry** and returns `held_by_other` immediately — it is not a CO-31 retry trigger. The helper consumes the `worker-active-lock.service.ts:236 tryAcquireLock` **discriminated union API** (PR7d-3 SEC M-1 lineage, `AcquireLockResult` = `{ ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: "already_held" } | { ok: false; reason: "redis_unavailable"; error: string }`), structurally separating transient vs held-by-other states that the legacy boolean wrapper `acquireLock` could not distinguish. CWE-770 mitigation completeness preserved as **degradation window reduction (NOT elimination)** contract: after retry budget exhaustion the existing fail-open final holds (`lockAcquired=false`), with re-acquire attempted on the next `ensureWorkerRunning` cycle / heartbeat tick / restart cycle.

### Changed / 変更

- **fix(cron): Item 2 / CO-30 closure — BackfillReconciliationCron polling cadence 1h → 5min (v0.4.0 Item 2) [EU GDPR Art.5(1)(d)/30/32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **fix(cron): Item 2 / CO-30 closure — BackfillReconciliationCron ポーリング間隔 1h → 5min (v0.4.0 Item 2)**:
  - **JP**: PR-E-1 §1.3 で carryover として登録された **CO-30 (M severity, deadline 2026-Q3, pipeline-engineer)** を Item 2 PR で closure。`apps/mcp-server/src/cron/backfill-reconciliation-cron.ts` の `DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS` を 1h → 5min に短縮し、`embedding-backfill-worker.ts:661 await updateEmbeddingBackfillStatus(webPageId, remainingStatus)` per-job aggregate UPDATE が late-arrive または drop した場合の reconciliation polling 頻度を高めた。**Root cause re-attribution (TPA-01 evidence)**: Plan v0.1 では root cause を `page-analyze-worker.ts safeUpdateBackfillStatus` に attribute していたが codebase 0 hits、aggregate UPDATE owner は `embedding-backfill-worker.ts:661` per-job execution path。4 mechanisms cause UPDATE late/drop: (1) `updateEmbeddingBackfillStatus` catch block DB connection error / (2) BullMQ retry path exit / (3) planned worker restart drop (`maxJobsBeforeRestart=1`) / (4) last-job race during SIGTERM。**SLO claim correction (TPA-02 evidence, path b 採用)**: "12x improvement" / "5min SLO" は arithmetically invalid (`staleThresholdMs=1h` immutable per IO Plan Decision option (b))。実 reconciliation upper bound = `max(staleThresholdMs=1h, intervalMs=5min) = 1h+5min worst-tail`、~12% reduction in worst-tail lag (was `[1h, 2h]`, now `[1h, 1h+5min]`)。True 12x SLO improvement (cadence + staleThresholdMs 両方 + Option B IPC notification path 補強) は **CO-30-FOLLOWUP (M, 2026-Q4, pipeline-engineer)** として dual-registry (PR-E-1 §1.3 + Item 2 §1.3.2) に新規登録。Severity M、deadline 2026-Q3 met。
  - **EN**: Closes **CO-30 (M severity, deadline 2026-Q3, pipeline-engineer)** registered in PR-E-1 §1.3 as a carryover, via Item 2 PR. Reduces `DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS` from 1h to 5min in `apps/mcp-server/src/cron/backfill-reconciliation-cron.ts`, increasing reconciliation polling frequency for cases where the `embedding-backfill-worker.ts:661 await updateEmbeddingBackfillStatus(webPageId, remainingStatus)` per-job aggregate UPDATE late-arrives or drops. **Root cause re-attribution (TPA-01 evidence)**: Plan v0.1 attributed the root cause to `page-analyze-worker.ts safeUpdateBackfillStatus` but that has 0 hits in codebase; the aggregate UPDATE owner is `embedding-backfill-worker.ts:661` per-job execution path. Four mechanisms cause UPDATE late/drop: (1) DB connection error in `updateEmbeddingBackfillStatus` catch block / (2) BullMQ retry path exit / (3) planned worker restart drop (`maxJobsBeforeRestart=1`) / (4) last-job race during SIGTERM. **SLO claim correction (TPA-02 evidence, path b adopted)**: "12x improvement" / "5min SLO" is arithmetically invalid (`staleThresholdMs=1h` is immutable per IO Plan Decision option (b)). Effective reconciliation upper bound = `max(staleThresholdMs=1h, intervalMs=5min) = 1h+5min worst-tail`, yielding ~12% reduction in worst-tail lag (was `[1h, 2h]`, now `[1h, 1h+5min]`). True 12x SLO improvement (combined cadence + staleThresholdMs + Option B IPC notification path PR) is registered as **CO-30-FOLLOWUP (M, 2026-Q4, pipeline-engineer)** in dual-registry (PR-E-1 §1.3 + Item 2 §1.3.2). Severity M, deadline 2026-Q3 met.

### Fixed / 修正

- **fix(worker): WorkerSupervisor lock nonce contract mismatch 恒久対応 (v0.4.0 PR-E-1) [EU GDPR Art.5(1)(d)/30/32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **fix(worker): permanent fix for WorkerSupervisor lock nonce contract mismatch (v0.4.0 PR-E-1)**:
  - **JP**: PR-E-1 (Severity H, current-release-cycle deadline) として WorkerSupervisor 内の lock nonce contract mismatch を恒久対応。`spawnWorker:687` で生成された per-spawn `randomUUID()` が acquire/extend/release path の per-supervisor `bootToken` と不一致のため、planned restart で SIGABRT 発生時に self-chained respawn が `foreign_lock` と誤判定し worker が永久 zombie 化していた問題を Option A (lockNonce を bootToken に統一、1 line modification) で解消。stripe.com page.analyze (jobId `019dcf77-0f77-7719-aae9-3eafe35b7713`, 2026-04-27) で planned restart + SIGABRT 後の embedding-backfill worker permanent zombie 化を再現していた production blocker を closure。NF-6 として `state="crashed"` セット両経路 (`runSelfChainedRespawnAndSchedule` outcome `foreign_lock` AND `handleUnexpectedExit` `crash_max_attempts`) で `clearLockHeartbeatTimer` helper を呼出し CWE-770 heartbeat 永久 refresh を防止。INV-WORKER-RESPAWN-LOCK-NONCE-007 standing test 新設 (8 cases、worker-lifecycle domain) で contract 検証を CI fail-fast で永続化。worker-supervisor unit test U-1..U-5 5 cases 追加。`audit-log.service.ts` SSOT 拡張 (`worker_zombie_recovered` action contract: 365d retention / `operator:<email>` actor / raw workerType target_id / details schema / `[EU GDPR Art.30][JP APPI 第23条]` compliance tags、LCC-04 explicit decision)。WorkerSupervisor LoC は **1500 = max-lines hard cap** に維持 (Phase 2 commit gate `wc -l ≤ 1500` step landing、CO-PRDD9-01 / OBS-7 enforcement layer closure)。standing regression worker-lifecycle 89/89 PASS (was 81/81、+8 INV-007 cases)。Severity H (production availability + GDPR Art. 32(1)(b))、accepted-risk forbidden、本 PR landing で **CO-22 (FIND-IMPL-TPA-D1b-03 M deadline 2026-Q3) + CO-PRDD9-01 / OBS-7 (worker-supervisor.service.ts max-lines enforcement layer) を formal closure**。
  - **EN**: PR-E-1 (Severity H, current-release-cycle deadline) permanently resolves the WorkerSupervisor lock-nonce contract mismatch. The per-spawn `randomUUID()` at `spawnWorker:687` diverged from the per-supervisor `bootToken` in the acquire/extend/release path, causing `foreign_lock` misclassification and permanent worker-zombie state on SIGABRT during planned restart. Resolved via Option A (unify `lockNonce` on `bootToken`, 1-line modification). Closes the production blocker reproduced on stripe.com page.analyze (jobId `019dcf77-0f77-7719-aae9-3eafe35b7713`, 2026-04-27) where the embedding-backfill worker became a permanent zombie after planned restart + SIGABRT. NF-6 invokes a `clearLockHeartbeatTimer` helper at both `state="crashed"` entry paths (`runSelfChainedRespawnAndSchedule` outcome `foreign_lock` AND `handleUnexpectedExit` `crash_max_attempts`) preventing CWE-770 heartbeat permanent refresh. New INV-WORKER-RESPAWN-LOCK-NONCE-007 standing test (8 cases, worker-lifecycle domain) enforces the contract as CI fail-fast. worker-supervisor unit test U-1..U-5 5-case extension. `audit-log.service.ts` SSOT extended (`worker_zombie_recovered` action contract: 365d retention / `operator:<email>` actor / raw workerType target_id / details schema / `[EU GDPR Art.30][JP APPI Art.23]` compliance tags, LCC-04 explicit decision). WorkerSupervisor LoC kept at **1500 = max-lines hard cap** (Phase 2 commit gate `wc -l ≤ 1500` step lands, CO-PRDD9-01 / OBS-7 enforcement layer closure). - **fix(worker): PR-D-9-patch post-merge runtime silent stall regression — IPC schema jobId composite-key acceptance via `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` SSOT z.union (v0.4.0 PR-D-9-patch) [EU GDPR Art.5(1)(d)/32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **fix(worker): PR-D-9-patch post-merge runtime silent stall regression — IPC schema jobId composite-key acceptance via `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` SSOT z.union (v0.4.0 PR-D-9-patch)**:
  - **JP**: PR-D-9 main 着地後 (stripe.com page.analyze, 2026-04-26-27 時間枠) に backfill drain が再び silent stall する事象を運用観測。原因は PR-D-8 で導入された `apps/mcp-server/src/schemas/worker-ipc.schema.ts` の `jobId: z.string().uuid().optional()` strict UUID 制約と、PR-D-9 の `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` 経路で `buildBackfillJobId(webPageId, category)` が生成する `<UUID>__<category>` 複合キー間の shape mismatch。Worker child → parent IPC message が `jobId` Zod parse fail → message reject → backfill drain handshake が成立せず production rows が `embedding_backfill_status='pending'` で固定。本 patch で **Option B (z.union + SSOT regex)** を採用し landing: (1) `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts` に `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` SSOT を export (RFC 4122 strict-hyphen lowercase + `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES` enum SSOT 動的 join, +40 LoC); (2) `worker-ipc.schema.ts` の `jobId` field を `z.union([z.string().uuid(), z.string().regex(BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX)]).optional()` に拡張 (+22/-1 LoC); (3) Standing regression `inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts` cases #20-#23 (post-renumber, INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 + INV-DATA-ACCURACY-001 cross-binding, +416 LoC net)。**WorkerSupervisor LoC 1497 unchanged** (CO-PRDD9-01 contract STRENGTHENED to absolute neutrality)。Quality Gate Pass^3 5/5: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify` (`pnpm test` + `pnpm format:check` を Plan v1.1 §8 enumeration から正式追補、Plan §13.6 corrected enumeration 参照)。Standing regression 167/167 PASS (was 165/167 pre-Wave-5; Wave 5 で 2 pre-existing test bug fix landing: vi.doUnmock cross-contamination + hardcoded `"embeddin..."` truncation literal)。SEC-PATCH-01 M は Path B (ADR accepted-risk per SEC sign-off `019dcabf-becd`) で landing — `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` 完全 anchor regex + `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES` SSOT + 5.89ms reject 経験的証拠 (10MB jobId DoS attack scenario) で per-string upper bound 不要を SEC が公式承認。**互換性破壊なし** (Backward compatible API)。
  - **EN**: After PR-D-9 main merge (stripe.com page.analyze, 2026-04-26-27 time window), backfill drain was operationally observed to re-enter silent stall. Root cause: shape mismatch between PR-D-8's `apps/mcp-server/src/schemas/worker-ipc.schema.ts` `jobId: z.string().uuid().optional()` strict UUID constraint and the `<UUID>__<category>` composite key generated by `buildBackfillJobId(webPageId, category)` in PR-D-9's `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` path. Worker child→parent IPC messages failed Zod parse on `jobId` → message reject → backfill drain handshake never completed → production rows stuck in `embedding_backfill_status='pending'`. This patch lands **Option B (z.union + SSOT regex)**: (1) export `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` SSOT from `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts` (RFC 4122 strict-hyphen lowercase + dynamic `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES` enum SSOT join, +40 LoC); (2) extend `worker-ipc.schema.ts` `jobId` field to `z.union([z.string().uuid(), z.string().regex(BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX)]).optional()` (+22/-1 LoC); (3) standing regression `inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts` cases #20-#23 (post-renumber, INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 + INV-DATA-ACCURACY-001 cross-binding, +416 LoC net). **WorkerSupervisor LoC 1497 unchanged** (CO-PRDD9-01 contract STRENGTHENED to absolute neutrality). Quality Gate Pass^3 5/5: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify` (`pnpm test` + `pnpm format:check` formally added to Plan v1.1 §8 enumeration, see Plan §13.6 corrected enumeration). Standing regression 167/167 PASS (was 165/167 pre-Wave-5; Wave 5 lands 2 pre-existing test bug fixes: vi.doUnmock cross-contamination + hardcoded `"embeddin..."` truncation literal). SEC-PATCH-01 M lands as Path B (ADR accepted-risk per SEC sign-off `019dcabf-becd`) — SEC formally approved that no per-string upper bound is required because `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` is a complete-anchor regex + `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES` SSOT enum + empirical 5.89ms reject evidence (10MB jobId DoS attack scenario). **Backward compatible** (no breaking change).
  - **6 commits / 6 コミット**: `0d5de99d` (Wave 1+2: SSOT regex + standing test) + `c4c0d3ef` (Wave 3: schema z.union) + `e753400c` (Wave 4: prettier collapse) + `a1d5830d` (chore: docs README format) + `8e040d29` (Wave 5 test: case renumber + 2 bug fixes) + `7337d21b` (Wave 5 doc: Registry §13 prettier fix + §13.11 IO Decision delta).
  - **Standing regression coverage / 常設 regression カバレッジ**: INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 (cases #20-#23 post-renumber, IPC schema jobId composite-key acceptance + Worker IPC spoofing rejection) + INV-DATA-ACCURACY-001 cross-binding (GDPR Art.5(1)(d) accuracy invariant — `embedding_backfill_status` transitions preserved under IPC spoofing attack scenarios, "GDPR Art.5(1)(d) accuracy invariant" literal × 17 occurrences). 167/167 PASS pass^3 3 consecutive.

(2) Vision unload precondition (UNB-IMPL-1) — `apps/mcp-server/src/services/vision/vision-unload-handshake.ts` (NEW, 229 LoC) が Ollama `/api/ps` を 3s timeout で照会し Vision residual loaded 検出時に secondary spawn を defer + audit_logs emit、32GB tier RSS budget 健全性 (~10.6GB Vision + ~1.9GB child + ~4GB peak page = ~16.5GB を回避) を担保、probe failure 時は **fail-closed** (GDPR Art.32(1)(b) integrity); (3) responsive backfill handler (Wave 3) — `embedding-backfill.service.ts:1050-1075` + `:1090-1104` に `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM web_pages WHERE id = ?::uuid)` 防御節を追加し GDPR Art.17 `data.delete` 同期削除中の TOCTOU resurrection invariant を保証 (PR2d LCC-M-2 contract 継承); (4) parts.visual bbox resolution (Wave 4) — `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-bbox-playwright.service.ts` に failure classification (iframe/shadow_dom/dom_disposed/unknown) 追加 + `classifyBboxFailure` private helper (CC delta ≤ +2) で `bbox_unresolvable` skipReason emit、`audit_logs.embedding_part_visual_skipped` SSOT 経由 (`apps/mcp-server/src/audit/audit-actions.ts`) で routing。Standing regression: INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 case #15-#19 (`inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts:1928-2042` 統合) + INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-001 case #1-#6 (`inv-embedding-integrity-001-bbox-and-responsive.test.ts` NEW 613 LoC 10 tests) + INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 16-value `EmbeddingSkipReason` exhaustiveness (TS-only `as const` SSOT per ADR-0018 §Decision 1 Supplement) + Integration drain test `tests/integration/page-analyze-backfill-drain.test.ts` (NEW 293 LoC 4 tests, `process.env.CI` gate, fixture URL allowlist 127.0.0.1:38765 + 4 negative SSRF tests CWE-918)。新規環境変数: `ENABLE_BACKFILL_AUTOSPAWN` (default true), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_ENABLED` (default false), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_ENABLED_MAX_RELOADS_PER_PAGE` (default 5), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_TOTAL_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 60000)。WorkerSupervisor LoC 1499 → **1497** (-2 LoC delta、CO-PRDD9-01 contract STRENGTHENED)。**互換性破壊なし** (Backward compatible API)。

- **feat(worker-supervisor): PR-D-8 extend WorkerSupervisor scope to EmbeddingBackfillWorker (per-type Map + 9 T1 must-fix contracts + 4 audit_logs actions) (v0.4.0 PR-D-8) [EU GDPR Art.30/32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][US CCPA §1798.150][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **feat(worker-supervisor): PR-D-8 extend WorkerSupervisor scope to EmbeddingBackfillWorker (v0.4.0 PR-D-8)**:
  - **JP**: PR-D-7 empirical verification (linear.app, 2026-04-24) と stripe.com 再現観測 (2026-04-25) で `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` が `WorkerSupervisor` scope 外で稼働している設計 gap を確認。`workerArgs: ["--page"]` ハードコード + `WorkerType="page"` 単一 union により RSS 閾値超過時 self-exit 後の OS-level restart 経路欠落 → production backfill drain が手動介入依存 (GDPR Art.32(1)(b) "ongoing resilience of processing systems" 劣化、APPI 第23条 安全管理措置 scope)。本 PR で `WorkerSupervisor` を **per-type Map 設計 (Option C)** に拡張し、9 T1 Canonical must-fix contracts (Registry v3 §20.1) を landing: (1) per-type child Map (`children: Map<WorkerType, WorkerChildState>`) + (2) IPC binding table dispatch + (3) 5-rule boot token contract (per-type isolation: `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN_PAGE` / `_BACKFILL`) + (4) self-chained respawn 3-layer protocol (Layer 1 release-retry budget=3 / Layer 2 `probeExistingLock` 3000ms timeout / Layer 3 60s TTL fallback) + (5) 4 audit_logs actions wiring (`worker_supervisor_restart` / `worker_type_spoofing_detected` / `worker_lock_ttl_fallback` / `worker_ipc_spoofing_detected` 全 365日保持 + actor=`system:worker-supervisor` + `targetId` `truncateId` PII-truncate + `details` raw payload 不含 = CWE-209 + SEC-V11-01 PII minimisation 保全) + (6) ESLint complexity gate (10 threshold) + (7) WorkerType SSOT (`apps/mcp-server/src/types/worker-type.ts`, 82 LoC) + (8) IPC Zod schema SSOT (`apps/mcp-server/src/schemas/worker-ipc.schema.ts`, 117 LoC, `parseWorkerIpcStrict`) + (9) `WorkerChildState` struct + helper module 抽出 (`worker-supervisor-helpers.ts`, 313 LoC, `verifyWorkerIpcMessage` + `emitSupervisorAuditLog` + binding-table dispatch)。Per-type Redis lock key 拡張: `reftrix:worker:active:page` (PageAnalyzeWorker) + `reftrix:worker:active:embedding-backfill` (EmbeddingBackfillWorker) — 両 key とも UUID nonce + 60s TTL + 30s heartbeat + Lua atomic release/extend を ADR-0011 §A から継承。Staggered scheduling (primary `page` 起動 → heartbeat 確立 → secondary `embedding-backfill` 起動) で 32GB tier RSS budget 健全性を担保 (TPA-V12-01 / TPA-V11-01 Ollama Vision unload coordination は PR-D-9 carryover)。MF-V12-01 unified fix で SEC-V12-01 H 🚫 (worker IPC spoofing audit emit) + LCC-V12-01 M 🚫 (Art.30 audit trail completeness) を同時 RESOLVED — `worker-supervisor-helpers.ts:308-317` で `emitSupervisorAuditLog('worker_ipc_spoofing_detected', expectedWorkerType, { pid, reason }, 'denied')` 発行 + `worker-supervisor.service.ts:839-852` で `dispatchVerifiedIpc` → `escalateSpoofing` (SIGTERM + 60s respawn suppress) wiring。MF-V12-02 で standing test 2 cases #13/#14 を新設 (unknown-workerType + schema-invalid IPC scenarios)。Standing regression INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 (14 cases / `inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts`) + INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 (1 case / `inv-schema-enum-004-worker-type.test.ts`) + INV-DATA-DELETE-002 (audit_logs cross-ref) 全 PASS pass^3 3 consecutive。**8/8 H findings RESOLVED**, **1/1 M finding RESOLVED**, **accepted-risk 0** (Severity → Landing rule compliant)。LoC re-baseline: Plan v1.0 estimate 350-450 → 1849 measured (1499 supervisor + 350 helpers, +302%) — 9 T1 contracts 同 PR 内 absorption により justified、PR-D-9 class split deferred per CO-20 (deadline 2026-06-30) で contract drift 回避。Legacy `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN` deprecated (1-cycle backward compat preserved via fallback、`[DEPRECATION]` warn hard landing は CO-21 deadline 2026-05-31)。
  - **EN**: PR-D-7 empirical verification (linear.app, 2026-04-24) and stripe.com re-reproduction (2026-04-25) confirmed a design gap in which `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` operated **outside `WorkerSupervisor` scope**. Hardcoded `workerArgs: ["--page"]` + single-value `WorkerType="page"` union meant no OS-level restart path post-self-exit, so production backfill drain depended on manual intervention (GDPR Art.32(1)(b) "ongoing resilience of processing systems" degradation; in scope of APPI Article 23 安全管理措置 / safeguarding measures). This PR extends `WorkerSupervisor` to a **per-type Map design (Option C)** and lands 9 T1 Canonical must-fix contracts (Registry v3 §20.1): (1) per-type child Map (`children: Map<WorkerType, WorkerChildState>`) + (2) IPC binding-table dispatch + (3) 5-rule boot token contract (per-type isolation: `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN_PAGE` / `_BACKFILL`) + (4) self-chained respawn 3-layer protocol (Layer 1 release-retry budget=3 / Layer 2 `probeExistingLock` 3000ms / Layer 3 60s TTL fallback) + (5) 4 `audit_logs` action wiring (`worker_supervisor_restart` / `worker_type_spoofing_detected` / `worker_lock_ttl_fallback` / `worker_ipc_spoofing_detected`; 365-day retention + `actor="system:worker-supervisor"` + `targetId` truncated via `truncateId` + `details` excludes raw payload — CWE-209 + SEC-V11-01 PII minimisation preserved) + (6) ESLint complexity gate (threshold 10) + (7) `WorkerType` SSOT (`apps/mcp-server/src/types/worker-type.ts`, 82 LoC) + (8) IPC Zod schema SSOT (`apps/mcp-server/src/schemas/worker-ipc.schema.ts`, 117 LoC, `parseWorkerIpcStrict`) + (9) `WorkerChildState` struct + helper module extraction (`worker-supervisor-helpers.ts`, 313 LoC, `verifyWorkerIpcMessage` + `emitSupervisorAuditLog` + binding-table dispatch). Per-type Redis lock keys: `reftrix:worker:active:page` (PageAnalyzeWorker) + `reftrix:worker:active:embedding-backfill` (EmbeddingBackfillWorker) — both inherit UUID nonce + 60s TTL + 30s heartbeat + Lua atomic release/extend from ADR-0011 §A. Staggered scheduling (primary `page` start → heartbeat established → secondary `embedding-backfill` start) preserves 32GB tier RSS-budget health (TPA-V12-01 / TPA-V11-01 Ollama Vision unload coordination is carried over to PR-D-9). The MF-V12-01 unified fix simultaneously resolves SEC-V12-01 H 🚫 (worker IPC spoofing audit emit) and LCC-V12-01 M 🚫 (Art.30 audit-trail completeness) — `worker-supervisor-helpers.ts:308-317` emits `emitSupervisorAuditLog('worker_ipc_spoofing_detected', expectedWorkerType, { pid, reason }, 'denied')` and `worker-supervisor.service.ts:839-852` wires `dispatchVerifiedIpc` → `escalateSpoofing` (SIGTERM + 60s respawn suppress). MF-V12-02 lands 2 new standing test cases #13/#14 (unknown-workerType + schema-invalid IPC scenarios). Standing regression: INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 (14 cases / `inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts`) + INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 (1 case / `inv-schema-enum-004-worker-type.test.ts`) + INV-DATA-DELETE-002 (audit_logs cross-ref) — all PASS pass^3 3 consecutive. **8/8 H findings RESOLVED**, **1/1 M finding RESOLVED**, **accepted-risk count 0** (Severity → Landing rule compliant). LoC re-baseline: Plan v1.0 estimate 350-450 → 1849 measured (1499 supervisor + 350 helpers, +302%) — justified by 9 T1 contracts absorbed in-PR; PR-D-9 class split deferred per CO-20 (deadline 2026-06-30) to avoid contract drift. Legacy `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN` is deprecated (1-cycle backward compat preserved via fallback; `[DEPRECATION]` warn hard landing tracked in CO-21, deadline 2026-05-31).
  - **Related contracts / 関連契約**: ADR-0011 Amendment 1 (PR-D-8) — per-type WorkerSupervisor scope expansion (§A.1 per-type Redis lock key + §A.2 GDPR Art.32(1)(b) processing-integrity expansion + §A.3 APPI 第23条 per-type expansion + §B.1 per-type BOOT_TOKEN secrets isolation + §B.2 legacy deprecation + §C staggered scheduling rationale + §D PR-D-9 class split footnote); INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 (expansion to embedding-backfill, +14 cases including v12 MF-V12-02 #13/#14); INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 (NEW WorkerType SSOT exhaustive switch); INV-DATA-DELETE-002 (audit_logs 4 new action cross-ref, GDPR Art.30 100%); 4-auditor evolution: TPA 88→42→92, SEC 90→42→78→**96** (+18), TDA 89→87→84 (CONDITIONAL absorbed via Phase 3 D-01), LCC 82→78→80→**96** (+16).

Updates ADR-0018 §3.9 wording from "Object-form canonical" to **"5-shape whitelist per §3.5.2 Step 5 (PR-D-7)"** (making explicit the 5-shape T1 production reality — (a) `string[]` / (b) `{name, error}[]` / (c) `{id, error}[]` / (d) `{sectionId, error}[]` / (e) `{patternId, error}[]` — with raw `error.message` push prohibited). - **feat(queue): PR-D-6 RC-A BullMQ jobId collision 根治 + atomic SETNX Lua + 6-variant EnqueueResult + both-queue QueueEvents listener wiring + 2 new audit actions (ADR-0018 Amendment 6) (v0.4.0 PR-D-6) [EU GDPR Art.30/5(1)(c)/33][JP APPI 第23条][US CCPA §1798.150]** / **feat(queue): PR-D-6 RC-A BullMQ jobId collision eradication + atomic SETNX Lua + 6-variant EnqueueResult + both-queue QueueEvents listener wiring + 2 new audit actions (ADR-0018 Amendment 6) (v0.4.0 PR-D-6)**:
  - **JP**: stripe.com 実証 (`019da991`, 2026-04-20) で 7/7 category に 16-21h Redis/DB drift が観測された **RC-A (BullMQ jobId 衝突による silent no-op)** を根治。ADR-0018 §Decision 4 の 3 state-branching 契約 (completed/failed → re-enqueue / active/waiting/delayed → trust existing / unknown → ADR-0017 limbo) を runtime 実装 + atomic SETNX Lua pattern (WorkerActiveLockService precedent 流用) で TOCTOU race を根絶。SSOT helper `addEmbeddingBackfillJobWithGuard` (`apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts` L810-847) + `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` (`apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts` L729-749) を新設し、generic `enqueueWithCollisionGuard` (`enqueue-with-collision-guard.ts`, 5 sub-handler dispatch + 6-variant `EnqueueResult` discriminated union) に委譲。`EnqueueResult` 6-variant = `enqueued_new` / `reused_active` / `enqueued_retry` / `limbo_forced` / `enqueued_fail_open` / `race_lost_atomic` (§Decision 4 case (c) `state === "unknown"` → `limbo_forced` emit contract alignment, FIND-TPA-IMPL-01 closure)。両 queue の `QueueEvents "duplicated"` listener を `start-workers.ts` で boot 時に wire + `shutdownWorkers` lifecycle で detach (`registerEmbeddingBackfillDuplicatedListener` embedding-backfill-queue.ts L872 + `registerPageAnalyzeDuplicatedListener` page-analyze-queue.ts L767、FIND-TPA-IMPL-02 RESOLVED + FIND-SEC-04 CLOSED-COCLOSE)。2 new audit actions を `audit_logs.action` 列挙集に append: (a) `embedding_backfill_collision_resolved` (actor `system:embedding-backfill-queue`, primary emit via `emitCollisionAudit`, Zod `.strict()` `CollisionAuditPayloadSchema` 5-field contract: `webPageId` 11-char / `originalJobId` regex-pinned / `retryJobId` regex-pinned / `originalState` enum / `timestamp` ISO-8601) (b) `page_analyze_collision_resolved` (actor `system:page-analyze-queue`, observability-only emit via `emitPageAnalyzeCollisionAudit`, Registry v3 §3 FIND-TPA-02 binding per observability-only scope)。両 action とも既存 365 日 retention + `truncateId` SSOT PII minimisation (utils/truncate-id.ts L17) + `system:<role>` actor naming convention を継承 (GDPR Art.30 audit trail continuity、domain-specific actor naming は T1 SoT Hierarchy resolution、FIND-LCC-IMPL-01 closure)。Retry jobId suffix は Node.js 20.19+ stdlib `crypto.randomUUID({version:7})` 採用 — **新規 npm 依存 0 (CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM drift 0、EU CRA 2026-09-11 compliance 保持)**。SLO tier mapping: 両 new action は L0/L1/L2 source として既存 5-tier framework に参加 (L3 CRIT breach notification gate は `embedding_parity_schema_drift` sentinel 専用で不変)。Standing regression +17 tests を 2 new file で landing: `worker-lifecycle/inv-worker-lock-003-jobid-collision.test.ts` (12 tests = Block A AST source-pin 4 + Block B fixture behavior 4 + Block C real Prisma + real Redis integration 4, INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 expansion) + `large-page/inv-page-queue-001-e-jobid-concurrent.test.ts` (5 tests = Block A 1 + Block B 2 + Block C 2, INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001-E expansion)。Dispatcher `handleCollisionEnqueue` cyclomatic complexity 実測 = 7、ESLint `complexity: ["error", 10]` threshold 内 (FIND-TDA-IMPL-01 closure)。`race_lost_atomic` variant の production emit site は 0 件の pre-existing design gap として **PR-D-7 carryover** (FIND-IO-PR-D-6-IMPL-04 Option Z: 型定義削除 or 実 emit 経路追加 + ADR Amendment 7 authoring を PR-D-7 で joint 判断、deadline 2026-05-30)。ADR-0018 Amendment 6 新設 (§Decision 4 case(c) emit contract + §Privacy Considerations Update 2 new audit actions + §Test Coverage + §Implementation Notes + §Notes + LCC cross-sign footer、PR-D-5 Amendment 5 anchor `019db52e` 継承 cross-sign chain)、DATA_RETENTION.md §Embedding Integrity SLO subsection に 2 row append + Changelog v2.14.0 entry 追加 (FIND-LCC-02 M binding)。
  - **EN**: Eradicates **RC-A (BullMQ jobId collision silent no-op)** — empirically confirmed on stripe.com (`019da991`, 2026-04-20) with 16–21h Redis/DB drift across all 7 categories. Runtime-lands ADR-0018 §Decision 4's 3 state-branching contract (completed/failed → re-enqueue / active/waiting/delayed → trust existing / unknown → ADR-0017 limbo) AND adds an atomic SETNX Lua pattern (reusing the WorkerActiveLockService precedent) to eradicate the TOCTOU race surface. Introduces SSOT helpers `addEmbeddingBackfillJobWithGuard` (`apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts` L810-847) + `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` (`apps/mcp-server/src/queues/page-analyze-queue.ts` L729-749) delegating to generic `enqueueWithCollisionGuard` (`enqueue-with-collision-guard.ts`, 5 sub-handler dispatch + 6-variant `EnqueueResult` discriminated union). The `EnqueueResult` 6-variant = `enqueued_new` / `reused_active` / `enqueued_retry` / `limbo_forced` / `enqueued_fail_open` / `race_lost_atomic` (§Decision 4 case (c) `state === "unknown"` → `limbo_forced` emit contract alignment, FIND-TPA-IMPL-01 closure). Both queues' `QueueEvents "duplicated"` listeners are wired in `start-workers.ts` at boot time with lifecycle detach in `shutdownWorkers` (`registerEmbeddingBackfillDuplicatedListener` embedding-backfill-queue.ts L872 + `registerPageAnalyzeDuplicatedListener` page-analyze-queue.ts L767; FIND-TPA-IMPL-02 RESOLVED + FIND-SEC-04 CLOSED-COCLOSE). 2 new audit actions are appended to the `audit_logs.action` enumeration: (a) `embedding_backfill_collision_resolved` (actor `system:embedding-backfill-queue`, primary emit via `emitCollisionAudit`, Zod `.strict()` `CollisionAuditPayloadSchema` 5-field contract: `webPageId` 11-char / `originalJobId` regex-pinned / `retryJobId` regex-pinned / `originalState` enum / `timestamp` ISO-8601) and (b) `page_analyze_collision_resolved` (actor `system:page-analyze-queue`, observability-only emit via `emitPageAnalyzeCollisionAudit`, Registry v3 §3 FIND-TPA-02 binding per observability-only scope). Both inherit the existing 365-day retention + `truncateId` SSOT PII minimisation (utils/truncate-id.ts L17) + `system:<role>` actor naming convention (GDPR Art.30 audit trail continuity; domain-specific actor naming reflects T1 SoT Hierarchy resolution, FIND-LCC-IMPL-01 closure). Retry jobId suffix uses the Node.js 20.19+ stdlib `crypto.randomUUID({version:7})` — **zero new npm dependency (CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM drift zero, EU CRA 2026-09-11 compliance preserved)**. SLO tier mapping: both new actions participate in the existing 5-tier framework as L0/L1/L2 sources (the L3 CRIT breach-notification gate remains exclusively triggered by the `embedding_parity_schema_drift` sentinel). Standing regression +17 tests landed across 2 new files: `worker-lifecycle/inv-worker-lock-003-jobid-collision.test.ts` (12 tests = Block A AST source-pin 4 + Block B fixture behavior 4 + Block C real Prisma + real Redis integration 4, INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 expansion) + `large-page/inv-page-queue-001-e-jobid-concurrent.test.ts` (5 tests = Block A 1 + Block B 2 + Block C 2, INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001-E expansion). Measured cyclomatic complexity of `handleCollisionEnqueue` dispatcher = 7, within the ESLint `complexity: ["error", 10]` threshold (FIND-TDA-IMPL-01 closure). The `race_lost_atomic` variant has zero production emit sites as a pre-existing design gap, **carried over to PR-D-7** (FIND-IO-PR-D-6-IMPL-04 Option Z: joint decision between removing the type-union variant vs. adding an explicit emit branch + ADR Amendment 7 authoring, PR-D-7 deadline 2026-05-30). ### Added / 追加

- **test(embedding): PR-D-3 `savePartEmbeddings` catch block sanitize + INV-SEC-SANITIZE standing regression (ADR-0018 §3.9 landed) (v0.4.0 PR-D-3)** / **test(embedding): PR-D-3 catch-block sanitize + INV-SEC-SANITIZE standing regression (v0.4.0 PR-D-3)**:
  - **New CI-failing regression test / 新規 CI-failing 回帰テスト**: `apps/mcp-server/tests/services/part/part-embedding-db-error-sanitize.test.ts` (INV-SEC-SANITIZE standing regression、7 CI-failing cases)。UC-D3-03 "whitelist positive match" (Prisma P2002 → "A record with this value already exists" 等の test-assertion 粒度 7 種 subset) + Test #7 "server-log preservation" (raw `error.message` + error.code が `logger.warn` で server-side に保持される契約) を enforce。 / New standing regression test with 7 CI-failing cases; UC-D3-03 whitelist positive match + Test #7 server-log preservation enforcement.
  - **Whitelist / SSOT relationship (documentation-only clarification)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/utils/sanitize-error.ts` L17-31 が 17 種 error-code → client-safe message マッピングの **SSOT (Single Source of Truth)** であることを ADR-0018 §3.9 に明記。Test whitelist 7 種は SSOT 17 種の **test-assertion 粒度 subset** であり、SSOT の代替ではない (whitelist 7 ⊂ SSOT 17)。SSOT 拡張は security-engineer 判断、whitelist 拡張は test-qa-engineer 判断。 / Documentation clarifies `sanitize-error.ts` L17-31 (17 entries) is the SSOT; test whitelist (7 entries) is a test-assertion-grained subset. SSOT expansion: security-engineer; whitelist expansion: test-qa-engineer.

- **feat(embedding): PR-D-2 `savePartEmbeddings` atomic 2-step write + `bbox_invalid` skip reason (ADR-0018 §Decision 3 + §Decision 2 tail-append) (v0.4.0 PR-D-2)** / **feat(embedding): PR-D-2 `savePartEmbeddings` atomic 2-step transaction + `bbox_invalid` skip reason (ADR-0018 §Decision 3 + §Decision 2 tail-append) (v0.4.0 PR-D-2)**:
  - (a) **Atomic 2-step transaction / atomic 2 段書込**: `savePartEmbeddings` を単一 `prisma.$transaction` 境界でラップし、(1) `createMany` による非ベクトル column 挿入 → (2) `$executeRawUnsafe` による `vector(768)` column UPDATE の 2 step を transaction-boundary atomic に実行。partial failure 時は rollback により "base row exists but vector is NULL" の半書込状態を絶対に残さない (INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-002-A)。NaN/Infinity pre-filter を transaction 境界前段で実施 (CWE-20 / SEC-02 対応)。/ Wraps `savePartEmbeddings` in a single `prisma.$transaction` boundary, executing (1) `createMany` for non-vector columns and (2) `$executeRawUnsafe` `UPDATE` for `vector(768)` columns as transaction-boundary atomic. Partial failure rollback eliminates the "base row exists but vector is NULL" half-written state (INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-002-A). NaN/Infinity pre-filter applied before the transaction boundary (CWE-20 / SEC-02).
  - (b) **`EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` 14→15 additive tail-append (`bbox_invalid`)**: 既存 14 値の order / value / semantic は完全不変、新規 1 値 `bbox_invalid` を enum 末尾に append (GDPR Art.5(1)(d) accuracy principle に従い "bbox invalid → skipped, retryable via next capture cycle" を明示)。`INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` で Prisma ↔ TS ↔ Zod ↔ OpenAPI/MCP spec の 4-way exhaustive mapping を 15 値対応に更新。GDPR Art.13/14: `bbox_invalid` は operator-facing technical identifier のみ、data subject には返却されない。/ Existing 14-value order / value / semantic fully preserved; new value `bbox_invalid` appended at the enum tail. `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` updated to 15 values for 4-way exhaustive Prisma ↔ TS ↔ Zod ↔ OpenAPI/MCP spec mapping. GDPR Art.13/14: operator-facing only.
  - (c) **New standing regression (Severity H)**: `tests/regression/standing/embedding-integrity/inv-embedding-integrity-002a-part-atomic-write.test.ts` (INV-002-A) + `inv-embedding-integrity-003-status-parity-partial.test.ts` (INV-003 partial — transaction atomicity sub-invariant; `-partial` suffix で完全 landing は PR-D-4 であることを明示) + `inv-embedding-integrity-005-bbox-zero-observability.test.ts` (INV-005 bbox=0 observability). / Three new CI-failing standing regression tests landed in `tests/regression/standing/embedding-integrity/`.
  - (d) **Related contracts / 関連契約**: ADR-0018 §Decision 3 (formal merge of §3.1-§3.9 Amendment); ADR-0018 §3.9 `result.errors[]` sanitize policy (new, with documented risk + FIND-IMPL-SEC-01 deadline 2026-05-04); INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-002-A (new, atomic dual-phase write); INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-003 (partial landing); INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-005 (bbox=0 observability).
  - (e) **CRA SBOM impact / CRA SBOM 影響**: **No dependency added, SBOM unchanged** — 本 PR は `@prisma/client` 既存 API (`$transaction` + `$executeRawUnsafe`) のみ使用、外部パッケージ追加なし。`pnpm sbom` で生成される CycloneDX 1.6 JSON は drift ゼロ。EU CRA (2026-09-11 vulnerability reporting obligation) 対応。/ No new dependency; only existing `@prisma/client` APIs used. CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM drift zero. EU CRA 2026-09-11 compliance preserved.

- **chore(api): `PartEmbeddingSaveResult.savedCount` → `generatedCount` 名称変更 (v0.4.0 PR-D-2)** / **chore(api): rename `PartEmbeddingSaveResult.savedCount` → `generatedCount` (v0.4.0 PR-D-2)**:
  - **Rationale / 理由**: ADR-0018 INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-002 "DB round-trip-derived generatedCount" に整合。`savedCount` は "DB に保存された件数" を示唆するが実際には "transaction commit 成功後 DB round-trip で確認された件数" であり、INV-002 の wording (`generatedCount`) との不一致を解消。/ Aligns with ADR-0018 INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-002 wording (`generatedCount`). The prior name `savedCount` ambiguously suggested "saved to DB" whereas the value is "count confirmed via DB round-trip after transaction commit".
  - **Migration guide / 移行手順**: Consumer コードで `result.savedCount` を使用している箇所を `result.generatedCount` に置換すること。`savedCount` は **deprecated alias として 48 時間併存** (PR-D-2 merge 後)、その後 **2026-04-27 (PR-D-3 cycle) に完全削除**。 / Replace `result.savedCount` with `result.generatedCount` in consumer code. `savedCount` remains as a **deprecated alias for a 48-hour window** post-PR-D-2 merge; full removal at **2026-04-27 (PR-D-3 cycle)**.

### Security / セキュリティ

- **fix(security): CWE-209 Information Exposure 修復 — `savePartEmbeddings` catch block sanitize (v0.4.0 PR-D-3) [JP][US][EU]** / **fix(security): CWE-209 Information Exposure remediation — `savePartEmbeddings` catch-block sanitize (v0.4.0 PR-D-3) [JP][US][EU]**:
  - **Remediation / 修復**: `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-embedding-db.service.ts` の `savePartEmbeddings` catch block で、従来 `result.errors[]` に push していた生 `error.message` を `sanitizeErrorMessage(error)` 経由化。Prisma error code (P2002 unique violation / P2025 record not found 等) の内部テーブル名・カラム名の client response 漏洩を遮断 (CWE-209 Information Exposure Through an Error Message 防御)。/ Replaced raw `error.message` push to `result.errors[]` with `sanitizeErrorMessage(error)` in the `savePartEmbeddings` catch block of `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-embedding-db.service.ts`. Blocks leakage of internal Prisma table/column names from error codes (P2002 unique violation / P2025 record not found, etc.) to client responses (CWE-209 Information Exposure Through an Error Message defense).
  - **Server-side log preservation (Test #7 enforced) / サーバーログ保持 (Test #7 で enforce)**: Server-side `logger.warn` では raw `error.message` + error.code (P2002 等) を継続保持 (debug 性能維持)。本契約は `part-embedding-db-error-sanitize.test.ts` Test #7 で CI-failing regression として enforce。/ Server-side `logger.warn` retains raw `error.message` + error.code (e.g. P2002) for debugging. This contract is enforced as a CI-failing regression in Test #7.
  - **Early landing / 早期 landing**: FIND-IMPL-SEC-01 (M severity, deadline **2026-05-04**, PR-D-2 で発行) を **2026-04-22 (PR-D-3) に landing** — deadline から **12 日早期遵守**。ADR-0018 §3.9 "Landing deadline" の status を Planned → Landed に更新。/ FIND-IMPL-SEC-01 (M severity, deadline **2026-05-04**, issued in PR-D-2) landed in PR-D-3 on **2026-04-22** — **12 days ahead of deadline**. ADR-0018 §3.9 "Landing deadline" updated from Planned → Landed.
  - **Regulatory alignment [JP][US][EU] / 法域アラインメント**:
    - **[EU] GDPR Art.32(1)(b) "integrity"**: state-of-the-art error message sanitization to preserve integrity of processing. / 処理の integrity を保つための state-of-the-art エラーメッセージサニタイズ。
    - **[JP] APPI 第23条 (安全管理措置) / Article 23 (Security control measures)**: 個人データに関する漏えい・滅失・毀損を防止するための組織的・技術的措置。error message sanitize は技術的措置として CWE-209 情報漏洩を防止。 / Technical measure preventing CWE-209 information leakage to protect personal data.
    - **[US] CCPA §1798.150 (breach surface reduction)**: breach notification 対象となる "unauthorized disclosure" の surface 削減。 / Reduces the breach surface subject to `unauthorized disclosure` under §1798.150.
    - **[EU] EU CRA 2024/2847 compliance readiness (2026-09-11 in force)**: state-of-the-art vulnerability handling の一環として CWE-209 latent risk を事前除去。 / Pre-removes CWE-209 latent risk as part of state-of-the-art vulnerability handling.
  - **Standing regression landing / standing regression landing**: `apps/mcp-server/tests/services/part/part-embedding-db-error-sanitize.test.ts` を INV-SEC-SANITIZE standing regression として追加 (CI-failing 7 cases + whitelist positive match UC-D3-03 + server-log preservation Test #7)。CI `regression-standing` job で常時実行。 / Added as INV-SEC-SANITIZE standing regression (CI-failing 7 cases, UC-D3-03 whitelist positive match, Test #7 server-log preservation). Runs continuously in the CI `regression-standing` job.

- **fix(embedding): Prisma transaction isolation + NaN/Infinity pre-filter defense (v0.4.0 PR-D-2)** / **fix(embedding): Prisma transaction isolation + NaN/Infinity pre-filter defense (v0.4.0 PR-D-2)**:
  - **UC-03 (transaction isolation)**: Prisma `$transaction` interactive transaction の rollback 挙動を CI-failing test で verify (`apps/mcp-server/tests/services/part/part-embedding-db-transaction-isolation.test.ts`)。Step 2 `$executeRawUnsafe` 失敗時に Step 1 `createMany` 挿入 row が rollback で撤回され、"half-written state" が DB に残存しないことを保証。 / Interactive `$transaction` rollback verified via CI-failing test (`part-embedding-db-transaction-isolation.test.ts`). Ensures Step 1 `createMany` rows are withdrawn on Step 2 `$executeRawUnsafe` failure.
  - **UC-04 (NaN/Infinity defense)**: transaction 境界前段で `Number.isFinite()` による pre-filter を実装 (`apps/mcp-server/tests/services/part/part-embedding-db-nan-infinity.test.ts`)。`NaN` / `Infinity` を含む embedding による pgvector query 失敗を防止 (CWE-20 Improper Input Validation 対策)。/ `Number.isFinite()` pre-filter implemented at the transaction boundary prologue. Prevents pgvector query failures from `NaN` / `Infinity` embeddings (CWE-20 Improper Input Validation defense).
  - **Documented risk (FIND-IMPL-SEC-01, M severity, deadline 2026-05-04)**: 現行 PR-D-2 実装の `savePartEmbeddings` catch block で生 `error.message` が `result.errors[]` に push されている CWE-209 latent risk を ADR-0018 §3.9 + `に documented risk として記載。PR-D-3 cycle (deadline 2026-05-04) で`sanitizeErrorMessage(error)`経由化 +`part-embedding-db-error-sanitize.test.ts`landing 予定。/ CWE-209 latent risk (raw`error.message`pushed to`result.errors[]`in the catch block) is documented in ADR-0018 §3.9 and` Will be routed through `sanitizeErrorMessage(error)` + CI-failing test added in PR-D-3 (deadline 2026-05-04).

### Compliance / 法務・コンプライアンス

- **chore(compliance): PR-D-2 GDPR / AGPL / CRA compliance verification (v0.4.0)** / **chore(compliance): PR-D-2 GDPR / AGPL / CRA compliance verification (v0.4.0)**:
  - **GDPR Art.5(1)(d) accuracy**: `bbox_invalid` skip reason の semantic は "bounding box が無効なため skip、次回 capture cycle で retry 可能" であり、Option D (recovery bucket: `bbox_invalid` → `skipped_fork_error` retry bucket 経由で次回 cycle で再試行) に分類。"inaccurate permanent loss" ではない旨を ADR-0018 §Decision 2 tail-append rationale + CHANGELOG §(b) に明記。 / `bbox_invalid` semantic is "skipped due to invalid bounding box, retryable in next capture cycle" (Option D: retry bucket), explicitly distinguished from "inaccurate permanent loss" in ADR-0018 and CHANGELOG.
  - **GDPR Art.13/14 transparency**: `bbox_invalid` は operator-facing technical identifier のみ、`audit_logs` metadata / MCP server logger にのみ出力され、`data.export` (Art.20) / `preference.get` で data subject に verbatim 返却されない。 / Operator-facing only; never returned verbatim to data subjects via `data.export` / `preference.get`.
  - **EU CRA (2024/2847) SBOM**: 新規依存ゼロ、CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM drift ゼロ。`pnpm sbom` 生成物不変 (SEC M-3 根拠)。EU CRA 2026-09-11 vulnerability reporting obligation 対応継続。 / Zero new dependencies; CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM drift zero. EU CRA 2026-09-11 compliance preserved.
  - **AGPL-3.0 transparency**: `savedCount` → `generatedCount` rename は internal service type のみで MCP public API schema には影響なし。ただし AGPL-3.0 §5 "modify the Program" 観点で CHANGELOG に rename + migration guide + deadline を明記することで transparency 要件を充足。 / Internal type rename documented in CHANGELOG with migration guide and deprecation deadline, satisfying AGPL-3.0 §5 transparency requirements.

- **feat(embedding): `EmbeddingSkipReason` 12→14 additive expansion (ADR-0018 §Decision 2) (v0.4.0 PR-D-1)** / **feat(embedding): additive `EmbeddingSkipReason` expansion from 12 to 14 values (ADR-0018 §Decision 2) (v0.4.0 PR-D-1)**:
  - (a) **Additive policy / 追加ポリシー**: 既存 12 値 (`v8_heap_headroom_low` / `system_memavailable_low` / `text_fork_failed` / `text_child_error` / `text_child_abnormal_exit` / `text_ipc_race` / `visual_fork_failed` / `visual_child_error` / `visual_child_abnormal_exit` / `visual_ipc_race` / `no_embeddable_items` / `dispatch_phase_failed`) の **order / value / semantic は一切不変**。新規 2 値 (`fork_terminated_before_done` + `parity_check_failed`) は enum 末尾に append する additive 拡張。`EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` の 12→14 拡張は `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/types.ts` に反映済、`INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` (`tests/regression/standing/schema-enum-sync/inv-schema-enum-004.test.ts` + 新規 `inv-schema-enum-004-embedding-v2.test.ts`) で Prisma ↔ TS ↔ Zod ↔ OpenAPI/MCP spec の 4-way exhaustive mapping を CI 検証。/ Order, value, and semantics of the existing 12 values are fully preserved; the two new values (`fork_terminated_before_done` + `parity_check_failed`) are appended at the enum tail as an additive extension. `EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` now holds 14 entries in `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/types.ts`; CI verifies the 4-way exhaustive Prisma ↔ TS ↔ Zod ↔ OpenAPI/MCP spec mapping via `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` (`inv-schema-enum-004.test.ts` + new `inv-schema-enum-004-embedding-v2.test.ts`).
  - (b) **TypeScript consumer migration note / TypeScript consumer 移行注記**: `EmbeddingSkipReason` に対して TypeScript の **exhaustive `switch`** や discriminated union (`never` type 到達チェック) を使う consumer は、本拡張で compile-time fail するため、**新値 2 件 (`fork_terminated_before_done` / `parity_check_failed`) の handler 追加が必要**。`result.skipReason` を単純に log / persist するのみの consumer は変更不要 (backward-compatible additive extension)。/ Consumers using TypeScript exhaustive `switch` or discriminated unions over `EmbeddingSkipReason` will see compile-time `never`-type failures and MUST add handlers for the two new values (`fork_terminated_before_done` / `parity_check_failed`). Consumers that simply log or persist `result.skipReason` require no change (backward-compatible additive extension).
  - (c) **GDPR display notice / GDPR 表示通知**: These enum values are **technical identifiers for processing classification**, not intended for direct display to data subjects (GDPR Art.13/14). They are surfaced in operator-facing logs (`audit_logs` action metadata, MCP server logger) only and are never returned verbatim to data subjects as part of `data.export` (GDPR Art.20) or `preference.get` payloads. / これらの enum 値は **処理分類のための技術識別子** であり、data subject (GDPR Art.13/14) への直接表示を意図していない。運用者向けログ (`audit_logs` metadata、MCP server logger) にのみ出力され、`data.export` (GDPR Art.20) や `preference.get` の payload で data subject に verbatim で返却されることはない。
  - (d) **CRA SBOM impact / CRA SBOM 影響**: **No dependency added, SBOM unchanged** — Enum 拡張は `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/types.ts` の TypeScript リテラル配列追加のみで、外部パッケージ追加 / version bump なし。`pnpm sbom` で生成される CycloneDX 1.6 JSON には影響なし (SEC M-3 根拠)。EU CRA (2026/9/11 vulnerability reporting obligation) に対する SBOM drift リスクゼロ。/ The enum extension adds TypeScript literal array entries only in `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/types.ts`; no external package is added and no version is bumped. The CycloneDX 1.6 JSON generated by `pnpm sbom` is unaffected (per SEC M-3). Zero SBOM drift risk against EU CRA vulnerability reporting obligations (in force 2026-09-11).

### Fixed / 修正

- **fix(mcp-progress): MCP `notifications/progress` async-path race over closed transport — `page.analyze` async mode crash 修復 (INV-MCP-PROGRESS-ASYNC-001) [JP][US][EU]** / **fix(mcp-progress): MCP `notifications/progress` async-path race over closed transport — `page.analyze` async mode crash repair (INV-MCP-PROGRESS-ASYNC-001) [JP][US][EU]**:
  - **JP**: `page.analyze` async mode 呼び出し時、jobId return 後 ~42 秒で MCP server + worker child 両方が crash していた race condition を修復。`apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts:498-501` で async-return path 直前に fire-and-forget で送信していた `void progressService.notifyPhaseStart("ingest")` (4 行: コメント 2 + コード 1 + 空行 1) を削除。**真因 (CWE-662 Improper Synchronization, primary / CWE-755 Improper Handling of Exceptional Conditions, secondary)**: response が return される前に notification を fire-and-forget するため、(1) MCP SDK client が response 受信時に `_progressHandlers.delete(messageId)` を実行 → (2) その後到着する progress notification は handler 不在で `_onerror` 発火 → (3) STDIO transport drop → MCP server stdin close → server exit + worker child cascade kill。**MCP spec 違反 (dual-cite)**: MCP **2025-06-18 + 2025-11-25** spec 両方で identical clause "Progress notifications **MUST** stop after completion" + "Progress notifications **MUST** only reference tokens that ... Are associated with an in-progress operation" — `page.analyze` async mode は `{async: true, jobId, status: "queued"}` を return した瞬間に request lifecycle 完了とみなされるため、後続の progress notification は contract violation。**正規経路**: 進捗取得は `page.getJobStatus(job_id, intervalSeconds: 10)` polling のみ (analyze.tool.ts L510-515 で client に明示済)。**INV-MCP-PROGRESS-ASYNC-001 standing test**: hybrid 設計 (Block A AST source-pin via `addMcpServerSourceFile` + `createAstProject` + `assertInvName` standing helper precedent / Block B runtime integration via `vi.spyOn(sendNotification)` + 100ms microtask drain wait) で `apps/mcp-server/tests/tools/page/analyze-async-no-progress.test.ts` に landing。Affected versions: **commit `be62a059` (v0.3.0 Tier 2 development cycle) 以降の全 build** — 公式 semver tag リリースは未発行 (`git tag --contains be62a059` 結果空、現状 tag は `archive/feature-v0.2.0-sprint` / `pre-webui-removal` / `v-pre-svg-removal` のみ)、現在 v0.4.0 WIP 期間中の `main` / `feat/*` branch 全 build が影響対象。
  - **EN**: Repairs the race condition that crashed both the MCP server and worker child ~42 s after `jobId` return whenever `page.analyze` was invoked in async mode. Removes the fire-and-forget `void progressService.notifyPhaseStart("ingest")` (4 lines: 2 comments + 1 code + 1 blank) at `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/analyze.tool.ts:498-501`, immediately preceding the async-return path. **Root cause (CWE-662 Improper Synchronization, primary / CWE-755 Improper Handling of Exceptional Conditions, secondary)**: the notification is fired-and-forgotten before the response returns, so (1) the MCP SDK client deletes `_progressHandlers[messageId]` upon receiving the response → (2) the later-arriving progress notification has no handler and triggers `_onerror` → (3) STDIO transport drops → MCP server stdin closes → server exits + worker child cascades to kill. **MCP spec violation (dual-cite)**: both MCP **2025-06-18 + 2025-11-25** specs share the identical clause "Progress notifications **MUST** stop after completion" + "Progress notifications **MUST** only reference tokens that ... Are associated with an in-progress operation" — `page.analyze` async mode marks the request lifecycle as completed at the moment `{async: true, jobId, status: "queued"}` is returned, so any subsequent progress notification is a contract violation. **Canonical channel**: progress polling via `page.getJobStatus(job_id, intervalSeconds: 10)` only (already documented to clients at analyze.tool.ts L510-515). **INV-MCP-PROGRESS-ASYNC-001 standing test** lands in `apps/mcp-server/tests/tools/page/analyze-async-no-progress.test.ts` with a hybrid design (Block A AST source-pin using `addMcpServerSourceFile` + `createAstProject` + `assertInvName` standing helper precedent / Block B runtime integration via `vi.spyOn(sendNotification)` + 100 ms microtask drain wait). Affected versions: **all builds since commit `be62a059` (v0.3.0 Tier 2 development cycle)** — no official semver tag releases yet (`git tag --contains be62a059` returns empty; current tags are only `archive/feature-v0.2.0-sprint` / `pre-webui-removal` / `v-pre-svg-removal`); all `main` / `feat/*` branch builds during the current v0.4.0 WIP period are affected.
  - **CRA SBOM impact / CRA SBOM 影響**: **No dependency added, SBOM unchanged** — 4 行 deletion + 1 new test file のみ、外部パッケージ追加ゼロ。CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM drift ゼロ。EU CRA 2026-09-11 compliance 保持。/ 4-line deletion + 1 new test file only; zero new dependencies; CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM drift zero; EU CRA 2026-09-11 compliance preserved.
  - **GHSA disclosure**: GHSA disclosure not required — internal STDIO race, AV:L+UI:R, CVSS Low/Medium estimate (~3.3-4.0), no external attacker triggers (MCP server runs locally over STDIO transport only). Detailed rationale in Finding Registry §8 (multi-axis affirmation: AV/AC/PR/UI/S/CIA + no external trigger + internal trusted-process race + PR-D-8 prior-art alignment). / GHSA disclosure intentionally skipped per multi-axis assessment in Finding Registry §8.
  - **Carryover / 期限付き継続課題 (5 items)**: CO-MCP-01 (M, 2026-05-31) Defense-in-depth `ProgressNotificationService.markCompleted()` API / CO-MCP-02 (M, 2026-05-31, **L→M elevated**) sync-processing.ts **10 occurrences** (L147/173/471/608/669/704/724/725/736/790, IO grep 確定) await 化 / CO-MCP-03 (L, Q3 2026) Redis orphan recovery + queue-cleanup audit_logs / CO-MCP-04 (L, Q3 2026) `isDevelopment()` URL log review / CO-MCP-05 (M, 2026-05-31) MCP notification lifecycle ADR.

    - **[EU] GDPR Art.32(1)(b) ongoing resilience of processing systems**: MCP transport stability + worker child lifecycle 健全性を回復

Finding Registry: `

- **PR7e-β3 hotfix: 4 面実装監査で検出された 3 件の指摘を統合解消 (v0.4.0)** / **PR7e-β3 hotfix: resolve 3 audit findings from 4-face implementation audit (v0.4.0)**:
  - **TDA NO GO → GO**: `phase-5-text-embedding-child.ts:setupDI()` の TS2741 型エラー解消。`setMotionPersistenceEmbeddingServiceFactory(() => sharedService)` は `motion-persistence.service.ts` の `IEmbeddingService.generateEmbedding(text, type)` を要求するが `LayoutEmbeddingService` は `generateFromText()` のみ公開で型不適合だった。sync path (`service-registrar-analysis.ts:236`) と同じ `mlEmbeddingService` (@reftrixmcp/ml) を渡すよう変更。turbo cache 越しの pre-commit typecheck では false PASS していたため、cache 迂回 `pnpm --filter @reftrixmcp/mcp-server exec tsc --noEmit` で再検証し 0 errors 確定。/ Fix TS2741 type error. Switched to `mlEmbeddingService` (matches the sync path instance and satisfies the `IEmbeddingService.generateEmbedding(text, type)` contract). Turbo cache masked this in pre-commit; verified via direct `tsc --noEmit` invocation.
  - **SEC-β3-M1 (CWE-209)**: `embedding-handler.ts:generateMotionEmbeddings()` の silent-skip 防御で `result.errors` に push していた error 文字列から内部関数名 (`setMotionPersistencePrismaClientFactory` / `setMotionPersistenceEmbeddingServiceFactory`) を除去し、`"Motion embedding DI configuration missing"` に汎用化。詳細は `logger.error` 側にのみ残し、MCP クライアント露出経路での CWE-209 情報漏洩リスクを遮断。/ Removed internal function names from the `result.errors` entry surfaced via `generateMotionEmbeddings()`'s silent-skip defense; diagnostic details remain only in `logger.error`. Closes a latent CWE-209 exposure if `result.errors` is ever returned via MCP.
  - **LCC MEDIUM-A**: `DATA_RETENTION.md` の Changelog テーブルに 2.11.0 行を追加 (2.10.0 と 2.9.0 の間)。ヘッダーのみ 2.11.0 で Changelog テーブルに行が未追加だった整合性違反を解消。GDPR Art.30 (処理活動記録) の履歴追跡性を確保。/ Added the 2.11.0 row to the DATA_RETENTION.md Changelog table (previously only the header was bumped). Restores document integrity for GDPR Art.30 traceability.
  - **テスト / Tests**: 全 15,361 passed / 0 failed を維持。pre-commit hook で docs-impact が canonical doc 更新を要求したため ` Embedding Backfill Pipeline セクションに PR7e-β3 追記 (既にメインコミットで対応済)。

- **PR7e-β3: motion_embeddings 全サイト 0 件問題を fork child DI 修復で解消 (v0.4.0)** / **PR7e-β3: resolve motion_embeddings zero-count across all sites via fork child DI repair (v0.4.0)**:
  - **真因 / Root cause**: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/phase-5-text-embedding-child.ts` の `setupDI()` が `setMotionPersistenceEmbeddingServiceFactory` / `setMotionPersistencePrismaClientFactory` を呼び出していなかった。その結果 fork child モジュールスコープで `motion-persistence.service.ts` の `prismaClientFactory` が `null` のままとなり、`getMotionPersistenceService().isAvailable()` が false を返し、`embedding-handler.ts:generateMotionEmbeddings()` で全件 silent skip されていた。sync 経路 (`service-registrar-analysis.ts:236-243`) は正しく factory 登録済だが fork child は DI 網が独立していた。DB 観測事実: reftrix.io 552 motion_patterns / 0 motion_embeddings、Stripe 216 / 0、全サイト motion_embeddings = 0 (js_animation_embeddings は 187/187 正常 — 直接 `ctx.prisma` 使用のため DI gate 迂回)。/ `setupDI()` in `phase-5-text-embedding-child.ts` was not calling `setMotionPersistenceEmbeddingServiceFactory` / `setMotionPersistencePrismaClientFactory`. The fork child's module-scoped `prismaClientFactory` in `motion-persistence.service.ts` remained `null`, causing `getMotionPersistenceService().isAvailable()` to return false and `embedding-handler.ts:generateMotionEmbeddings()` to silently skip every record. The sync path (`service-registrar-analysis.ts:236-243`) registered the factories correctly, but the fork child has an independent DI network. DB evidence: reftrix.io 552 motion_patterns / 0 motion_embeddings, Stripe 216 / 0, motion_embeddings = 0 across every page (js_animation_embeddings 187/187 healthy because that path uses `ctx.prisma` directly and bypasses the DI gate).
  - **修正 / Fix**: `setupDI()` に `setMotionPersistenceEmbeddingServiceFactory(() => sharedService)` と `setMotionPersistencePrismaClientFactory(() => prisma as never)` の 2 行を追加 (Background / Layout factory と同パターン)。これにより Phase 5 text embedding child で motion embedding 生成が正常動作する。Vision-detected motion も同じ `generateMotionEmbeddings` 経路を通るため同時救済。/ Added 2 lines to `setupDI()`: `setMotionPersistenceEmbeddingServiceFactory(() => sharedService)` and `setMotionPersistencePrismaClientFactory(() => prisma as never)`, mirroring the Background / Layout factory pattern. Motion embedding generation is now functional in the Phase 5 text embedding child. Vision-detected motion takes the same `generateMotionEmbeddings` path and is simultaneously rescued.
  - **backfill worker 側の DI 補強 / Backfill worker DI reinforcement**: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` にも `setFramePrismaClientFactory(() => prisma as never)` を追加。将来 motion backfill が enqueue された際に `saveMotionEmbedding` が throw しないよう予防。/ Added `setFramePrismaClientFactory(() => prisma as never)` to `embedding-backfill-worker.ts` as defensive DI for future motion backfill jobs (prevents `saveMotionEmbedding` from throwing "PrismaClient not initialized").
  - **silent-skip 防御 (TDA P2) / silent-skip defense (TDA P2)**: `embedding-handler.ts:generateMotionEmbeddings()` の `isAvailable() === false` 分岐で従来 `logger.warn` だったものを `logger.error` に格上げし、`result.errors.push({ patternId: "__di_unavailable__", error: ... })` で discriminated error を追加。本番ログで DI 欠落を即座に検知可能。/ Upgraded the `isAvailable() === false` branch in `generateMotionEmbeddings()` from `logger.warn` to `logger.error` and added a discriminated `result.errors` entry (`patternId: "__di_unavailable__"`) to surface DI misconfiguration immediately in production logs.
  - **既知ギャップ (data.delete motion カバレッジ) / Known gap (data.delete motion coverage)**: `motion_patterns` は `onDelete: SetNull` で web_page に紐付くため、`data.delete` (GDPR Art.17) が web_page を削除しても motion_patterns は残存する。MCP tool `data.delete` は現時点で motion_patterns / motion_embeddings を削除対象に含まない (`apps/mcp-server/src/tools/data/data.tool.ts` に motion 参照なし)。別 PR で data.delete に motion カバレッジを追加予定 (`DATA_RETENTION.md` 2.11.0 に明記)。/ Known gap documented in `DATA_RETENTION.md` 2.11.0: `motion_patterns` use `onDelete: SetNull` (not CASCADE), so `data.delete` (GDPR Art.17) does not remove them; the MCP tool `data.delete` does not yet list motion_patterns / motion_embeddings for deletion (`apps/mcp-server/src/tools/data/data.tool.ts` has no motion references). A separate PR will add motion coverage to `data.delete`.
  - **テスト / Tests**: `apps/mcp-server/tests/workers/phases/phase-5-text-embedding-child-di.test.ts` (新規 17 tests) — fork child `setupDI()` に全ての必須 factory 呼び出しが含まれることをソースレベルで検証、backfill worker の `setFramePrismaClientFactory` module-scope 呼び出しを検証、`generateMotionEmbeddings` の silent-skip 防御 (logger.error + result.errors) を検証。mcp-server スイート全体 15,361 passed / 0 failed。/ New test file with 17 cases; full mcp-server suite: 15,361 passed / 0 failed.
  - **既存 768 件の復旧 / Recovery of existing 768 records**: reftrix.io 552 件 + Stripe 216 件の既存 motion_patterns は修正後に該当ページを page.analyze で再実行することで motion_embeddings が自動生成される (別 CLI 不要)。本番環境では DB 状態に応じて個別対応。/ The existing 552 (reftrix.io) + 216 (Stripe) motion_patterns can be recovered by re-running `page.analyze` on those pages; no dedicated CLI is needed. Production recovery is situational.

- **backfill-reconciliation SSOT 統一で false-failed バグ根本解決 (v0.4.0, commit `7ad4e448`)** / **SSOT unification in backfill-reconciliation fixes false-failed bug (v0.4.0, commit `7ad4e448`)**:
  - **真因 / Root cause**: `backfill-reconciliation.service.ts` の `countRemainingGaps` が **part_text / part_visual 2 カテゴリのみ**を backfill-eligible 絞り込みなしで数えていたため、blank image skip や section カバー外で legitimate にスキップされた parts を「pending」としてカウントし `embeddingBackfillStatus = 'failed'` に false-failed pin していた。一方 backfill worker 側 `computeRemainingStatus` は 7 カテゴリ + `countPartVisualBackfillTargets()` で正しく絞り込み済 (SSOT drift)。Stripe 実例: 254 parts のうち visual 30 件 (224 legitimate skip) → 旧 reconciliation が `visualPending=224` と判定 → false-failed pin。/ `countRemainingGaps` counted only 2 categories without backfill-eligible filtering, incorrectly flagging legitimately-skipped parts as pending and false-failed pinning. Stripe case: 254 parts / 30 visual (224 legit skip) → `visualPending=224` → false-failed.
  - **修正 / Fix**: 新規 SSOT helper `apps/mcp-server/src/services/backfill-status.helper.ts` を導入し (循環 import 回避)、`computeRemainingStatusWithPrisma(webPageId, prisma)` が 7 カテゴリ全件を backfill-eligible 絞り込み済で判定する。backfill-worker の `computeRemainingStatus` は helper wrapper に delegate し公開 API 契約維持。reconciliation は `countRemainingGaps` を完全削除し helper を直接呼出、`'in_progress'` → `'failed'` マッピングで stale pin 責務維持。/ New SSOT helper `backfill-status.helper.ts` avoids circular imports; `computeRemainingStatusWithPrisma` handles all 7 categories with proper filtering. Worker delegates via wrapper (public API preserved); reconciliation deletes `countRemainingGaps` and maps `'in_progress'` → `'failed'` for stale pin semantics.

### Changed / 変更

- **PR7e-β2 P0 (partial): Phase 5 entry blocker 緩和 + Phase 4→5 選択的メモリ解放 (v0.4.0, commit `4c15ef47`)** / **PR7e-β2 P0 (partial): mitigate Phase 5 entry blocker + selective Phase 4→5 memory disposal (v0.4.0, commit `4c15ef47`)**:
  - **背景 / Context**: β1 で `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` を 3072→4096 に緩和したが、reftrix.io (実測 RSS 5528MB) および Stripe (実測 RSS 5027MB) では Phase 5 entry 時点で 4096 を超え early-skip → 全 embedding 0 件が継続していた。β2 P0 は真因 A (RSS guard 不足) と F (Phase 4→5 中間データの非選択的維持) を緩和し、Phase 5 fork が成立する条件を整える。本 PR は **partial success** であり、Phase 5 entry 通過と fork 成立は達成したが `success=true` は別途 P1 で対応する。/ β1 relaxed `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` from 3072→4096 but reftrix.io (RSS 5528MB) and Stripe (RSS 5027MB) still exceeded 4096 at Phase 5 entry, triggering early-skip and zero embeddings DB-wide. β2 P0 addresses root causes A (insufficient RSS guard) and F (non-selective preservation of Phase 4→5 intermediate data) so Phase 5 fork can proceed. This PR is a **partial success**: Phase 5 entry and fork are now reachable, but `success=true` requires follow-up work in P1.
  - **P0-1: `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` デフォルト 4096 → 7168 (7 GB) に段階緩和** / **P0-1: tier relaxation 4096 → 7168 (7 GB)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/config/phase5-config.ts` の `DEFAULT_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` を 4096 から 7168 に引き上げ。計画段階では 6144 を暫定値としたが再検証で reftrix.io 実測 6363/6480 MB が依然超過したため、観測最大 6480MB に約 10% のマージンを加えて 7168 に確定。16GB RAM 環境では parent 7168 + Phase 5 子プロセス (DINOv2 ~800MB + e5-base ~500MB ≈ 1300MB) = 8468MB で RAM 総量の 50% 強に収まる single-tenant 安全圏。ADR-0013 で決定根拠を正式化し、ADR-0012 §2.3 の β3 上限 8192 との境界逸脱を Amendment で記述。運用影響: env var `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` でデプロイごとに override 可。/ Raised `DEFAULT_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` in `phase5-config.ts` from 4096 to 7168. The β2 plan-time provisional value of 6144 was insufficient on re-verification (reftrix.io measured 6363/6480 MB), so the final value is set to the observed maximum 6480 MB + ~10% margin = 7168. On a 16 GB RAM single-tenant node, parent 7168 + Phase 5 children (~1300 MB) = 8468 MB stays well under 50% of RAM. ADR-0013 formalises the rationale; ADR-0012 §2.3 is amended to document that β2 picks 7168 against the β3 ceiling of 8192. Operational impact: override per-deployment via the `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` env var.
  - **P0-2: `disposePhase4Memory` の選択的中間データ解放 (非破壊) / Selective intermediate disposal at Phase 4→5 handoff (non-destructive)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` の `disposePhase4Memory` を拡張し、Phase 5 が **使う** reference (sections / patterns / animations / save results) は完全保持しつつ、**使わない** 中間フィールドのみを選択解放したうえで `tryGarbageCollect()` を発火。`MotionServiceResult` からは `frame_capture` / `frame_analysis` (5-30 MB) / `webgl_animations` / `video_info` / `runtime_info` / `warnings` / `js_animation_summary` / `js_animations` (embedding 用 `jsAnimationsForEmbedding` は別フィールドで保持) を破棄。`ScrollVisionResult` からは per-capture `analyses` (Ollama Vision raw response) のみ破棄し `scrollTriggeredAnimations` は保持。過去 PR7b の `state = null` 強制撤回 (PR7e-α / ADR-0012 §2.1) とは異なり、Phase 5 入力 reference は 1 つも壊さない。保持/破棄の境界は新規 `tests/workers/page-analyze-worker-phase4-dispose.test.ts` の 7 ケースで担保。実測効果: reftrix.io で beforeRssMb=6484 → afterRssMb=6480 (reclaimed 4 MB)、現状は小幅だが安定化。/ Extended `disposePhase4Memory` in `page-analyze-worker.ts` to keep every reference Phase 5 **uses** (sections / patterns / animations / save results) while selectively dropping intermediate fields it does **not**, then calls `tryGarbageCollect()`. `MotionServiceResult` drops `frame_capture` / `frame_analysis` (5-30 MB) / `webgl_animations` / `video_info` / `runtime_info` / `warnings` / `js_animation_summary` / `js_animations` (the embedding-bound `jsAnimationsForEmbedding` lives in a separate field and is preserved). `ScrollVisionResult` drops per-capture `analyses` (raw Ollama Vision response) while keeping `scrollTriggeredAnimations`. Unlike the PR7b `state = null` forced null-out reverted by PR7e-α (ADR-0012 §2.1), this preserves every reference Phase 5 actually consumes. The keep/drop boundary is enforced by 7 new cases in `tests/workers/page-analyze-worker-phase4-dispose.test.ts`. Measured effect on reftrix.io: beforeRssMb=6484 → afterRssMb=6480 (reclaimed 4 MB) — modest but stabilising.
  - **P0-3 (revert): `onnxruntime-node` 1.21.0 → 1.24.3 upgrade 試行後に revert、1.21.0 を維持** / **P0-3 (reverted): `onnxruntime-node` 1.21.0 → 1.24.3 upgrade attempted then reverted; keeping 1.21.0**: β2 計画では packages/ml/package.json の `optionalDependencies.onnxruntime-node` を 1.21.0 → 1.24.3 に upgrade する予定だったが、検証段階で 1.24.3 に切り替えると in-process embedding 経路が CUDA library missing で全 fail する regression が判明したため revert。packages/ml/package.json は 1.21.0 のまま据え置き。`packages/ml/node_modules/onnxruntime-node` の 1.24 系バイナリが Node.js `worker_threads` 内の CUDA 初期化で provider library を解決できない事象と整合 (ADR-0014 §Context 参照)。再評価トリガと upstream issue link は ADR-0014 に記録。/ The β2 plan called for bumping `optionalDependencies.onnxruntime-node` in `packages/ml/package.json` from 1.21.0 to 1.24.3. Verification revealed a regression where 1.24.3 caused in-process embedding paths to fail universally with CUDA library missing errors, so the bump was reverted and 1.21.0 was kept. Consistent with a known class of failures where the 1.24 binary cannot resolve CUDA provider libraries inside Node.js `worker_threads` (see ADR-0014 Context). Reevaluation triggers and the upstream issue link are tracked in ADR-0014.
  - **影響範囲 / Scope**: 5 files changed, +296 / -64 (net ~232 lines). 破壊的変更なし。DB migration / API 変更なし。env var `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` デフォルト値のみ変更 (4096 → 7168、override 可)。運用者は RAM 総量 < 16 GB のホストでは override 推奨 (例: 8 GB ホストでは `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB=3584` 等)。/ 5 files, +296/-64 (net ~232 lines). No breaking change; no DB migration; no API change. Only the `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB` env-var default changes (4096 → 7168, still overridable). Operators on hosts with < 16 GB RAM should override (e.g. `PHASE5_PARENT_RSS_MAX_MB=3584` on an 8 GB host).
  - **受け入れ基準 — partial success / Acceptance criteria — partial success**: reftrix.io `page.analyze` (jobId `019d9764-591a-738f-8704-7f76f59bfa40`) 検証では **success=true は未達**。達成項目: (1) Parent RSS Guard 通過 (6480 < 7168、β1 の 4096 なら skip 確定)、(2) Phase 5 fork 成立、(3) Visual Embedding (DINOv2 in-process) initialization 成功。阻害要因 (P1 範囲): (a) Phase 5 text fork child が abnormal exit (skipReason=`text_child_abnormal_exit`、exitCode=signal)、(b) LayoutEmbedding in-process が CUDA library missing で全 fail。これら 2 件は onnxruntime-node 1.21 / 1.24 共通の課題で P0-3 の scope 外、別途 P1 で対応予定。/ Verified on reftrix.io `page.analyze` (jobId `019d9764-591a-738f-8704-7f76f59bfa40`): **`success=true` not achieved**. Achieved: (1) Parent RSS Guard passes (6480 < 7168; β1's 4096 would skip), (2) Phase 5 fork proceeds, (3) Visual Embedding (DINOv2 in-process) initialisation succeeds. Remaining blockers (P1 scope): (a) Phase 5 text fork child exits abnormally (skipReason=`text_child_abnormal_exit`, exitCode=signal), (b) LayoutEmbedding in-process fails universally with CUDA library missing. Both exist on onnxruntime-node 1.21 _and_ 1.24, so they are out of scope for P0-3 and tracked to P1.

- **PR7e-β2 P1: fork child CUDA 伝搬修正 + RSS delta kill 閾値緩和で `success=true` 達成 (v0.4.0, commit `f749e0f4`)** / **PR7e-β2 P1: fix fork child CUDA propagation + raise RSS delta kill threshold, achieving `success=true` (v0.4.0, commit `f749e0f4`)**:
  - **真因 / Root causes**: (1) `.env.local` の `ONNX_EXECUTION_PROVIDER=cuda` が `buildChildEnv()` 経由で fork child に伝搬 → CUDA provider 不在で child 異常終了。(2) e5-base CPU 推論の glibc sbrk ヒープが delta 3121MB → kill threshold 3072MB 超過。/ (1) `.env.local` `ONNX_EXECUTION_PROVIDER=cuda` propagated to fork child via `buildChildEnv()`, causing crash without CUDA provider. (2) e5-base CPU inference glibc sbrk heap reached delta 3121MB, exceeding kill threshold 3072MB.
  - **修正 / Fixes**: `phase-5-fork-orchestrator.ts` の `buildChildEnv()` に `ONNX_EXECUTION_PROVIDER = "cpu"` 強制。`phase-5-child-ipc.ts` の RSS delta 閾値を warn 2048→2560, kill 3072→4096 に緩和。/ Force `ONNX_EXECUTION_PROVIDER = "cpu"` in `buildChildEnv()`. Raise RSS delta thresholds: warn 2048→2560, kill 3072→4096.
  - **検証 / Verification**: reftrix.io `page.analyze` で **success=true**, failedPhases=[], 全 7 フェーズ完走。Section 8 text + 8 vision / Part 98 text + 42 visual。/ reftrix.io `page.analyze`: **success=true**, failedPhases=[], all 7 phases completed. Section 8 text + 8 vision / Part 98 text + 42 visual.

### Changed / 変更

- **PR7d-2: Worker 起動経路の統一 — `spawnPageAnalyzeWorkerChild()` ヘルパー化 (v0.4.0)** / **PR7d-2: Unified Worker spawn path via `spawnPageAnalyzeWorkerChild()` helper (v0.4.0)**: `analyze-50-award-sites.ts` / `reanalyze-serial.ts` / `reanalyze-large-html.ts` の 3 バッチスクリプトは `apps/mcp-server/scripts/_worker-spawn-helper.ts::spawnPageAnalyzeWorkerChild()` 経由で統一起動。旧 `spawn(...)` 直書きは削除。`REFTRIX_ALLOW_MANUAL_WORKER=true` が自動注入され、起動前に BullMQ `queue.getWorkers()` で既存 Worker を probe し warning を出す。`package.json` の dead script `worker:start:quality` (Phase Quality Worker 廃止 v0.3.0 以来参照元なし) も同時削除。/ The 3 batch scripts now spawn through `_worker-spawn-helper.ts::spawnPageAnalyzeWorkerChild()`. Direct `spawn(...)` calls removed. The helper auto-injects `REFTRIX_ALLOW_MANUAL_WORKER=true` and pre-probes BullMQ `queue.getWorkers()` with a warning. Dead script `worker:start:quality` (no referents since v0.3.0) also removed.

- **PR7d-3: ドキュメント同期 + 監査要件対応（audit_logs / OSS 公開 / fail-open 整合）(v0.4.0)** / **PR7d-3: docs sync + audit compliance (audit_logs / OSS exposure / fail-open alignment) (v0.4.0)** (`CVE-not-assigned (internal reliability improvements)`):
  - **背景 / Context**: PR7d-1 (d98fece4) + PR7d-2 (a1b6c4e0) の計 8 回の CONDITIONAL PASS 監査で deferred された LOW/MEDIUM 指摘を集約する最終 PR。リリース可能状態を実現する。/ Final PR aggregating all LOW/MEDIUM deferred items from the 8 CONDITIONAL PASS audits of PR7d-1 (d98fece4) and PR7d-2 (a1b6c4e0), bringing the branch to release-ready state.
  - **SEC M-1 (Redis fail-closed → fail-open 整合)**: `start-workers.ts` の `evaluateDualRunGuard()` が ADR-0011 記載の「Redis 障害時は fail-open」設計意図と不整合だった。`WorkerActiveLockService.checkExistingLock()` / `acquireLock()` が内部で catch して両ケース (Redis 不可到達 / race lost) を null/false に潰していたため、呼び出し側は Redis 障害でも fail-closed で `process.exit(1)` せざるを得なかった。`tryAcquireLock()` / `probeExistingLock()` の discriminated union API を追加し、fail-open vs fail-closed を明示的に区別。既存 `acquireLock` / `checkExistingLock` は legacy wrapper として残置 (後方互換)。/ `start-workers.ts`'s `evaluateDualRunGuard()` was fail-closed even on Redis outage, contradicting ADR-0011's documented fail-open intent. Added discriminated union APIs `tryAcquireLock()` / `probeExistingLock()`; legacy methods kept as wrappers.
  - **LCC MEDIUM-1 (`.ossfilter` 更新)**: `repair-orphaned-backfill-records.ts` を OSS 公開対象に追加 (`.ossfilter` に `+ /apps/mcp-server/scripts/repair-orphaned-backfill-records.ts` include exception 追加)。operator が OSS 利用時に破損レコード修復できるよう透明性向上 (AGPL-3.0)。`_worker-spawn-helper.ts` は内部バッチ専用のため除外継続。/ Added `repair-orphaned-backfill-records.ts` as an OSS-exposed script (include exception in `.ossfilter`) so OSS operators can repair orphaned records. `_worker-spawn-helper.ts` remains excluded (internal batch-only).
  - **LCC MEDIUM-2 (TTL cron + repair script audit_logs 記録)**: `ScreenshotPersistenceService.cleanupExpired()` が削除件数 > 0 時に `audit_logs` へ `action=screenshot_ttl_cleanup` エントリ追加 (GDPR Art.30 処理活動記録)。`repair-orphaned-backfill-records.ts` は dry-run / confirm 両モードで `action=backfill_orphaned_repaired` エントリ追加 (dry-run でも検出件数は運用証跡として重要)。両経路とも `webPageId` は `truncateId` で 8 文字 + `...` に truncate (PII 最小化 GDPR Art.5(1)(c))。/ `cleanupExpired()` writes an `audit_logs` entry when `deletedCount > 0`. The repair script writes an entry in both dry-run and confirm modes. Both paths truncate `webPageId` to 8 chars (PII minimisation).
  - **SEC L-1 (sanitizeErrorMessage 適用)**: `start-workers.ts` の fatal error handler (3 箇所) / `_worker-spawn-helper.ts` の probe error (1 箇所) / `repair-orphaned-backfill-records.ts` の fatal handler (1 箇所) の生 `error.message` を全て `sanitizeErrorMessage()` に置換。CWE-209 (Information Exposure Through Error Message) 対応。/ Replaced raw `error.message` in 5 locations with `sanitizeErrorMessage()` (CWE-209).
  - **SEC L-2 (heartbeat extendLock timeout)**: `start-workers.ts` の standalone heartbeat の `extendLock()` 呼び出しを `Promise.race` で 10s タイムアウト保護。Redis hang による heartbeat cadence の詰まりを防止。/ Wrapped heartbeat `extendLock()` in `Promise.race` with 10s timeout; prevents heartbeat cadence stall on hung Redis calls.
  - **TPA LOW-1 (`acquireRedisLockBestEffort` 再入防止)**: `WorkerSupervisor` に `lockAcquireInflight` フラグを追加。連続 `ensureWorkerRunning()` 呼び出しで `acquireLock()` が多重 Redis round-trip を発生させる race を防止。/ Added `lockAcquireInflight` flag to prevent concurrent `acquireLock()` round-trips on repeated `ensureWorkerRunning()` calls.
  - **TPA LOW-3 (fatal path での lock release)**: `start-workers.ts` の `process.on('exit', ...)` ハンドラに standalone lock の best-effort release を追加。crash 時に 60s TTL まで lock がリークするのを短縮。/ Added best-effort `releaseLock` in `process.on('exit', ...)`; reduces leaked-lock window on crash from 60s TTL to near-zero.
